**[00:05:57.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000063)  
I again have the honor of welcoming Secretary Juanito Victor "Jonvic" Remulla,

**[00:06:02.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000064)  
the honorable members of this committee, the heads, officials, and personnel of the Department of the Interior and Local Government,

**[00:06:11.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000066)  
and its attached agencies, our resource persons, and our distinguished guests

**[00:06:17.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000067)  
for the briefing on the Department's proposed budget for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:06:23.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000068)  
When we speak of poverty, we usually speak of income.

**[00:06:27.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000069)  
We speak of the ability of every family to afford food, secure decent work,

**[00:06:33.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000070)  
access essential services, and pursue a better life.

**[00:06:38.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000071)  
There is, however, one dimension of poverty we tend to overlook, the poverty of security.

**[00:06:46.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000072)  
To my fellow lawmakers, peace, safety, disaster, resilience, and the capacity of our local government units

**[00:06:55.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000073)  
to deliver basic necessities are not expenses that compete with economic development.

**[00:07:01.290] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000074)  
They are the conditions which make economic development possible.

**[00:07:05.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000075)  
Congress therefore supports the Department's direction

**[00:07:09.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000076)  
towards safer, more resilient communities,

**[00:07:13.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000077)  
stronger local governments, and law enforcement institutions that are trusted, capable, and responsive.

**[00:07:21.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000078)  
This is precisely why the DILG's requested budget of P332.5 billion

**[00:07:28.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000079)  
under the fiscal year 2027 National Expenditure Program.

**[00:07:34.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000080)  
7.1% higher than

**[00:07:37.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000081)  
the Fiscal Year 2026 General Appropriations Act level

**[00:07:42.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000082)  
will be a strategic public commitment that should yield clear and measurable returns in peace and order,

**[00:07:52.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000083)  
public safety, disaster preparedness, and local governance.

**[00:07:57.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000084)  
It will provide sustained institutional support for the department

**[00:08:01.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000085)  
and will convert national policy into peace, safety, security, and services

**[00:08:08.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000086)  
at the level where Filipinos actually experience government.

**[00:08:12.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000087)  
Of the total proposal, P242.5 billion is intended for the Philippine National Police,

**[00:08:21.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000088)  
underscoring its central role of effective policing and maintaining a secure domestic environment.

**[00:08:28.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000089)  
Over the past year, the DILG has delivered significant gains in public safety,

**[00:08:34.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000090)  
law enforcement, corrections, and local governance.

**[00:08:37.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000091)  
The department posted a 96.33 efficiency rate in emergency response

**[00:08:46.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000092)  
with response times of 5 to 7 minutes.

**[00:08:50.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000093)  
As we enter the rainy season, we now have 50,441 evacuation centers nationwide

**[00:08:57.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000094)  
with more than 126,000 uniformed personnel deployed for disaster response

**[00:09:05.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000095)  
ready to assist Filipinos in distress,

**[00:09:08.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000096)  
especially in light of the recent Habagat.

**[00:09:10.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000097)  
The crime rate has continued to go down

**[00:09:13.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000098)  
with lower numbers across focused crimes,

**[00:09:17.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000099)  
index and non-index crimes,

**[00:09:20.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000100)  
and cybercrimes cases.

**[00:09:22.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000101)  
These gains deserve sustained support,

**[00:09:25.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000102)  
especially as the threat environment continues to evolve.

**[00:09:31.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000103)  
Recent incidents of school and academic violence involving students,

**[00:09:36.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000104)  
together with threats

**[00:09:37.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000105)  
circulated through online platforms,

**[00:09:39.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000106)  
create a new challenge for the PNP

**[00:09:42.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000107)  
and other law enforcement agencies with related mandates.

**[00:09:47.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000108)  
The PNP's report of 50 school-related incidents involving minors from June 16 to August 18, 2026

**[00:09:56.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000109)  
reinforces the need for prevention, early detection, rapid response,

**[00:10:02.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000110)  
and responsible digital monitoring that respects lawful safeguards.

**[00:10:08.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000111)  
In this regard,

**[00:10:09.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000112)  
we welcome and support the Department's review of school security protocols,

**[00:10:15.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000113)  
the strengthening of safer school zones,

**[00:10:19.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000114)  
closer DILG-DepEd coordination,

**[00:10:22.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000115)  
and active police-community partnership,

**[00:10:25.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000116)  
including mobilization of barangay peace and safety mechanisms.

**[00:10:29.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000117)  
The proposed budget provides concrete support through

**[00:10:33.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000118)  
P3.1 billion for PNP

**[00:10:36.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000119)  
information and communication technologies projects,

**[00:10:40.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000120)  
P2 billion for the PNP capability enhancement program,

**[00:10:46.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000121)  
and P48.95 million for Phase 2 of the Philippine Public Safety College National Cyber Training Institute.

**[00:10:55.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000122)  
These resources should translate into trained investigators,

**[00:11:00.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000123)  
stronger digital forensics capability,

**[00:11:03.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000124)  
secure and interoperable systems,

**[00:11:07.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000125)  
practical threat reporting channels,

**[00:11:11.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000126)  
and clear response protocols for schools and communities.

**[00:11:14.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000127)  
According to the United Nations,

**[00:11:17.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000128)  
peace is not simply the absence of conflict.

**[00:11:21.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000129)  
It is the foundation on which all sustainable development is built.

**[00:11:26.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000130)  
No less than the President himself said in his SONA

**[00:11:29.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000131)  
that our goal as a government is to uplift the lives of struggling Filipinos.

**[00:11:34.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000133)  
If our objective is to uplift the poor,

**[00:11:38.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000134)  
we must make sure places where Filipinos live are safe,

**[00:11:43.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000135)  
orderly, and resilient with their local governments

**[00:11:46.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000136)  
capable of responding immediately when crisis strikes.

**[00:11:50.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000137)  
The DILG's budget affirms our shared commitment

**[00:11:54.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000138)  
to strengthen the government's capacity to act.

**[00:11:57.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000139)  
Communities in crisis need a government capable of responding today,

**[00:12:03.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000140)  
not tomorrow.

**[00:12:04.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000141)  
Our shared challenge

**[00:12:06.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000142)  
is to help the Department move these reforms forward

**[00:12:10.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000143)  
while ensuring that every appropriation is used

**[00:12:15.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000144)  
strategically, promptly, and accountably to prevent threats,

**[00:12:19.600] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000145)  
protect the vulnerable, and preserve peaceful communities.

**[00:12:24.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000146)  
Congress stands ready to support Secretary Remulla and the entire DILG family

**[00:12:30.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000147)  
in advancing programs that strengthen peace and order

**[00:12:34.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000148)  
and allow our people's school, business, and local economies

**[00:12:38.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000149)  
to thrive.

**[00:12:39.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000150)  
We must protect our constituents' right to safety and security.

**[00:12:44.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000151)  
When our communities are peaceful,

**[00:12:47.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000152)  
our constituents are put in a better position for growth,

**[00:12:51.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000153)  
prosperity, and meaningful development.

**[00:12:54.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000154)  
Secretary Remulla, we look forward to your presentation.

**[00:14:45.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000195)  
Fiscal Year 27 Budget Proposal.

**[00:14:56.840] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000196)  
The Department of the Interior

**[00:14:59.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000197)  
and Local Government is at the forefront of advancing peace and order, public

**[00:15:05.940] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000198)  
safety and responsive local governance across the country. Together with its attached agencies,

**[00:15:14.180] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000199)  
the Department works to strengthen local government units, enhance public safety and

**[00:15:20.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000200)  
emergency response and promote inclusive programs that uplift the lives and welfare of Filipinos.

**[00:15:27.760] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000201)  
With the support, resources and confidence entrusted to us by Congress, the Department

**[00:15:35.520] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000202)  
has continued to fulfill its mandate and deliver programs aligned with the President's priorities

**[00:15:42.120] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000203)  
for the country. Safeguarding our collective peace and prosperity, and ensuring our people

**[00:15:49.660] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000204)  
safe and secure, remained among our foremost priorities. We continue to strengthen the unified

**[00:15:57.440] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000205)  
911 system

**[00:15:58.880] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000206)  
through acquiring additional assets and expanding access to emergency services,

**[00:16:05.320] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000207)  
which led to more calls, fewer prank calls, and an improved emergency response time.

**[00:16:13.980] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000208)  
The aggressive anti-criminality campaign by the PNP led to reduced crimes throughout the country

**[00:16:21.700] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000209)  
while the NAPOLCOM fulfilled its commitment of zero backlog in cases involving police officers

**[00:16:28.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000210)  
that helped restore accountability, integrity and public trust. Our unrelenting drive

**[00:16:36.640] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000211)  
against illegal drugs led to billions worth of drugs seized, thousands arrested, and more

**[00:16:44.280] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000212)  
barangays nationwide cleared of drug-related threats. This we did, even as we completed

**[00:16:52.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000213)  
development projects in conflict-affected areas and gave former rebels more opportunities to

**[00:16:59.060] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000214)  
begin anew. Through the BJMP, we significantly reduced jail congestion as well as provided

**[00:17:08.200] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000215)  
holistic rehabilitation and reintegration services for persons deprived of liberty.

**[00:17:15.860] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000216)  
We instituted sweeping reforms within the BFP to strengthen transparency, integrity

**[00:17:23.080] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000217)  
and accountability, which contributed to more effective and efficient fire prevention and

**[00:17:29.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000218)  
suppression services, thereby reducing fire incidents and saving more lives.

**[00:17:36.520] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000219)  
The Department continues to empower and transform our local government units as capable and agile

**[00:17:43.620] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000220)  
partners for national development. Our rigorous monitoring of national and local government

**[00:17:50.360] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000221)  
funded projects ensured the completion of thousands of projects such as farm-to-market roads,

**[00:17:57.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000222)  
evacuation centers, rice subsidies, water and sanitation systems, and other community facilities

**[00:18:05.500] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000223)  
that benefited millions of Filipino families, especially in underserved and disadvantaged areas.

**[00:18:14.180] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000224)  
We nurtured our newly elected local officials as well as our barangay and Sangguniang Kabataan

**[00:18:21.000] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000225)  
officials through various capacity-building interventions. We accelerated the

**[00:18:27.200] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000226)  
digitalization and automation of LGU services that led to faster and more efficient governance,

**[00:18:34.120] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000227)  
which is essential in attracting businesses and investments and boosting economic development among

**[00:18:40.740] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000228)  
LGUs. The Department likewise played a pivotal role in making our LGUs adequately prepared,

**[00:18:49.860] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000229)  
which cushioned the impact of typhoons and other calamities that hit the various parts of the country.

**[00:18:56.600] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000230)  
These include the constant monitoring of evacuation centers in LGUs,

**[00:19:02.480] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000231)  
as well as the timely and strategic deployment of first responders in affected areas

**[00:19:08.020] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000232)  
to provide immediate assistance during and after disasters.

**[00:19:14.360] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000233)  
We also monitor the completion of rehabilitated and constructed facilities and maintain the

**[00:19:21.300] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000234)  
cleanliness of barangays and communities to help prevent further risks to public health.

**[00:19:30.520] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000235)  
Uplifting the lives and welfare of every Filipino has been the driving force behind our sectoral interventions.

**[00:19:39.500] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000236)  
We advanced the institutionalization of early childhood care and development while investing in the capacity and potential of the next generation of Filipinos.

**[00:19:51.780] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000237)  
We also continued to address the needs of our Muslim brethren while strengthening our efforts in advancing gender equality in the country

**[00:20:00.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000238)  
that are at par with or even better than those of other countries in Asia and across the globe.

**[00:20:07.780] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000239)  
These and much more have been made possible as reflected in our strong financial performance in 2025

**[00:20:16.360] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000240)  
with a 96% obligation rate.

**[00:20:20.300] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000241)  
And a 95% disbursement rate.

**[00:20:25.460] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000242)  
Similarly, we remain on track to achieve our financial targets for the year

**[00:20:31.120] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000243)  
with a 55% obligation rate and a nearly 97% disbursement rate as of mid-year.

**[00:20:41.300] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000244)  
Our proposal for the next fiscal year reflects our commitment to sustaining our ongoing initiatives

**[00:20:48.740] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000245)  
and advancing our goals, amounting to a total of

**[00:20:53.180] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000246)  
P332.5 billion, a 7% increase from last year's appropriation.

**[00:21:02.280] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000247)  
Of this amount, Bureau of Jail Management and Penology, Bureau of Fire Protection, Philippine National Police,

**[00:21:11.440] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000248)  
and the Office of the Secretary have registered increases in their respective appropriations.

**[00:21:17.740] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000249)  
While the Early Childhood Care and Development Council, Philippine Commission on Women,

**[00:21:23.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000250)  
National Commission on Muslim Filipinos, National Youth Commission, and National Police Commission

**[00:21:30.660] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000251)  
have experienced decreases compared with their previous appropriations.

**[00:21:37.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000252)  
Consistent with the previous years, the majority of our proposed budget, equivalent to 84%,

**[00:21:44.120] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000253)  
is allocated to personal services, while 13% is earmarked for maintenance and other operating expenses

**[00:21:52.900] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000254)  
and 3% for capital outlay.

**[00:21:57.380] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000255)  
The proposed budget is broken down by expense class per agency.

**[00:22:05.340] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000256)  
With your help, we will be able to build on these gains through the proposals each agency has put forward for your consideration.

**[00:22:16.700] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000257)  
The Office of the Secretary will continue to uplift local governance in every community

**[00:22:23.460] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000258)  
through the implementation of various programs and projects

**[00:22:27.460] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000259)  
that strengthen LGU capacity.

**[00:22:30.260] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000262)  
hasten implementation of development programs,

**[00:22:33.950] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000263)  
advance digitalization, good governance, and citizen participation.

**[00:22:39.800] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000264)  
We will remain unwavering in improving our emergency response system

**[00:22:46.560] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000265)  
to make it more efficient and accessible,

**[00:22:49.440] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000266)  
while strengthening the fight against illegal drugs

**[00:22:53.380] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000267)  
and advancing peace and order in the communities.

**[00:22:58.120] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000268)  
Our drive continues to focus on the rehabilitation and cleanup of Manila Bay,

**[00:23:04.320] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000270)  
strengthening disaster response and preparedness,

**[00:23:07.580] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000271)  
enhancing the Department's ICT capabilities,

**[00:23:11.280] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000272)  
and fully operationalizing our new central office and regional offices.

**[00:23:20.060] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000273)  
The LGA will continue to strengthen LGUs and the DILG personnel

**[00:23:26.000] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000274)  
through training newly elected barangay officials,

**[00:23:30.060] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000276)  
advancing disaster readiness, innovation, creative industries, and security,

**[00:23:36.640] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000277)  
and strengthening their capacity to deliver these interventions.

**[00:23:43.260] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000278)  
To uphold integrity and trust in our police force,

**[00:23:47.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000279)  
the NAPOLCOM will prioritize investments in critical facilities in Regions 3, 5,

**[00:23:54.880] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000280)  
and Cordillera Administrative Region, ICT projects, ISO certification, and equipment for Negros Island Region.

**[00:24:06.800] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000281)  
The Philippine National Police will strengthen its ranks to become a reliable and trustworthy partner in law enforcement

**[00:24:15.300] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000282)  
through the recruitment of new personnel, enhanced equipment and ICT capabilities,

**[00:24:21.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000283)  
and stronger support for the welfare of its personnel.

**[00:24:28.000] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000284)  
This will also include

**[00:24:30.200] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000285)  
fulfilling commitments under the Program Convergence Initiatives,

**[00:24:34.160] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000286)  
intensifying police operations, and constructing new facilities.

**[00:24:40.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000287)  
The Bureau of Fire Protection will likewise accelerate efforts in fire prevention, suppression, and emergency response

**[00:24:49.960] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000288)  
through hiring new fire officers, operationalizing newly completed fire stations,

**[00:24:56.440] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000289)  
deploying new fire trucks, procuring equipment,

**[00:25:00.240] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000290)  
and constructing new fire stations, training centers, and a fire arson laboratory.

**[00:25:09.380] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000291)  
To safeguard and rehabilitate persons deprived of liberty,

**[00:25:14.240] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000292)  
the Bureau of Jail Management and Penology will bring in recruits to reinforce operational capability

**[00:25:21.420] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000293)  
and ensure hygiene and medical support.

**[00:25:24.800] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000294)  
It will also counter illegal drugs in our jails,

**[00:25:29.620] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000295)  
procure new firearms, radios, and vans,

**[00:25:33.520] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000296)  
and construct new jail facilities in Calabarzon.

**[00:25:40.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000297)  
Molding the minds of our future public order and safety leaders,

**[00:25:44.940] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000298)  
the Philippine Public Safety College shall continue its education and training programs,

**[00:25:50.700] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000299)  
research and curriculum development,

**[00:25:53.280] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000300)  
while undertaking the construction of facilities and procurement of PPEs and ICT equipment.

**[00:26:02.600] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000301)  
The National Youth Commission shall lead the charge in empowering the Filipino leaders of tomorrow

**[00:26:08.960] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000306)  
through mandatory and continuing training for Sangguniang Kabataan,

**[00:26:14.240] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000307)  
participation in youth-oriented programs and activities,

**[00:26:17.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000308)  
and support for anti-bullying and youth health initiatives.

**[00:26:23.440] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000309)  
The National Commission on Muslim Filipinos.

**[00:26:26.940] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000310)  
This includes moonsighting activities for Eid al-Adha and Eid al-Fitr, the administration and supervision of Hajj operations,

**[00:26:36.260] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000311)  
strengthening Madaris and Islamic studies, supporting Muslim MSMEs, providing legal and paralegal assistance,

**[00:26:45.480] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000312)  
and promoting peace-building and dialogue in Muslim Filipino communities.

**[00:26:52.200] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000313)  
Championing the causes of women and girls, the Philippine Commission on Women will expand

**[00:26:58.680] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000314)  
its reach in the Visayas while sustaining its initiatives on gender and development.

**[00:27:05.480] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000315)  
Lastly,

**[00:27:06.520] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000316)  
The Early Childhood Care and Development Council will intensify its efforts to give

**[00:27:12.820] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000317)  
every child a stronger start by capacitating intermediaries and partners, ensuring accreditation

**[00:27:20.240] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000318)  
of ECCD service providers, and monitoring child development centers.

**[00:27:27.220] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000319)  
We will continue to stand firm and unwavering in serving the interests of the Filipino people

**[00:27:35.840] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000320)  
and in carrying forward our commitment to

**[00:27:39.320] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000321)  
building a nation that embodies the ideals of a Bagong Pilipinas.

**[00:27:44.940] DILG Video Narrator** (asr-000322)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:27:53.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000323)  
Thank you, Secretary, for the very concise brief presentation and even the accomplishment

**[00:28:00.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000324)  
of the DILG and attached agencies. Basta Upsilon 'yan, magaling po talaga.

**[00:28:07.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000325)  
I think Tau Gamma will agree.

**[00:28:11.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000326)  
Bong Teves will agree with that.

**[00:28:14.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000327)  
Thank you very much, sir.

**[00:28:17.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000327-split-1)  
Okay, so before the interpellation, I would like to recognize

**[00:28:21.600] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000328)  
the presence of our Deputy Speaker, Bambi Emano, Vice Chair Maria Isabel Sagarbarria, Vice Chair

**[00:28:33.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000329)  
Keith Micah "Mike" Tan, Vice Chair Bernadette Barbers, Representative Edgar Erice,

**[00:28:44.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000330)  
Vice Chair Joboy Aquino, Representative Jurdin Jesus Romualdo. Manong JJ pala, Manong JJ,

**[00:28:54.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000331)  
our Majority Leader sa CA. So before the interpellation, may I remind our distinguished colleagues

**[00:29:02.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000332)  
that each member is allotted five minutes for questions or interpellations,

**[00:29:09.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000333)  
excluding the response of the resource persons.

**[00:29:12.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000334)  
When the bell sounds, the member has 30 seconds to conclude or round up.

**[00:29:22.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000335)  
Gumaganda na yung bell sa Kongreso.

**[00:29:25.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000335-split-1)  
Consistent with the long-standing practice of the House, the Chair shall recognize minority,

**[00:29:31.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000336)  
independent, and majority members in an alternating manner when practicable.

**[00:29:37.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000337)  
The Chair first recognized the Honorable Representative Dadah Kiram Ismula. Ma’am, you have the floor.

**[00:29:47.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000337-split-1)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:29:48.820] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000338)  
Assalamualaikum warahmatullahi wabarakatuhu.

**[00:29:53.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000339)  
magandang umaga to my colleagues uh

**[00:29:57.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000340)  
sa DILG family

**[00:29:58.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000341)  
and to its attached agency.

**[00:30:02.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000342)  
Una ko pong magiging katanungan para po sa DILG,

**[00:30:07.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000343)  
PNP mentioned sa Senate hearing that true crime community or TCC

**[00:30:12.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000344)  
and its subcultures 764, The Com may be involved sa school shootings in Tacloban.

**[00:30:20.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000345)  
As regards to this, can you briefly share some findings relating to this

**[00:30:25.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000346)  
sa shooting incident in Zamboanga City?

**[00:30:29.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000347)  
Yes, ma'am. The TCC allegations were a theory that the boy, the CICL, searched prior to his history.

**[00:30:40.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000348)  
But there's no conclusive link that the CICL, that the, through, what do you call the organization?

**[00:30:49.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000349)  
It was mentioned in the PNP during the Senate hearing.

**[00:30:53.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000350)  
Yes, there's no conclusive link that the group influenced the boy into his actions.

**[00:30:59.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000351)  
Okay. Maraming salamat po.

**[00:31:01.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000352)  
How about sa NBI and the DOJ are also investigating NVE cases sa school shootings po.

**[00:31:08.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000353)  
Paano kayo nagko-coordinate ng investigation at pag-prevent ng future school violence incidents targeting children?

**[00:31:15.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000354)  
Well, ma'am, the first part is that the Department of Education and the DILG are entering into a memorandum circular

**[00:31:24.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000355)  
to allow barangay tanods inside

**[00:31:28.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000356)  
the school buildings.

**[00:31:30.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000357)  
So, they will be part of the security augmentation force to inspect the bags before they enter.

**[00:31:35.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000358)  
Outside of that, the PNP is regularly scheduled in the ingress and egress of students during school time.

**[00:31:44.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000359)  
They are required to be there.

**[00:31:46.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000360)  
So, all schools are to be secured between yung pasokan nila at saka yung pauwihan nila.

**[00:31:54.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000361)  
Thank you for that, Mr. Chair.

**[00:31:56.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000362)  
It is also apparent that in both the Tacloban at Zamboanga City incident, government-issued firearms ang ginamit. Ano ba ang protocol sa handling at ano ang mga nilabag? Most importantly, ano ang babaguhin ng PNP at DILG patungkol sa handling ng issued firearms?

**[00:32:15.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000363)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[00:32:16.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000364)  
There are 800,000 uniformed personnel in the country divided between the PNP, the AFP, the NBI, Bureau of Customs, BJNP,

**[00:32:28.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000365)  
and the BFP.

**[00:32:29.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000366)  
In the 800,000, we have had two incidences of unauthorized use of their firearms.

**[00:32:35.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000367)  
It is stated in the protocol that they are responsible at all times for their guns.

**[00:32:39.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000368)  
And they are supposed to put it in safekeeping under lock and key if not in their possession.

**[00:32:47.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000369)  
In both cases, the aunt of the Tacloban shooter just left it in her table.

**[00:32:55.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000370)  
And in the...

**[00:32:58.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000371)  
In the Zamboanga case, it was left in his car.

**[00:33:00.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000372)  
Now, what we are proposing is that the IRR has to be amended that all lawfully issued firearms are to be...

**[00:33:15.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000373)  
The PNP will be granted access to all homes of lawfully issued firearms to inspect their procedures in safekeeping.

**[00:33:26.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000374)  
This might...

**[00:33:27.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000375)  
upset a few people because it might be interpreted as an impingement of rights.

**[00:33:37.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000376)  
However, we must remember that the privilege to own a gun is not enshrined in our constitution.

**[00:33:46.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000377)  
It is not like the United States of America where it is a part of the Second Amendment.

**[00:33:51.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000378)  
It is a privilege granted by law to those who wish to avail of a firearm.

**[00:33:57.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000379)  
So,

**[00:33:58.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000380)  
the PNP must have access,

**[00:34:00.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000381)  
must give notice,

**[00:34:01.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000382)  
and inspect where they put their firearms and how they safekeep it.

**[00:34:07.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000383)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:34:07.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000384)  
sa ngayon po,

**[00:34:08.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000385)  
ano pong kaso or may kaso na bang nakahain doon sa dalawang incident na ito?

**[00:34:15.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000386)  
That is another part that I am asking Congress to upgrade.

**[00:34:18.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000387)  
As of now,

**[00:34:20.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000388)  
the only case of the aunt and the father of those who own the guns

**[00:34:26.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000389)  
are reckless imprudence.

**[00:34:27.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000390)  
Reckless imprudence resulting in homicide.

**[00:34:30.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000391)  
It is parang humiram ka lang ng kotse,

**[00:34:32.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000392)  
pinahiram mo yung kotse,

**[00:34:33.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000393)  
tapos [unclear] ka sa [unclear].

**[00:34:35.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000394)  
Reckless imprudence na.

**[00:34:36.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000395)  
There should be a greater penalty for the owners of the guns that are used for illegal purposes.

**[00:34:43.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000396)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[00:34:45.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000397)  
Dadako naman po ko sa isa pang katanungan para sa PNP

**[00:34:49.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000398)  
on mistaken identity arrest.

**[00:34:52.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000399)  
Bagamat mandato po ng PNP ang pagpapatupad ng batas at pag-aresto,

**[00:34:58.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000400)  
ng mga lumalabag dito.

**[00:35:00.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000401)  
May mga insidente ng mistaken identity na nagdudulot ng pangamba

**[00:35:04.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000402)  
at kawalan ng tiwala sa kanilang operasyon.

**[00:35:08.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000403)  
Nitong August 19 of 2026,

**[00:35:11.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000404)  
kiniulat ng Bangsa Moro Human Rights Commission

**[00:35:13.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000405)  
na sa Basilan,

**[00:35:15.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000406)  
ilang residente ang paulit-ulit na naaaresto batay sa mga warrant na nakapangalan sa ibang tao.

**[00:35:23.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000407)  
Kaugnay dito,

**[00:35:24.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000408)  
ano ang nakalaan na pondo ng PNP sa NEP?

**[00:35:28.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000409)  
Ano ang nakalaan na pondo ng PNP sa NEP para sa intelligence gathering at verification

**[00:35:31.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000410)  
bago masagawa ng arrest operation?

**[00:35:35.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000411)  
At paano masisigurong ginagamit ito ng tama

**[00:35:38.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000412)  
para maiwasan ang ganitong kaso ng mistaken identity cases?

**[00:35:43.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000413)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[00:35:44.600] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000414)  
In the total budget of the PNP for 2026 and 2027,

**[00:35:50.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000415)  
we have accumulated almost 4 billion in savings

**[00:35:54.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000416)  
to implement a PNP community ID.

**[00:35:57.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000417)  
This community IDP will involve sophisticated biometrics and records keeping

**[00:36:05.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000418)  
so that mistaken identities can no longer happen.

**[00:36:08.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000419)  
What happens now is sometimes when we arrest a person

**[00:36:11.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000420)  
and we put a picture,

**[00:36:13.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000421)  
five names come up

**[00:36:15.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000422)  
because we have, I think, 35 government IDs

**[00:36:19.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000423)  
and that they can take it easily.

**[00:36:21.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000424)  
And it is easily reflected in the SIM card registration.

**[00:36:26.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000425)  
One person,

**[00:36:26.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000426)  
can accumulate 400 phones

**[00:36:30.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000427)  
and using different IDs issued by the government

**[00:36:33.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000428)  
that is accepted.

**[00:36:34.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000429)  
So we are proposing that

**[00:36:35.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000430)  
the PNP is already instituting a community ID

**[00:36:39.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000431)  
so that there will be one single ID for the entire country.

**[00:36:43.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000432)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:36:44.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000433)  
napapaloob ba dito sa budget sa NEP

**[00:36:49.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000434)  
yung tinatawag na award or rewards

**[00:36:53.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000435)  
in the event po na may nahuli sila

**[00:36:56.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000436)  
pero apparently,

**[00:36:57.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000437)  
it was a mistaken identity.

**[00:36:59.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000438)  
There is no reward for a mistaken identity

**[00:37:03.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000439)  
but there is an investigation for that.

**[00:37:05.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000440)  
And sa kasalukuyan po, Mr. Chair,

**[00:37:08.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000441)  
may mga naulat na po ba sa inyo

**[00:37:10.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000442)  
at may mga action na po ba na nagawa

**[00:37:15.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000443)  
in the event na may nagkamalinga

**[00:37:17.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000444)  
from the PNP personnels,

**[00:37:19.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000445)  
ano po yung mga kaagarang cases

**[00:37:23.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000446)  
or filed cases na nagagawa sa PNP?

**[00:37:25.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000447)  
Well, in those cases,

**[00:37:27.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000448)  
first, there is the admin aspect

**[00:37:28.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000449)  
where they are investigated.

**[00:37:30.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000450)  
If there was malicious intent in it,

**[00:37:34.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000451)  
then we will forward it to the NAPO-COM

**[00:37:36.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000452)  
for their disposal.

**[00:37:39.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000453)  
Patungkol doon sa ALFAD family,

**[00:37:41.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000454)  
it was reportedly,

**[00:37:43.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000455)  
it is in Zamboanga City po,

**[00:37:45.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000456)  
it was reportedly na mistaken identity din.

**[00:37:49.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000457)  
Ano po yung status ng kasong yun?

**[00:37:52.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000458)  
Sino ba ang ano?

**[00:37:53.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000459)  
Let me get back to you, ma'am.

**[00:38:00.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000460)  
Ma'am, can we get back to you?

**[00:38:02.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000461)  
Before the session starts,

**[00:38:04.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000462)  
we will gather the information.

**[00:38:06.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000463)  
Yeah, Mr. Chair, okay lang po yun.

**[00:38:09.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000464)  
Dadako na lang ako muna sa ibang katanungan

**[00:38:11.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000465)  
while waiting for that.

**[00:38:13.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000466)  
Sige po.

**[00:38:14.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000467)  
Patungkol po ito sa

**[00:38:15.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000468)  
violence against women and children

**[00:38:18.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000469)  
sa VAWC.

**[00:38:21.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000470)  
Noong Marso of 2026,

**[00:38:23.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000471)  
inuulat ng DILG na 40,886 barangays

**[00:38:29.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000472)  
o 97% ng kabuuang bilang ng mga barangay

**[00:38:33.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000473)  
Ang may funksyonal VAWC GBV desk,

**[00:38:37.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000474)  
which is a welcome development.

**[00:38:39.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000475)  
Ngunit may mga natanggap kaming ulat

**[00:38:41.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000476)  
sa grounds ng ilang desk

**[00:38:43.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000477)  
ay hindi talaga funksyonal

**[00:38:44.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000478)  
at walang tao, walang tamang lugar

**[00:38:47.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000479)  
o ginagamit bilang storage lang.

**[00:38:50.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000480)  
Ano kaya ang plano ng DILG

**[00:38:52.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000481)  
para sa sustainability ng mga help desk

**[00:38:55.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000482)  
beyond initial funding?

**[00:38:57.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000483)  
May recurrent ba na budget line item

**[00:39:01.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000485)  
Para sa staffing, training at maintenance

**[00:39:04.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000486)  
o nakadepende lang ito sa one-time allocation?

**[00:39:07.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000487)  
Ma'am, the VAWC desk is a function

**[00:39:11.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000488)  
of the barangay system.

**[00:39:13.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000489)  
Their budget emanates from the budget

**[00:39:19.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000490)  
that is allocated to them

**[00:39:21.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000491)  
according to the local government fund.

**[00:39:24.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000492)  
In this case, our function is to monitor

**[00:39:28.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000493)  
and to cite their needs

**[00:39:31.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000494)  
for improvement.

**[00:39:32.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000495)  
So far, we still have room to improve

**[00:39:36.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000496)  
and we will make sure that

**[00:39:38.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000497)  
we will continue to monitor

**[00:39:40.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000498)  
and make sure that all these barangays

**[00:39:42.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000499)  
VAWC desks are functional.

**[00:39:44.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000500)  
Ibig sabihin po, may mga naulat na rin po sa inyong...

**[00:39:47.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000501)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[00:39:48.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000502)  
Ma'am, we have 40,000 barangays

**[00:39:50.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000503)  
and it is inescapable that there are

**[00:39:54.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000504)  
some barangays which do not function

**[00:39:56.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000505)  
as well as the others.

**[00:39:58.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000506)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:39:59.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000507)  
May isa pa po akong topic.

**[00:40:02.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000508)  
Ito naman po yung

**[00:40:03.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000509)  
LGSF funds natin.

**[00:40:05.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000510)  
Ang local government support fund,

**[00:40:07.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000511)  
particularly ang financial assistance

**[00:40:10.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000512)  
sa local government unit,

**[00:40:12.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000513)  
ay ini-administer ng DBM

**[00:40:15.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000514)  
at ginagamit ng LGUs

**[00:40:17.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000515)  
para sa iba't-ibang development projects.

**[00:40:19.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000516)  
Kabilang na rito,

**[00:40:20.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000517)  
ang assistance sa indigent individuals

**[00:40:22.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000518)  
para sa education o scholarship.

**[00:40:26.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000519)  
Gusto ko lang i-confirm

**[00:40:28.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000520)  
kung ano ang papel ng DILG

**[00:40:30.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000521)  
sa distribution o

**[00:40:31.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000522)  
implementation po nito.

**[00:40:33.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000523)  
Ma'am, the sole function of DILG

**[00:40:37.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000524)  
is to monitor the distribution

**[00:40:39.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000525)  
and to present reports.

**[00:40:41.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000526)  
It is the DBM

**[00:40:43.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000527)  
which actually disperses the monies

**[00:40:45.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000528)  
and it is the COA and the DILG

**[00:40:48.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000529)  
which monitors its distribution.

**[00:40:50.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000530)  
Dahil po, Mr. Chair,

**[00:40:51.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000531)  
na-mention na parte po

**[00:40:54.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000532)  
ay magmamonitor ang DILG,

**[00:40:56.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000533)  
may mga naulat po kasi sa ground

**[00:40:58.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000534)  
na nagiging highly politicized

**[00:41:00.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000535)  
ang paggamit ng DILG.

**[00:41:03.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000536)  
Sa barangay level.

**[00:41:04.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000537)  
Madalas, pabor lang sa malapit

**[00:41:06.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000538)  
sa barangay kapitan

**[00:41:07.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000539)  
ang nabibigyan.

**[00:41:08.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000540)  
At madalas, mas malala.

**[00:41:11.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000541)  
May mga pagkakataon na picture-taking

**[00:41:14.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000542)  
lang ang nangyayari

**[00:41:15.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000543)  
pero hindi talaga nakakarating

**[00:41:17.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000544)  
ang assistance sa intended beneficiaries.

**[00:41:20.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000545)  
In your monitoring,

**[00:41:21.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000546)  
have you received similar reports po ba?

**[00:41:24.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000547)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[00:41:25.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000548)  
We have seen that.

**[00:41:26.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000549)  
We are monitoring it.

**[00:41:27.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000550)  
However, to say for us

**[00:41:31.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000551)  
to ascertain

**[00:41:33.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000552)  
whether or not malakas lang sa kapitan

**[00:41:35.660] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000553)  
ang nakakukuha is a very difficult task.

**[00:41:38.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000554)  
To establish civil relations

**[00:41:40.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000555)  
or civic relations

**[00:41:42.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000556)  
between party recipients

**[00:41:43.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000557)  
and disbursement people

**[00:41:45.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000558)  
are very hard to ascertain.

**[00:41:47.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000559)  
But if there are complaints,

**[00:41:48.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000560)  
we are open to investigating

**[00:41:50.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000561)  
if they give it to us.

**[00:41:51.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000562)  
Mr. Chair, huling katanungan na lang po.

**[00:41:54.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000563)  
Yes, po.

**[00:41:54.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000564)  
May mandato po ba

**[00:41:56.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000565)  
o kapangyarihan ang DILG

**[00:41:58.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000566)  
para tanggapin ng mga ganitong reports?

**[00:42:00.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000567)  
Sakaling,

**[00:42:01.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000568)  
may [unclear] kami sa inyo.

**[00:42:04.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000569)  
Ano po kaya yung action

**[00:42:06.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000570)  
na pwede ninyong magawa?

**[00:42:08.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000571)  
Well, ma'am,

**[00:42:08.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000572)  
if we ascertain

**[00:42:10.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000573)  
that there has been wrongdoing,

**[00:42:11.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000574)  
then we will refer the case

**[00:42:13.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000575)  
either to the local government authority,

**[00:42:20.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000576)  
which is maybe the municipality

**[00:42:22.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000577)  
or the city.

**[00:42:23.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000578)  
But if it is of a graver level,

**[00:42:27.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000579)  
then we will refer it to the ombudsman

**[00:42:28.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000580)  
for disposition.

**[00:42:30.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000581)  
Mr. Chair, maraming salamat.

**[00:42:32.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000583)  
Aantayin ko na lang yung update tungkol doon

**[00:42:34.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000584)  
sa Alphard family report

**[00:42:36.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000585)  
or investigation

**[00:42:38.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000586)  
within the day?

**[00:42:39.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000587)  
Or bago matapos ito?

**[00:42:41.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000588)  
Within the hour, ma'am.

**[00:42:42.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000589)  
Yes, thank you.

**[00:42:47.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000590)  
So next to interpolate,

**[00:42:49.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000591)  
We have Chairman Rolando Valeriano.

**[00:42:51.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000592)  
Sir.

**[00:42:53.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000593)  
Recognized.

**[00:42:54.460] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000594)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:42:56.140] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000595)  
Good morning po sa DILG.

**[00:42:58.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000596)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:43:00.020] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000597)  
Magaling na ako nang nagtanong po

**[00:43:01.700] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000598)  
sa kanina.

**[00:43:02.660] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000599)  
Halos lahat ang tatanong ko

**[00:43:04.100] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000600)  
nakuha niyan.

**[00:43:04.900] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000601)  
Pero nadaanan ko lang ito.

**[00:43:06.660] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000602)  
Sek,

**[00:43:08.700] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000603)  
Paki-ano mo nga ako,

**[00:43:10.360] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000604)  
paki-educate mo nga ako rito.

**[00:43:11.500] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000605)  
Ang sabi mo kanina,

**[00:43:12.740] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000606)  
yung mga local gun owner,

**[00:43:15.480] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000607)  
parang gusto ninyo

**[00:43:16.460] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000608)  
inspecting sa bahay

**[00:43:18.120] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000609)  
or sa community,

**[00:43:19.360] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000610)  
paano pong procedure

**[00:43:20.700] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000611)  
ang gusto ninyong gawin?

**[00:43:21.760] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000612)  
Di ba tayo magka-problema sa

**[00:43:24.460] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000613)  
batas

**[00:43:25.080] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000614)  
o baka kailangan natin

**[00:43:26.660] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000615)  
ng enabling law?

**[00:43:28.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000616)  
Your Honor,

**[00:43:29.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000617)  
it is a

**[00:43:30.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000618)  
function of our IRR.

**[00:43:32.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000620)  
And I think

**[00:43:34.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000621)  
it is only incumbent upon us

**[00:43:36.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000622)  
that the privilege

**[00:43:38.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000623)  
we give to the gun owners

**[00:43:39.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000624)  
are also concordant

**[00:43:41.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000625)  
with their responsibility

**[00:43:42.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000626)  
of safekeeping.

**[00:43:44.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000627)  
In both cases

**[00:43:45.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000628)  
of school shootings,

**[00:43:46.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000629)  
the safekeeping of the guns

**[00:43:48.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000630)  
were not in standard procedure.

**[00:43:51.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000631)  
What we propose to do

**[00:43:52.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000632)  
is that we will revise the IRR,

**[00:43:54.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000633)  
inform the person

**[00:43:55.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000634)  
ahead of time

**[00:43:57.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000635)  
that we will put a...

**[00:43:58.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000636)  
we will inspect their premises

**[00:44:00.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000637)  
for how they

**[00:44:02.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000638)  
safekeep their guns

**[00:44:03.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000639)  
so that we can have

**[00:44:04.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000640)  
a checklist

**[00:44:05.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000641)  
and remind them

**[00:44:06.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000642)  
of the proper practices.

**[00:44:07.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000643)  
Mind you, sir,

**[00:44:09.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000644)  
there are eight million,

**[00:44:11.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000645)  
over eight million

**[00:44:13.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000646)  
no, there are over

**[00:44:15.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000647)  
four million handguns

**[00:44:15.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000648)  
that are legal

**[00:44:18.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000649)  
in this country.

**[00:44:20.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000650)  
Both from uniformed personnel

**[00:44:21.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000651)  
and private owners.

**[00:44:24.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000652)  
So,

**[00:44:24.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000653)  
I think it should be

**[00:44:25.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000654)  
the responsibility

**[00:44:26.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000655)  
of the PNP

**[00:44:27.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000656)  
to inspect

**[00:44:28.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000657)  
how they

**[00:44:29.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000658)  
safekeep their guns

**[00:44:30.600] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000659)  
and to make sure

**[00:44:31.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000660)  
it is out of reach

**[00:44:32.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000661)  
of children.

**[00:44:33.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000662)  
Thank you po.

**[00:44:34.960] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000664)  
At as chairman of

**[00:44:37.040] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000665)  
Public Order and Safety

**[00:44:38.280] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000666)  
dito sa Congress,

**[00:44:39.320] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000667)  
anytime naman,

**[00:44:40.240] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000668)  
tinatapos ko lang itong

**[00:44:43.520] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000669)  
appropriation hearing,

**[00:44:44.520] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000670)  
mag-hear din naman kami

**[00:44:46.280] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000671)  
motu proprio

**[00:44:47.140] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000672)  
regarding diyan sa shooting

**[00:44:48.680] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000673)  
na nangyayari ngayon.

**[00:44:49.900] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000674)  
Anong thoughts mo, Sek,

**[00:44:51.580] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000675)  
sa nangyayari na ito?

**[00:44:53.040] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000676)  
At ano yung dapat

**[00:44:54.700] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000677)  
proactive tayo eh, di ba?

**[00:44:56.320] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000678)  
Actually,

**[00:44:56.880] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000679)  
yun ang nagiging

**[00:44:57.460] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000680)  
comment ng tao.

**[00:44:58.100] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000690)  
In the future, ano yung plano ninyo or ano yung measure na ginagawa ninyo para ma-prevent yung mga ganyang happenings?

**[00:45:05.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000690-split-1)  
Well, number one, I have been very vocal in public about Congress acting on repealing the Robin Padilla law. Sir, as of our statistics, 98% of violent crimes are committed with loose firearms, not lawful firearms. Number two,

**[00:45:26.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000691)  
It's about time that we, as a country, reflect upon the use of children of first-person point-of-view shooters, which in this case was Roblox, which was used by the two CICLs.

**[00:45:45.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000692)  
If you look at the game, which I am not a gamer, but maybe there are others here, there are chat groups behind it.

**[00:45:54.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000693)  
And Roblox in particular has a very...

**[00:45:57.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000694)  
insidious community which goads children into acting into their violent tendencies.

**[00:46:05.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000695)  
That's why I'm proposing that Roblox be banned in the Philippines for you, in its entirety.

**[00:46:12.550] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000696)  
So, i-ban yung mga ganyang...

**[00:46:15.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000697)  
Well, not all first-person shooter games have that kind of community, but Roblox in particular has a deep, dark community

**[00:46:23.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000698)  
which incites

**[00:46:29.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000699)  
unformed young minds into acting into their impulses.

**[00:46:32.600] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000700)  
Thank you. So, iwanan ko na po yung issue [unclear] Chair.

**[00:46:35.380] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000701)  
Dito na tayo ulit sa Mr. Secretary, sa budgeting.

**[00:46:39.900] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000702)  
Ano ang utilization niyo po noong 2025? Ilang percent?

**[00:46:43.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000703)  
Ninety-five point two percent, I think, sir.

**[00:46:47.040] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000704)  
And 2026, halos tingin niyo yun din, 95 percent.

**[00:46:52.180] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000706)  
Kasi napansin ko po,

**[00:46:55.060] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000707)  
mukhang may mga budget

**[00:46:57.020] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000709)  
na natanggap or hindi masyadong naibigay yung budget.

**[00:47:00.060] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000710)  
Specially, chine-check ko,

**[00:47:03.080] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000711)  
medyo malaki pa kulang natin sa

**[00:47:05.260] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000712)  
Bureau of Fire po. Opo, medyo malaki pa kulang natin sa mga firetruck, ha?

**[00:47:10.160] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000713)  
Opo,

**[00:47:10.820] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000714)  
at mukhang hindi kasama sa budget?

**[00:47:13.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000715)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:47:14.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000716)  
The budget for the firetrucks comes from the collections from the fire inspection of the BFP.

**[00:47:22.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000717)  
Every year, [unclear],

**[00:47:24.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000718)  
almost four.

**[00:47:25.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000719)  
Four point five billion

**[00:47:27.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000720)  
is collected for the fire code fees.

**[00:47:31.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000721)  
But this is what we remit directly into the National Treasury.

**[00:47:35.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000722)  
Unfortunately, that four point five billion is not committed entirely for the use of the BFP.

**[00:47:41.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000723)  
I think this year, only one billion was committed by the Treasury,

**[00:47:47.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000724)  
although it should be in a lockbox for the use of the BFP.

**[00:47:52.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000725)  
However, sir, we have come up with a novel plan

**[00:47:55.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000726)  
of entering into a government-to-government deal with Korea.

**[00:48:00.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000727)  
We just came there recently, a few weeks ago.

**[00:48:03.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000728)  
And we are in the process of completing a government-to-government transaction of 1,500 trucks over the next four years

**[00:48:11.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000729)  
so that all municipalities will be covered in the next four years.

**[00:48:17.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000730)  
And furthermore, we will now remove the corruption that has been endemic in the BFP

**[00:48:22.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000731)  
in the procurement process.

**[00:48:25.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000732)  
So, a government-to-government transaction immediately erases all doubts

**[00:48:31.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000733)  
because it is only the governments that talk.

**[00:48:35.840] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000734)  
Thank you.

**[00:48:36.200] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000735)  
And the reason why I'm asking that, Sek,

**[00:48:39.000] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000736)  
kasi siyempre kung ano yung maitutulong ng Kongreso kung kailangan na magdagdag ng pondo since budget season ngayon,

**[00:48:47.000] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000737)  
pero maliit lang naman din yung requirement na kailangan.

**[00:48:51.560] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000738)  
So, Mr. Chair,

**[00:48:52.400] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000739)  
I move sana na mahanapan pa natin ng additional budget

**[00:48:57.820] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000740)  
yung DILG, especially the Bureau of Fire, tsaka yung PNP,

**[00:49:02.540] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000741)  
and also ito na rin, Bureau of Jail Management,

**[00:49:05.560] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000742)  
para kahit papaano makaganap sila ng katungkulan.

**[00:49:08.840] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000743)  
Kasi parang narinig ko, ang firetruck is mga forty years na?

**[00:49:13.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000744)  
Meron, meron tayo, sir.

**[00:49:15.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000745)  
Nineteen eighty-six, functional pa rin.

**[00:49:17.080] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000746)  
Baka tinutulak na lang yun, sir.

**[00:49:18.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000747)  
Hindi, gumagana pa naman, surprisingly.

**[00:49:20.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000748)  
That's why yung refleeting program [unclear] is a four-year program,

**[00:49:24.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000749)  
1,500 trucks.

**[00:49:26.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000750)  
Purchased at 40% less of the cost of the previous fire trucks.

**[00:49:30.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000751)  
And it will have a maintenance contract with it

**[00:49:33.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000752)  
so that we will make sure that all municipalities can maintain all their trucks.

**[00:49:38.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000753)  
Sir, if you allow me,

**[00:49:41.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000754)  
if we do repeal the Robin Padilla law

**[00:49:46.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000755)  
and I hope that this distinguished body acts on it,

**[00:49:50.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000756)  
I would request that you add additional funds for the BJMP

**[00:49:55.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000757)  
to enhance their facilities.

**[00:50:00.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000758)  
Because the loose firearms in this country are prevalent,

**[00:50:04.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000759)  
and if we catch them, it will be non-bailable.

**[00:50:06.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000760)  
So we will need more room in our jails.

**[00:50:07.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000761)  
Pag na-repeal yun, mapupuno natin yung BJMP.

**[00:50:10.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000762)  
More than,

**[00:50:11.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000763)  
if you're at 200 capacity, baka mag-300% capacity tayo, sir.

**[00:50:15.860] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000764)  
Yeah.

**[00:50:16.560] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000765)  
As a matter of fact, Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chair,

**[00:50:20.860] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000766)  
Natapos na dito sa Congress yung batas natin,

**[00:50:23.700] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000767)  
yung from 56 to 57 years old.

**[00:50:25.960] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000768)  
Then extend natin yung ng one year in PNP.

**[00:50:28.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000769)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:50:29.420] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000770)  
Kasunod na rin yung sa Bureau of Fire, tsaka yung sa BJMP.

**[00:50:32.900] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000771)  
Are you agree with that?

**[00:50:34.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000772)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:50:35.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000773)  
Sir, if I were to,

**[00:50:36.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000774)  
if I had my choice,

**[00:50:39.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000775)  
sir, I would extend it to 60.

**[00:50:42.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000776)  
And then let me explain.

**[00:50:44.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000777)  
When the law was enacted in 1992,

**[00:50:46.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000778)  
The PNP

**[00:50:48.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000780)  
-INP law was enacted in 1992,

**[00:50:51.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000781)  
the average life expectancy age of a Filipino then was 64 years old.

**[00:50:56.660] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000782)  
And that was 34 years ago.

**[00:51:00.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000783)  
Today, sir, our life expectancy for the Filipino is 70.5 years old.

**[00:51:05.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000784)  
So it is increased already.

**[00:51:07.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000785)  
Health is better.

**[00:51:09.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000786)  
They are more productive.

**[00:51:10.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000787)  
And the knowledge that they have accumulated through the years are invaluable.

**[00:51:15.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000788)  
But I will live with 57,

**[00:51:17.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000789)  
but hopefully over the years we can increase the retirement age

**[00:51:22.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000790)  
because the experience that they gain is much too valuable just to let them retire.

**[00:51:27.480] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000791)  
Pero Mr. Secretary, Secretary, during the hearing,

**[00:51:30.780] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000792)  
kasi ako chairman ng public order noon,

**[00:51:33.440] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000793)  
kinuha namin ang inputs ng PNP.

**[00:51:36.100] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000794)  
And according to them, as a matter of fact, gusto namin 60.

**[00:51:40.000] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000795)  
Pero sabi lang kasi ng PNP hierarchy,

**[00:51:43.240] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000796)  
ano raw,

**[00:51:44.260] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000797)  
sama na yung training nila.

**[00:51:46.000] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000798)  
So pagdating ng 60, medyo exhausted na sila.

**[00:51:49.840] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000799)  
Ang sabi ko nga, nakikita ko pa,

**[00:51:52.660] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000800)  
yung ibang PNP nga nakakatakbo pa,

**[00:51:54.600] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000801)  
even after 60.

**[00:51:55.740] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000802)  
Di ba?

**[00:51:56.100] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000803)  
[unclear] ng hagdan eh.

**[00:51:57.620] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000805)  
Nadadapa nga lang minsan.

**[00:52:00.200] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000807)  
Kaya sila rin, idea rin nila na maging 57 lang.

**[00:52:03.820] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000809)  
kasi parang sinama nila yung training nila bago sila pumasok sa sa serbisyo.

**[00:52:09.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000811)  
Sir, ang 57 is still prime productivity for a policeman.

**[00:52:13.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000812)  
I think in this body,

**[00:52:17.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000813)  
Sino ba 57 years old dito?

**[00:52:19.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000814)  
Congressman Richard?

**[00:52:24.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000815)  
Si Congressman Richard is only 37 years old.

**[00:52:27.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000816)  
So, Congressman Teves, I think,

**[00:52:28.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000817)  
and a lot of us here are past 57 years old

**[00:52:33.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000818)  
but are still very productive.

**[00:52:34.600] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000819)  
And our wealth of knowledge is incalculable into making the department run better.

**[00:52:39.840] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000820)  
So, yun lang, Mr. Chair.

**[00:52:41.140] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000821)  
Sa mga tanong pa, wala naman akong masyadong tanong

**[00:52:43.600] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000822)  
dahil natanong nila.

**[00:52:45.380] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000823)  
I-move ko lang sana na ibang mga natanggal na budget nila,

**[00:52:48.560] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000824)  
mahanapan natin ang budget.

**[00:52:50.980] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000825)  
Kasi nakikita ko minsan pag may sunog, parang 5 is to 1,

**[00:52:55.260] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000826)  
yung volunteer, isa lang yung...

**[00:52:57.340] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000827)  
eh

**[00:52:57.840] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000828)  
hindi ko naman masisi dahil ang tingin ko nga, kakulangan din ng budgetary.

**[00:53:03.520] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000829)  
So kung may magagawa po kami rito sa Kongreso,

**[00:53:06.060] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000831)  
tutal, naghihimay-himay pa tayo ng budget,

**[00:53:08.920] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000832)  
malagyan natin ng kaukulang budget tong

**[00:53:12.380] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000833)  
PNP, Bureau of Fire, and the rest of your family.

**[00:53:17.000] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000834)  
Thank you, Mr. Sec.

**[00:53:18.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000835)  
You're welcome.

**[00:53:18.620] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000836)  
Thank you very much, sir.

**[00:53:19.340] Rep. Rolando M. Valeriano** (asr-000836-split-1)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:53:22.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000837)  
Thank you.

**[00:53:22.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000840)  
In addition lang po regarding the fire trucks,

**[00:53:25.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000841)  
kung ang DBM lang po ay ibigay kahit 72% ng collection po nila in one year,

**[00:53:34.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000842)  
yung 100 fire trucks natin and even 40 plus fire stations in one year,

**[00:53:39.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000843)  
tapos na po tayo.

**[00:53:41.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000844)  
So, tulungan po natin sila.

**[00:53:43.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000845)
