Siguro, there's a need to pressure the DBM para lumabas yung...

**[00:53:47.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000845-split-1)  
25% lang po ng collection nga.

**[00:53:49.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000846)  
We're asking for 72%, so we will support Secretary Remulla.

**[00:53:53.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000847)  
Since malinis naman po ang BFP.

**[00:53:57.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000848)  
Malinis na po natin.

**[00:53:58.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000849)  
And the government-to-government also.

**[00:54:01.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000850)  
But, Secretary, may I suggest also that not only Korea, let's venture out Japan.

**[00:54:07.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000851)  
Kasi yung mga Isuzu parang 40 years.

**[00:54:09.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000852)  
We checked it out, sir.

**[00:54:10.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000853)  
It's 30% more expensive.

**[00:54:13.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000854)  
Okay.

**[00:54:13.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000855)  
Yeah.

**[00:54:14.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000856)  
So, I think Korea is the best option for us right now.

**[00:54:17.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000857)  
Yes.

**[00:54:18.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000858)  
I believe in your wisdom and leadership, Senior Brod.

**[00:54:23.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000859)  
So, before I go to the next interpellator, I would like to recognize Representative Julius Vergara,

**[00:54:33.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000860)  
Deputy Majority Leader Alyssa Mica Gonzales,

**[00:54:39.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000861)  
Congresswoman Florabel Yatco,

**[00:54:43.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000862)  
Representative Zaldy Villa.

**[00:54:47.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000863)  
For the next representative from the minority, may I recognize Representative Percival Cendaña.

**[00:54:54.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000864)  
Sir, you have the floor.

**[00:54:58.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000865)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:54:59.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000866)  
Magandang umaga po, Mr. Secretary at sa buong DILG family.

**[00:55:03.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000867)  
Sige po, simulan natin yung unang round with a question on the burning issue.

**[00:55:07.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000868)  
Matagal na po natin naririnig.

**[00:55:09.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000869)  
Ako po, taga-[unclear] Visaya ako,

**[00:55:10.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000870)  
mga kaibigan ko sa North,

**[00:55:12.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000871)  
yung usapin po ng slots for sale sa BFP.

**[00:55:15.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000872)  
At ito po, recently may nakita tayo, may nahuli na hiring official.

**[00:55:19.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000873)  
Ano po ang nangyayari sa BFP dito sa slots for sale?

**[00:55:22.780] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000874)  
At ano pong ginagawa natin para tugunan nito?

**[00:55:25.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000876)  
Number one, sir, the case is officially solved.

**[00:55:28.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000877)  
Yung slots for sale na isang nahuli natin.

**[00:55:31.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000878)  
Right now, I will defer to the BFP chief to explain our new hiring procedures and how we make it almost impossible to have the slots for sale.

**[00:55:40.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000879)  
Take note that before this,

**[00:55:42.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000880)  
the slots for sale ranged from 200,000 to 800,000 per slot.

**[00:55:48.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000881)  
But for one slot, with our new system, which I will defer to the BFP chief to explain,

**[00:55:53.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000882)  
we will make it almost impossible for that so that the slots will be for sale.

**[00:55:58.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000883)  
Chief Kwan Tiu?

**[00:56:00.220] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000884)  
Thank you.

**[00:56:00.620] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000885)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair, and Your Honors, and to our good Secretary.

**[00:56:04.900] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000886)  
To cut the story short, Mr. Chair,

**[00:56:08.000] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000887)  
the Oplansang Tindig of the BFP, a mechanism that prevents and arrests persons who are into corruptions,

**[00:56:20.880] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000888)  
not only outside but also the inside personnel of the BFP.

**[00:56:24.320] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000889)  
It's working, and that is why just recently, nahuli po natin yung isang SFO1 na nag-engage into anomalies.

**[00:56:35.700] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000890)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:56:36.400] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000891)  
matagal na pong naging kuwento ng BFP ang slot for sale.

**[00:56:41.100] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000892)  
Gumawa po tayo ng bagong makinarya, and we called it the e-hiring that somehow prevents this kind of people doing anomalies during recruitment.

**[00:56:51.900] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000893)  
And so far, wala po tayong narinig on the first part of recruitment because it's system-generated.

**[00:56:57.500] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000894)  
It's online application at the doorstep of their home,

**[00:57:00.880] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000895)  
pwede na po silang mag-apply sa BFP,

**[00:57:02.880] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000896)  
wala po silang gagastusin,

**[00:57:03.980] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000897)  
and during examination,

**[00:57:05.760] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000898)  
automatically makukuha po nilang result,

**[00:57:07.680] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000899)  
system-generated po.

**[00:57:08.920] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000900)  
So natigilan po natin noon.

**[00:57:10.160] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000901)  
However, as I said, hindi 100% na nagagawa namin kasi human na po itong pagdating sa dulo.

**[00:57:15.640] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000902)  
So ang ginawa po nung isang bumbero na ito ay tiningnan niya sino po yung umabot na sa stage na kung saan matatapos na po yung recruitment phase sa kanya.

**[00:57:25.500] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000903)  
So randomly, tiningnan niya sa Facebook at nung may nakita siyang may sumagot sa kanya, nag-offer po siya ng tulong.

**[00:57:32.460] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000904)  
Ito po ay panibagong mekanismo

**[00:57:35.720] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000905)  
na naman na ginagawa ng mga kasamahan po namin.

**[00:57:38.400] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000906)  
Just very unfortunate na kasamahan nga po namin.

**[00:57:40.840] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000907)  
The entire men and women of BFP felt sad on what transpired just recently. Kasi nga po lahat kami ang direksyon ay gusto na pong pag-uwi.

**[00:57:49.160] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000908)  
Nahuli na po siya.

**[00:57:50.000] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000909)  
As I said, epektibo po yung mekanismo ng Oplansang Tindig.

**[00:57:54.560] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-000910)  
And I commend the bravery and courage of that applicant para ho isumbong niya dahil naniniwala siya na nagbabago na ho ang pananaw ng BFP, Mr. Chair.

**[00:58:04.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000912)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[00:58:07.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000914)  
At linawin po natin,

**[00:58:09.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000915)  
so sana simula pa lamang ito

**[00:58:11.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000916)  
at mas maging matindi sana yung pagbabantay natin kasi nakalulungkot po na may galawang sindikato sa loob ng BFP pagdating po dito sa slots for sale.

**[00:58:16.940] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000917)  
Let me move on to a more pressing issue.

**[00:58:19.320] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000918)  
Sa ngayon po, nagluluksa ang buong bansa dito sa sunod-sunod na karahasan sa ating mga eskwelahan.

**[00:58:25.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000919)  
Hindi lamang po ito red flag, kundi isa na pong red alert ang nababalitaan natin na school violence.

**[00:58:32.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000920)  
Last week lang yung school shooting sa Zamboanga,

**[00:58:35.440] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000921)  
tapos sa Tacloban, two months ago.

**[00:58:37.080] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000922)  
Pero hindi po ito yung buong kuwento.

**[00:58:38.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000923)  
Recently yung PNP nagsabi that they recorded at least 50 incidents in schools involving children in conflict with the law from June 16 to August 19.

**[00:58:47.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000924)  
So ang range po niyan ay bomb threats, alleged shooting threats, stabbing incidents, illegal possession of firearms, death threats.

**[00:58:56.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000925)  
Dito po sa nakaraang Senate hearing chaired by our colleague, Sen. Risa Hontiveros,

**[00:59:00.900] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000926)  
the PNP sa Anti-Cybercrime Group said that they had handled 74 cases linked to nihilistic violent extremism,

**[00:59:09.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000927)  
at they rescued 42 minors in connection with these cases.

**[00:59:14.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000928)  
Nagbago po ba ang strategy ng DILG at PNP after the recent events?

**[00:59:19.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000929)  
At ano po yung mga efforts to closely monitor and apprehend nihilistic violent extremism online?

**[00:59:25.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000930)  
Sir, there are two parts to that.

**[00:59:27.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000931)  
One is the physical manifestation

**[00:59:30.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000932)  
of how we deal with the children in conflict with the law

**[00:59:35.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000933)  
and how we prevent.

**[00:59:37.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000934)  
Again, the PNP is present in all schools in the country during entry and dismissal.

**[00:59:46.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000935)  
That's number one.

**[00:59:47.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000936)  
Number two, again, as I repeat, we have barangay tanods who will inspect the bags and identify the children who enter the school.

**[00:59:54.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000937)  
However,

**[00:59:55.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000938)  
what happens in their homes that influence them

**[00:59:59.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000939)  
is beyond our control.

**[01:00:01.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000940)  
I urge the country, all of us, the government as a whole,

**[01:00:07.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000941)  
to act on these nihilistic sites

**[01:00:10.340] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000942)  
and have them cancelled as soon as possible.

**[01:00:14.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000943)  
Other countries have done that.

**[01:00:16.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000944)  
Australia is very successful in handling the use of

**[01:00:20.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000945)  
minors using the internet.

**[01:00:22.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000946)  
China is very good at handling that. It is not

**[01:00:26.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000947)  
censorship but rather

**[01:00:28.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000948)  
it is a filtering out of dangerous

**[01:00:32.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000949)  
sites so that children will not get influenced.

**[01:00:35.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000950)  
You will be surprised at the depravity of these websites.

**[01:00:42.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000951)

**[01:00:55.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-000955)

**[01:01:26.040] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000965)

**[01:01:26.780] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000966)

**[01:01:28.300] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000967)

**[01:01:31.120] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000968)

**[01:01:35.000] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000969)

**[01:01:37.100] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000970)

**[01:01:39.680] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000971)

**[01:01:46.880] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000973)

**[01:01:49.120] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000974)

**[01:01:53.860] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000975)

**[01:01:55.200] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000976)

**[01:01:59.120] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000977)

**[01:02:07.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000978)

**[01:02:18.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000980)

**[01:02:22.500] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000981)

**[01:02:28.060] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000982)

**[01:02:32.480] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000983)

**[01:02:37.460] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000984)

**[01:02:45.420] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000985)

**[01:02:52.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000986)

**[01:02:53.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000987)

**[01:02:55.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000988)

**[01:02:59.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000989)

**[01:03:03.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000990)

**[01:03:10.260] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000991)

**[01:03:14.680] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000992)

**[01:03:22.920] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000994)

**[01:03:34.140] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000996)  
So iyon po yung ginagawa namin, Mr. Chair.

**[01:03:35.800] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-000997)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:03:37.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000998)

**[01:03:38.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000999)

**[01:03:41.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001000)  
kaya nga hindi lamang whole-of-government approach ang kailangan natin dito sa pagtugon sa karahasan sa ating mga eskwelahan,

**[01:03:48.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001001)

**[01:03:51.160] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001002)

**[01:03:54.040] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001004)

**[01:03:59.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001005)  
Sabi po ng EDCOM sa kanilang Early Childhood Dashboard, aabot sa 4,600 na barangay ang walang Child Development Center sa ating bansa.

**[01:04:08.020] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001006)

**[01:04:10.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001007)

**[01:04:13.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001008)

**[01:04:17.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001009)  
Ngayon ba lang po pinirmahan ang IRR ng Early Child Care and Development System Act?

**[01:04:22.820] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001011)  
Sa tingin po ba natin sa DILG ay matutugunan na ang kakulangan?

**[01:04:26.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001012)

**[01:04:32.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001013)  
Sir, the daycare centers are allotted in the budget of the barangay.

**[01:04:36.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001014)

**[01:04:37.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001015)

**[01:04:48.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001016)  
So in terms of this, sir, for additional daycare centers, it is not

**[01:04:53.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001017)  
the DILG's budget that is involved,

**[01:04:56.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001018)  
but rather the DPWH, the province, and the other local government units that are involved.

**[01:05:02.640] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001019)

**[01:05:08.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001020)  
Pero napansin ko po, isa yung ECCDC sa mayroong budget cuts this year.

**[01:05:14.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001021)

**[01:05:25.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001022)

**[01:05:31.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001023)

**[01:05:37.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001024)

**[01:05:39.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001025)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:05:42.340] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001027)

**[01:05:47.980] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001028)

**[01:05:50.260] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001029)

**[01:05:53.920] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001030)

**[01:05:59.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001031)

**[01:06:05.740] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001032)

**[01:06:09.180] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001033)

**[01:06:12.300] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001034)  
Pero ang pinakasignificant po actually,

**[01:06:14.260] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001035)

**[01:06:18.740] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001036)

**[01:06:25.200] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001037)

**[01:06:33.480] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001038)

**[01:06:43.620] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001039)

**[01:06:47.100] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001040)

**[01:06:51.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001042)

**[01:06:56.800] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001043)

**[01:07:03.280] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001044)  
So ang count po sa Quezon City is around 300,000.

**[01:07:08.560] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001045)  
National po in different parts of the Philippines was 500,000.

**[01:07:11.240] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001047)  
So ano po ang plano natin to be able to replicate this in other areas around the country?

**[01:07:16.560] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001048)  
Mr. Chair, sa sobrang maganda na effect na binigay nito sa ating kabataan,

**[01:07:22.800] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001049)

**[01:07:28.080] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001050)  
However, wala pa po siya sa NEP namin. Meron lang po kaming 5 million for Manila.

**[01:07:33.680] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001051)

**[01:07:40.880] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001053)

**[01:07:42.200] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001054)

**[01:07:45.900] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001055)  
Pero given the nature na binigyan po kami ng 8 million, we are requesting for additional 12 million po.

**[01:07:51.960] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-001056)  
Para kasama na po dito yung kababaihan, kasama na rin po dito yung adolescent sexual reproductive health.

**[01:07:56.800] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001057)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:07:58.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001058)

**[01:08:07.060] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001059)

**[01:08:12.500] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001062)

**[01:08:19.860] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001064)

**[01:08:30.540] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001066)

**[01:08:35.680] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001067)

**[01:08:39.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001068)

**[01:08:41.960] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001070)

**[01:08:44.760] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001071)

**[01:08:47.520] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001072)

**[01:08:48.560] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001073)

**[01:08:53.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001074)

**[01:08:55.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-001075)

**[01:09:02.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001076)

**[01:09:11.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001077)

**[01:09:15.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001078)

**[01:09:22.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001079)

Perci,

Mr. Chair,

Mr. Chair,

2,000

**[01:14:00.600] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001161)

**[01:14:06.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001163)  
The reason for

**[01:14:08.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001164)  
the reduction

**[01:14:10.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001165)  
of the capital outlay was because we had

**[01:14:12.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001166)  
to reconstitute our procurement

**[01:14:14.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001167)  
program for the BFP.

**[01:14:16.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001168)  
Before I entered,

**[01:14:17.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001169)  
the corruption

**[01:14:19.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001170)  
of the procurement in the

**[01:14:21.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001171)  
BFP was

**[01:14:22.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001172)  
rampant. But now

**[01:14:25.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001173)  
now that we have instituted a system,

**[01:14:28.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001174)  
you will see that in this year,

**[01:14:30.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001175)  
calendar year 2027,

**[01:14:31.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001176)  
the capital outlay will increase

**[01:14:34.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001177)  
because we have instituted reforms

**[01:14:36.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001178)  
and systems so that the

**[01:14:37.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001179)  
corruption which used to be endemic before in the department

**[01:14:40.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001180)  
will now

**[01:14:41.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001181)  
be almost zero.

**[01:14:44.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001182)  
That is in the procurement,

**[01:14:46.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001183)  
that is in the recruitment,

**[01:14:47.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001184)  
that is in the deployment.

**[01:14:49.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001185)  
All of that, sir, we have reformed

**[01:14:51.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001186)  
and now you can expect a more

**[01:14:53.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001187)  
streamlined and efficient BFP.

**[01:14:57.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001188)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001189)  
Isa rin sa napunako rito. Tumaas ng halos 16 billion ang budget ng PNP pero bumaba naman ng halos P150M ang panukalang budget ng NAPOLCOM.

**[01:15:15.460] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001190)  
Paano po natin mapapalakas ang ating kapulisan kung babawasan natin ang pondong nakalaan para sa kanilang supervision, discipline at accountability?

**[01:15:26.740] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001191)  
Hindi po ba sapat na nagdagdag lamang ng pondo ng PNP? Kailangan din nating palakasin ang ahensyang tumitiyak na ang ating mga pulis ay propesyonal, disiplinado at mananagot sa batas.

**[01:15:43.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001192)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:15:43.860] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001193)  
Kung kinakailangan, i-encourage po natin ang ating mga kasama na mga congressman na dagdagan ang pondo ng DILG,

**[01:15:53.280] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001194)  
bakit hindi nang sa ganoon ang mga importanteng ahensiya na nandito po ngayon under ng DILG ay ating pong masuportahan?

**[01:16:03.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001195)  
Thank you very much, Chair. I would like to commend the performance of the NAPOLCOM.

**[01:16:08.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001196)  
When we assumed office in 2024, there was a 15,000 case backlog in the cases in NAPOLCOM.

**[01:16:15.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001197)  
Some dated up to 2001. So the cases were 25 years old and unacted upon.

**[01:16:23.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001198)  
Upon the leadership of our VCEO, in a matter of one year, we eliminated all 15,000 cases, disposed of it, rendered judgment on it, and now we have a zero backlog profile in the NAPOLCOM.

**[01:16:41.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001199)  
So, the reason for the reduction of this, sir,

**[01:16:43.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001200)  
is what the Chair VCEO requested and what the DBM approved are two different things.

**[01:16:53.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001201)  
So, if you can accommodate the request of the Vice Chairman for the budget needed, that would be most greatly appreciated.

**[01:17:01.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001202)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Siguro naman napakinggan natin ang paliwanag ng ating butihing Secretary.

**[01:17:07.420] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001203)  
Siguro pagtulong-tulungan natin dagdagan ang pondo.

**[01:17:11.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001204)  
And lastly, Mr. Chair,

**[01:17:13.180] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001205)  
last week, nagkaroon po ng matinding pagbaha sa Metro Manila.

**[01:17:20.420] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001206)  
Halos nakita natin kung paano pumara ang basura sa kanal.

**[01:17:28.720] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001207)  
The DILG is in charge of overseeing the LGU on the implementation of solid waste management under Republic Act 9003.

**[01:17:39.760] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001208)  
Ang katanungan ko po, meron na po ba ngang

**[01:17:43.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001209)  
naimbestigahang

**[01:17:45.400] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001210)  
siyudad tungkol po rito

**[01:17:50.440] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001211)  
at tulad po ng sinasabi ng ating isang butihing Secretary, Secretary Boying Remulla,

**[01:17:58.700] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001212)  
na merong mga siyudad o bayan na may korupsiyon sa basura.

**[01:18:06.960] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001213)  
Ang akala po ni Congressman Bong Teves ng TGP Party-list ay may kita sa basura.

**[01:18:15.220] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001214)  
Ngayon po, may korupsiyon na pala sa basura.

**[01:18:19.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001215)  
Thank you very much, sir.

**[01:18:21.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001216)  
I think the greater defect lies in the local government code

**[01:18:25.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001217)  
that we have to redefine. According to the local government code, waste disposal is on the municipal and city level.

**[01:18:37.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001218)  
So let's say Catanduanes, it is not the governor who is in charge who creates a master plan for

**[01:18:45.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001219)  
garbage collection.

**[01:18:48.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001220)  
It is a plan of each and every municipality that is not necessarily integrated.

**[01:18:54.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001221)  
So garbage collection is a scale business.

**[01:18:59.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001222)  
If a small municipality engages in contracts left by themselves, then the costs become higher and more inefficient.

**[01:19:10.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001223)  
If we can restudy the local government code to make it a regional program,

**[01:19:15.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001224)  
a regional approach to solving the garbage problem, then that can be more efficient, sir.

**[01:19:25.120] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001225)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:19:26.600] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001226)  
Siguro ang kailangan din nating i-revisit ang Republic Act 9003.

**[01:19:31.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001227)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:19:32.420] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001228)  
And lastly, Mr. Chair, may I know what the stage of the,

**[01:19:37.720] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001229)  
with regards to the hazing accident na nangyari po kay Mark Kenneth Alcedo?

**[01:19:45.820] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001230)  
At

**[01:19:46.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001231)  
alam ko po

**[01:19:47.120] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001232)  
ito'y

**[01:19:47.400] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001234)  
naimbestigahan ng ating NAPOLCOM through sa CIDG.

**[01:19:52.400] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001235)  
Kasi ang lumalabas po sa aming pag-

**[01:19:55.120] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001236)  
aaral

**[01:19:59.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001237)  
ay isa pong PNP ang leader nito.

**[01:20:02.680] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001238)  
At kayo po mismo, Secretary, ang nagsabi sa akin na pagtulungan natin na ma-resolve ang kasong ito at huwag nang maulit.

**[01:20:12.540] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001239)  
Kaya siguro, nandito po ang ating PNP chief.

**[01:20:17.920] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001240)  
Sana maaksyunan po ninyo ito, lalong-lalo na ang leader pong involved dito ay isang kabaro ninyo, ay colonel ng PNP.

**[01:20:28.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001241)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[01:20:29.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001242)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:20:30.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001243)  
This regards to the death of a student in Dasmariñas, Cavite.

**[01:20:35.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001244)  
We have almost—I think we have resolved the case already.

**[01:20:41.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001245)  
But the anti-hazing law must be strengthened even more.

**[01:20:45.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001246)  
As of now,

**[01:20:47.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001247)  
as of now, the PNP has been vigilant in their own departments, the PNPA.

**[01:20:55.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001248)  
However, in school-based,

**[01:20:57.680] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001249)  
in community-based organizations, we have a more difficult time in monitoring that.

**[01:21:02.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001250)  
I think the best way to do that is to have a community-based ID so that we will know the actors in these certain communities.

**[01:21:12.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001251)  
But that case has been solved, sir.

**[01:21:15.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001252)  
Okay.

**[01:21:16.220] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001253)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:21:18.700] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001255)  
Mr. Chair, this representation manifests its full support of the proposed budget of the Department of Interior and Local Government

**[01:21:28.900] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001256)  
under the productive leadership of Secretary Boying Remulla.

**[01:21:35.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001257)  
At tulad ng sinasabi ko po, I encourage my fellow congressmen to support the budget of the DILG as an agency.

**[01:21:44.280] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001258)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:21:45.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001259)  
Thank you, Kong Bong Teves.

**[01:21:47.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001261)  
Next to interpellate, we have Representative Sarah Jane Elago.

**[01:21:52.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001262)  
Ma'am, you have the floor.

**[01:21:55.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001263)  
Thank you, maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:21:57.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001264)  
Sa Philippine Commission on Women, may malaking budget cut na P27 million para sa susunod na taon.

**[01:22:04.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001265)  
Maaaring niyo bang maibahagi sa atin kung paano maapektuhan ang inyong napakahalagang mandato at serbisyo pagdating sa napakalaking makakalta sa inyong pondo?

**[01:22:16.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001266)  
PCW?

**[01:22:17.820] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001268)  
Thank you very much for that question

**[01:22:20.540] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001269)  
and magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[01:22:22.240] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001270)  
The Philippine Commission on Women has asked for P266,360,000 and it was given only P177,000.

**[01:22:37.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001271)  
Mr. Chair, can we request the PCW Chair to move closer to the microphone?

**[01:22:42.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001272)  
Kindly speak closer to the microphone. We can't really hear you, ma'am. Thank you.

**[01:22:51.620] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001275)  
I'm closer, ma'am.

**[01:22:53.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001275-split-1)  
Please, pakilapit po sa inyo.

**[01:22:55.780] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001276)  
The areas of our work which will be affected by the cuts in our budget proposal are basically the areas of regionalization because last year you gave us like P30 million and we are now in the Visayas because of the support that was given by this committee.

**[01:23:14.760] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001277)  
And we hope that before the end of the administration of the President, we can go to Mindanao.

**[01:23:21.460] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001278)  
If we can be given another P30 million, but for the rest, we are prepared to live with what we are given.

**[01:23:30.020] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001279)  
I think that the most important part of our work is to be able to reach the women. We are 51 years now, but this is the only time that we are moving down to the local level.

**[01:23:42.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001280)  
Nag-issue ang PCW ng proper utilization and safeguards para sa GAD. Kumusta ho ang inyong assessment sa ating mga national government agencies

**[01:23:55.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001281)  
and bureaus pagdating sa kanilang GAD compliance? Mahalaga ito, Mr. Chair, dahil at least 5% dapat na gagastos para sa gender and development mandate.

**[01:24:02.600] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001282)  
Tama po iyan kasi if we have a policy, we have a very strong policy on women, but if we don't have a budget, then that is a joke.

**[01:24:09.720] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001283)  
So we have strengthened our approach in GAD planning and budgeting with our partnership with the Commission on Human Rights,

**[01:24:19.600] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001284)  
which is the ombuds machinery of the Philippines for gender equality.

**[01:24:23.220] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001285)  
And just to show you that we have improved a lot, as of 2025, December, we had 378 government agencies that were registered in our gender mainstreaming system,

**[01:24:37.940] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001286)  
which means that they are submitting and complying. But as of now, July 2026, we have 488, which is about 77.46% compliance in a period of six months.

**[01:24:51.640] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001287)  
Because what we did is we reminded the heads of government agencies about their liability, that they can be charged administratively if they do not comply with the provisions of the Magna Carta of Women.

**[01:25:04.400] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001288)  
So we are pushing hard to get things done. And we are also focusing on assessment of the performance because after one year, they submit their annual report and we assess the performance.

**[01:25:18.780] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001289)  
And before, we used to focus on how much was allocated,

**[01:25:22.580] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001290)  
how much was spent. But this year, we are focusing on what has been the good or the benefits that has reached women as a result of the expenditures on the GAD budget.

**[01:25:32.580] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001291)  
And we are now in the process of assessment. By September, we will be providing awards to those who are complying well.

**[01:25:42.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001292)  
Mr. Chair, mahalaga ang regionalization at pagtitiyak ng GAD compliance ng ating mga NGAs.

**[01:25:48.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001293)  
Kaya dapat ma-restore ang budget ng PCW doon

**[01:25:52.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001294)  
sa 2026 level at kung kaya pa ngang mataasan para magawa nila ang kanilang napakahalagang mandato.

**[01:25:59.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001295)  
Ngayon, pagdating naman sa ECCDC, ito ay nalipat na mula sa DepEd at tungo sa DILG.

**[01:26:06.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001296)  
Ngunit sa paglipat na ito, nakita rin natin iyong napakalaking budget cut para po sa kanilang pondo para sa 2027.

**[01:26:15.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001297)  
Mababawasan ng P232 million para sa taong 2027.

**[01:26:19.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001298)  
What explains this huge budget cut?

**[01:26:29.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001299)  
Kindly answer, please.

**[01:26:31.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001300)  
Ma'am, we submitted the budget to the DBM and that we thought was adequate.

**[01:26:40.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001301)  
However, DBM was the one who cut our budget so that is what was presented.

**[01:26:44.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001302)  
If it could be restored as we asked, then I think we will have a more effective and usable budget, ma'am.

**[01:26:51.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001303)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:26:53.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001304)  
Sorry. Mr. Chair, can we hear from the ECCDC

**[01:26:57.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001305)  
how this huge budget cut will affect their services and their mandate for 2027?

**[01:27:04.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001306)  
Ma'am, kayo na ba?

**[01:27:06.160] ECCD Council Executive Director Dr. Teresita G. Inciong** (asr-001307)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor, the conversion of this daycare center and child development center will be affected greatly.

**[01:27:20.720] ECCD Council Executive Director Dr. Teresita G. Inciong** (asr-001308)  
Because we do not have the FALGU.

**[01:27:23.820] ECCD Council Executive Director Dr. Teresita G. Inciong** (asr-001309)  
Actually, funding only covers the rehabilitation and repair of the DCC, not including contents as provided in GAA.

**[01:27:34.280] ECCD Council Executive Director Dr. Teresita G. Inciong** (asr-001310)  
Procurement of content for CDC ensures provision of appropriate learning materials, furniture, fixtures, and other essential components for child learning and development.

**[01:27:47.580] ECCD Council Executive Director Dr. Teresita G. Inciong** (asr-001311)  
The ECCD Council allotted content [unclear] 360 only from [unclear] under the [unclear].

**[01:28:01.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001314)  
Ibig sabihin ba, Mr. Chair, ilan ho ang bago nating itatayo na CDC for 2027?

**[01:28:13.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001315)  
Ang tingin po kasi dito, Mr. Chair, parang napunta na po lahat sa barangay at sa LGU yung responsibility.

**[01:28:18.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001316)  
Pero yung dati po natin na ninanais na palakasin talaga yung programa natin para sa child care ay hindi na po natin makita sa ating national budget.

**[01:28:30.800] ECCD Council Executive Director Dr. Teresita G. Inciong** (asr-001317)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair, after validation with some provincial social welfare development, we lack all 3,743 centerless barangay.

**[01:28:49.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001318)  
At ang pondo po niyan, dapat saan po nakalagay?

**[01:28:54.580] ECCD Council Executive Director Dr. Teresita G. Inciong** (asr-001319)  
Under the new law, nasa LGU.

**[01:28:59.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001320)  
Nasa LGU. Iyan nga ho, ang malaking problema dito, Mr. Chair.

**[01:29:05.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001321)  
Pinalakas natin iyong batas, pero pagdating naman sa pondo, pinasa natin lahat sa LGU.

**[01:29:12.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001322)  
Hindi naman po lahat ay may kakayahan para pondohan itong pagtatayo ng ating napakahalagang mga child development centers.

**[01:29:19.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001323)  
Kaya ho, at a proper time, magpo-propose tayo ng special line item para po mapondohan itong mga child development centers na kailangan natin sa buong bansa.

**[01:29:26.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001324)  
Ngayon naman, Mr. Chair, pagdating ho

**[01:29:29.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001325)  
sa temporary shelters para sa VAW victim-survivors at sa situation ng ating barangay VAW officers, may study ang PCW na tumatanggap ang mga barangay VAW desk officers

**[01:29:39.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001326)  
ng nasa P500 lang na honorarium kada buwan.

**[01:29:43.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001327)  
Napakababa po nito kumpara doon sa napakalaki nilang responsibility sa ating mga barangay.

**[01:29:49.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001328)  
Napag-aralan n'yo na ho ba kung ano iyong mga dapat nating gawin para matiyak na mapalakas ang mga barangay VAW desk officers

**[01:29:56.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001329)  
at madagdagan iyong kanilang mga natatanggap na kompensasyon?

**[01:30:02.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001330)  
The DILG or the PCW can answer, Mr. Chair.

**[01:30:10.760] PCW Chairperson Ermelita V. Valdeavilla** (asr-001331)  
I would refer to the Secretary on this because we do not have a mandate at the local level.

**[01:30:18.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001332)  
Pardon me, ma'am. Can you, if I can...

**[01:30:21.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001333)  
Mr. Chair, sana hindi po mabawas sa aking oras.

**[01:30:24.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001334)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[01:30:24.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001335)  
Tinanong ko iyong kalagayan ng ating barangay VAW desk officers na madalas ay mga kagawad rin.

**[01:30:30.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001336)  
At napakaliit ng natatanggap na honorarium, nasa P500.

**[01:30:35.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001337)  
Pinapaaral po natin kung paano ba natin matitiyak na mas mataas yung kanilang compensation,

**[01:30:41.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001338)  
magkaroon ng hazard pay dahil hindi po madali yung kanilang ginagawa.

**[01:30:45.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001339)  
Minsan 25-8 po iyan on-call iyong trabaho ng mga barangay VAW desk officers natin.

**[01:30:51.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001340)  
May programa at mga bagong policy ba tayo para ma-improve naman yung kalagayan ng kanilang trabaho?

**[01:30:57.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001341)  
Ma'am, that question is larger

**[01:31:00.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001342)  
than what you have asked.

**[01:31:02.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001343)  
If you look at a barangay, on a barangay level, meron silang lupon ng tagapayapa,

**[01:31:09.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001344)  
meron silang barangay tanod, meron silang barangay health workers,

**[01:31:13.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001345)  
meron silang nutrition scholars, meron silang marami po iyan eh.

**[01:31:17.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001346)  
It adds to something like 50 to 60 per barangay.

**[01:31:21.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001347)  
Now, in the previous Congress, they tried to pass the Magna Carta for barangay health workers.

**[01:31:30.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001348)  
But it was opposed because there would have been a Barangay Magna Carta for Barangay tanods,

**[01:31:37.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001349)  
Barangay Magna Carta for all the others.

**[01:31:40.660] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001350)  
So, that question cannot be easily answered by giving priority to one at the expense of the others.

**[01:31:48.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001351)  
So it is a balancing act that the local government addresses and to what capacity that they can fulfill the expectation.

**[01:32:01.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001352)  
Okay.

**[01:32:02.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001353)  
Pagdating sa gender sensitivity training, hindi lang ng mga barangay VAW desk officers,

**[01:32:08.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001354)  
kundi ng lahat ng ating mga barangay workers, ito madalas sa LGU pa rin po,

**[01:32:13.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001355)  
kinukuha yung ating mga pondo para rito.

**[01:32:16.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001356)  
At tinatawagan natin iyong PCW, again, para matiyak na iyong mga kinakailangang training

**[01:32:21.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001357)  
at iyong tulong na rin para sa stress debriefing ng mga officers natin at mga workers natin at the barangay level,

**[01:32:30.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001358)  
na frontliners natin sa ganitong tipo ng mga kaso, ay mabibigyan din ng karampatang suporta.

**[01:32:37.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001359)  
Ngayon, pagdating naman po sa sexual assault investigation kit,

**[01:32:40.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001360)  
naglabas ang DILG ng memorandum circular tungkol sa pagsusulong ng paggamit ng SAKIT na locally made

**[01:32:50.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001361)  
para sa ating women and children protection units.

**[01:32:54.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001362)  
May nakalaan ba na pondo para rito ang DILG?

**[01:32:59.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001363)  
That's a local matter, ma'am, that we encourage them to address within their budget.

**[01:33:07.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001364)  
Again, Mr. Chair, wala na naman pong pondo para rito.

**[01:33:10.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001365)  
Kaya kung hindi man sa DILG, puwede po nating tingnan halimbawa sa DOH kung mapasok sa mga WCTU funding nila,

**[01:33:21.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001366)  
iyong napakahalagang SAKIT, iyong access sa justice at iyong paghabol sa mga perpetrators ng gender-based violence,

**[01:33:30.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001367)  
mahalaga po iyong timely collection ng forensic evidence.

**[01:33:33.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001368)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[01:33:33.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001369)  
Pagdating naman po sa PNP-related na VAWC, naglabas po kayo ng policy on addressing VAWC among PNP personnel.

**[01:33:40.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001370)  
Yes.

**[01:33:41.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001371)  
Ngunit nakita po namin dito sa inyong policy na pinapayagan n'yo na magkaroon ng pag-uusap, marriage counseling

**[01:33:50.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001372)  
doon sa VAWC victim at sa kanyang asawa na PNP personnel.

**[01:33:56.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001373)  
Ito pa rin po ba iyong policy ngayon ng PNP?

**[01:33:59.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001374)  
Ito iyong policy.

**[01:34:07.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001375)  
It is an existing policy, ma'am, that we try to reconcile the differences within the department, but of course it has its limits, so how it is resolved eventually is beyond our control, but it has to be a mutual agreement within the two parties that the case in the VAWC.

**[01:34:32.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001377)  
Mr. Chair, ang violence against women and their children ay isang krimen sa ating mga batas.

**[01:34:37.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001378)  
Hindi po ito simpleng away lang ng mag-asawa na pribado kahit pa po involved ang PNP personnel.

**[01:34:43.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001379)  
I want to give you an example.

**[01:34:46.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001380)  
The provincial director of Batangas was relieved of duty and about to be relieved from service

**[01:34:54.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001381)  
by a simple text message alone to a fellow policewoman.

**[01:35:01.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001382)  
We take it very seriously, ma'am.

**[01:35:03.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001383)  
That if there is

**[01:35:05.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001384)  
harassment among the ranks, that we will file the appropriate charges, whoever they may be.

**[01:35:08.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001385)  
In fact, maybe a member of Congress was also charged by the PNP for violence against women

**[01:35:17.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001386)  
among the security personnel and the charges have been filed by the DOJ.

**[01:35:21.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001387)  
We do not allow, ma'am.

**[01:35:23.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001388)  
I particularly am very sensitive about this.

**[01:35:27.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001389)  
If there is any complaint against harassment of women personnel in the PNP,

**[01:35:30.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001390)  
I take it very seriously and I prosecute.

**[01:35:33.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001391)  
I prosecute it to the best of my ability.

**[01:35:35.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001392)  
Dapat n'yo pong baguhin itong policy na ito kasi sa ilalim pong ng anti-VAWC law,

**[01:35:40.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001393)  
bawal pong ang pag-aareglo.

**[01:35:42.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001394)  
Lalong-lalo na po doon sa mga kaso po ng physical abuse.

**[01:35:46.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001395)  
Hindi po dapat ito pinapayagan.

**[01:35:48.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001396)  
Hindi po ito iyong simpleng away lang.

**[01:35:50.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001397)  
Yes, I get it.

**[01:35:52.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001398)  
Ito po ay a VAWC case.

**[01:35:54.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001399)  
Kaya po pagdating sa VAWC case, dapat po kasama rin po iyong PNP doon sa susunod sa ating Anti-VAWC Act.

**[01:36:00.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001400)  
Okay. We will, duly noted, ma'am,

**[01:36:02.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001401)  
and we will make the necessary adjustments.

**[01:36:04.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001402)  
Alright.

**[01:36:04.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001403)  
Pagdating naman po dito sa, nakakuha po kami ng report na meron pa rin pong insensitibo na mga nilalabas na anti-rape prevention post ang Philippine National Police.

**[01:36:18.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001404)  
Kamusta po ang inyong guidelines pagdating dito?

**[01:36:23.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001405)  
Ito po iyong ilan sa mga nakarating sa aming office.

**[01:36:26.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001406)  
Ano po ba yung policy n'yo pagdating dito sa anti-rape prevention

**[01:36:30.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001407)  
guidelines and

**[01:36:32.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001408)  
posting?

**[01:36:32.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001409)  
These are, ma'am, just guidelines what to do just in case.

**[01:36:38.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001410)  
But, ma'am, I would like to add that in our Safer Cities campaign,

**[01:36:43.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001411)  
rape has gone down by 60% from April to August.

**[01:36:49.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001412)  
Simply because that we increase enforcement of loitering in the streets, drinking in the streets,

**[01:36:57.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001413)  
shirtless individuals, minors, curfew.

**[01:37:02.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001414)  
Rape has drastically gone down and I think the statistics will bear it out.

**[01:37:06.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001415)  
Mr. Chair, bakit po ito nire-report sa amin sa Gabriela Women's Party?

**[01:37:12.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001416)  
Kasi po, tulad nitong sumigaw ng saklolo, alam n'yo po ba na numero uno hong nangyayari sa mga biktima ng sexual assault at rape ay iyong involuntary freeze dahil po sa takot, sa matinding takot.

**[01:37:27.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001417)  
Kaya po sobrang insensitibo po ng ganitong tipo ng mga

**[01:37:32.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001418)  
post na sinisisi pa yung mga aksyon ng biktima.

**[01:37:37.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001419)  
Tulad po ng paglalakad halimbawa sa gabi, paano po iyong mga BPO workers natin na nagtatrabaho at hindi po maiwasan na maglakad ho mag-isa.

**[01:37:47.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001420)  
So ang tugon, DILG matter din naman po, siyempre, pagtitiyak po ng maayos na lighting, ligtas na kalsada, ligtas na mga siyudad, katulad ng mga nasabi ninyo.

**[01:37:57.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001421)  
At pagtitiyak na iyong anti-rape prevention post po natin,

**[01:38:01.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001422)  
nakatutok sa saan ba lalapit?

**[01:38:03.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001423)  
Ano iyong mga hotlines?

**[01:38:05.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001424)  
Ano iyong mga referral system?

**[01:38:06.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001425)  
Ano iyong mga psychosocial services, legal assistance para sa mga nabibiktima ng rape?

**[01:38:12.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001426)  
At pagpapaalala din na huwag mang-rape at habang-buhay ang pagkakakulong para sa ganyang tipo ng seryosong krimen.

**[01:38:20.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001427)  
Dapat baguhin ito, Mr. Chair, ng DILG, ng PNP,

**[01:38:24.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001428)  
sana po may malinaw na commitment na ma-take down ang ganitong tipo ng mga post at palitan natin ang gender-sensitive na mga posters.

**[01:38:32.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001429)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[01:38:33.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001431)  
Ma'am, just as a rejoinder, this has been corrected already.

**[01:38:39.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001432)  
But also, according to the latest research by OCTA,

**[01:38:44.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001433)  
as of from last quarter to this quarter, 63% of women now feel safer going home alone from their jobs.

**[01:38:53.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001434)  
So I think that's a testament to the PNP and the Safer Cities campaign.

**[01:38:56.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001435)  
However, it is not perfect and we will do better.

**[01:38:59.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001436)  
Thank you.

**[01:39:00.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001437)  
Just a short manifestation, Mr. Chair.

**[01:39:02.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001438)  
Go ahead, ma'am.

**[01:39:03.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001439)  
Opo.

**[01:39:03.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001440)  
May mga documents din po kami na i-request po from DILG, PNP, at sa ating mga attached agencies.

**[01:39:10.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001441)  
At PNP din po ang naglabas ng mahalagang statistics na 1 out of 10 gender-based violence victims ang hindi po nagre-report dahil sa takot.

**[01:39:19.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001442)  
Kaya po dapat manguna rin tayo doon sa paglaban sa victim blaming, shaming at iyong stigma na natatanggap po ng mga biktima.

**[01:39:27.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001443)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair, at maraming salamat po sa mga kasagutan.

**[01:39:31.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001445)  
Thank you.

**[01:39:32.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001446)  
Thank you, Kong Sara.

**[01:39:34.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001447)

**[01:40:54.820] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001469)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:40:58.780] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001470)  
First of all, I'd like to manifest my pleasure at the performance of the DILG

**[01:41:07.050] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001471)

**[01:41:10.660] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001472)

**[01:41:14.280] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001473)  
And because of that pleasure,

**[01:41:17.620] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001474)  
I would like to manifest my complete support for the budget of the DILG,

**[01:41:26.860] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001475)  
most especially for Region 1.

**[01:41:31.360] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001476)  
I'd just like to make a few comments,

**[01:41:34.540] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001477)  
and maybe the DILG can take note.

**[01:41:38.760] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001478)  
The first comment is the PNP road,

**[01:41:44.420] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001479)  
Cadre Road in the Municipality of Lingayen,

**[01:41:49.680] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001480)  
which is the capital town of the province of Pangasinan.

**[01:41:56.980] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001481)  
We provided a budget of 60 million for the widening of this road.

**[01:42:06.440] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001482)  
It remains unimplemented,

**[01:42:11.100] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001487)  
because we have had a difficult time

**[01:42:14.580] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001488)  
getting the signature of the Chief PNP.

**[01:42:18.520] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001489)  
It has already passed through several Chief PNPs,

**[01:42:25.060] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001490)  
and up to now, we do not have a signature

**[01:42:29.480] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001491)  
which allows us to build a new wall

**[01:42:33.660] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001492)  
within the property of PNP

**[01:42:35.240] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001493)  
so that we can demolish,

**[01:42:37.380] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001494)  
the old wall,

**[01:42:39.240] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001495)  
so that we can widen the road.

**[01:42:42.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001496)  
May I know if this is forthcoming,

**[01:42:47.740] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001497)  
and also may I know if the Secretary also needs to sign,

**[01:42:53.400] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001498)  
aside from Chief PNP Nartatez?

**[01:42:57.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001499)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:42:58.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001500)  
That road in question was delayed by a little bit

**[01:43:02.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001501)  
because of the problems of

**[01:43:06.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001502)  
the destruction of the perimeter wall

**[01:43:11.660] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001503)  
and gate of the PNP.

**[01:43:15.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001504)  
So, that was addressed by the DPWH?

**[01:43:19.780] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001505)  
There is a 60 million fund

**[01:43:23.100] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001506)  
for the replacement of the wall and the gates,

**[01:43:26.660] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001507)  
Your Honor, since 2025.

**[01:43:29.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001508)  
And my worry is that these funds will lapse

**[01:43:33.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001509)  
by the end of the year

**[01:43:34.860] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001510)  
and be returned to the National Treasury.

**[01:43:37.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001511)  
We will make sure, sir.

**[01:43:38.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001512)  
We will make sure that it is implemented as [unclear], sir.

**[01:43:40.840] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001513)  
Okay.

**[01:43:41.360] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001514)  
So, that's one point.

**[01:43:43.400] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001515)  
Second point,

**[01:43:44.340] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001516)  
there is an adjacent property

**[01:43:47.540] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001517)  
to the PNP

**[01:43:49.100] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001518)  
which this representation has brought to the attention

**[01:43:55.260] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001519)  
of the DILG

**[01:43:56.780] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001520)  
and the Secretary also

**[01:44:01.280] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001521)  
that was occupied

**[01:44:05.220] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001522)  
by a very, very powerful,

**[01:44:07.900] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001523)  
very powerful political figure.

**[01:44:11.020] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001524)  
And, what do you call it?

**[01:44:14.340] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001525)  
Philippine Constabulary

**[01:44:16.140] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001526)  
and PNP figure also.

**[01:44:22.260] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001527)  
And this person has taken possession of the property

**[01:44:28.080] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001528)  
which is over a hectare in size.

**[01:44:32.460] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001529)  
And we have requested that

**[01:44:36.360] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001530)  
several DILG secretaries already

**[01:44:41.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001531)  
to act on this,

**[01:44:44.940] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001532)  
to file a case,

**[01:44:48.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001533)  
to take possession,

**[01:44:50.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001534)  
that the occupiers need to be evicted.

**[01:44:55.600] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001535)  
and the possession of the PNP be restored to this property.

**[01:45:03.600] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001536)  
May I know what the status of this is, Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chairman?

**[01:45:12.340] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001536-split-1)  
Good afternoon or good noon, Mr. Chair and Your Honor.

**[01:45:18.660] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001537)  
In behalf of the Philippine National Police, as the Chief PNP,

**[01:45:24.920] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001538)  
I would like to personally give you after this the update on the lot that

**[01:45:33.080] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001539)  
the Congressman, Your Honor, had been saying.

**[01:45:43.960] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001540)  
One of the problems of the Philippine National Police, of course, is

**[01:45:49.860] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001541)  
lot acquisition. And this one is one among these

**[01:45:53.920] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001542)  
challenges or problems that kailangan namin makuha.

**[01:45:59.720] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001543)  
And to brief the Honorable Congressman, the PNP lot is now a question of

**[01:46:11.380] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001544)  
our dealing with the DENR, the Office of the Secretary General, Provincial Prosecutor, Assessor,

**[01:46:20.020] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001545)  
and other agencies such that this lot should mapunta sa amin.

**[01:46:25.420] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001546)  
And this will pave the way also for the construction and or giving funds as well

**[01:46:33.560] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001547)  
as the utilization of funds that the Honorable Congressman have said a while ago.

**[01:46:38.480] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001548)  
And the PNP is committed to, committed such that this lot be finally.

**[01:46:50.400] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001549)  
Mr. Chair, matagal ko na pong nadidinig yung committed ang PNP pero wala pong nangyayari.

**[01:47:00.040] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001550)  
E kung sabihin ko po sa inyo ngayon na sigurado lang mababalik sa PNP yung lupa, yung pong scalawag na gumawa po nitong kagagawa na inagaw ang lupa ng gobyerno,

**[01:47:19.060] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001551)  
sisiguraduhin nyo ba na makasuhan itong taong ito?

**[01:47:23.220] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001552)  
Kasi po, nangkukulong tayo

**[01:47:26.080] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001565)  
ngayon ng mga politikong inaakala natin nagnanakaw sa gobyerno. E, ito bang dating nagnakaw?

**[01:47:37.200] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001566)  
E, pababayaan ba nating makalibre?

**[01:47:42.640] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001567)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. The PNP is committed na makapag-file ng kaso after the conduct of thorough investigation.

**[01:47:50.280] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001568)  
So for the information of the DILG and the PNP, there is a DENR Administrative Order No. 2026-11

**[01:48:03.900] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001569)  
dated February 25, 2026, subject declaring certain portions of the unclassified public land

**[01:48:14.880] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001570)  
as forest land for permanent forest purposes.

**[01:48:19.340] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001571)  
Under land classification project number 47-A and 38-A of the municipalities of Lingayen and Binmaley, respectively,

**[01:48:31.500] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001572)  
province of Pangasinan, signed by then-Secretary Rafael P. M. Lotilla.

**[01:48:39.680] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001573)  
Ito po tinrabaho ng representative ng distrito, hindi po ng PNP.

**[01:48:48.100] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001574)  
So,

**[01:48:48.620] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001575)  
solve na po ang problema ninyo.

**[01:48:51.780] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001576)  
Diniklara ng itong lupang ito ay pag-aari ng gobyerno.

**[01:48:56.780] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001577)  
So, what will you do with the actions of this scalawag police that did this?

**[01:49:07.820] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001578)  
If that is the basis, Mr. Chair, Your Honor, for the filing of criminal cases or even administrative case for the subject person,

**[01:49:18.260] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001579)  
then we'll be referring it to that honorable body, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[01:49:23.600] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001580)  
Sana po itong representation ay magkaroon ng regular reporting on the status of this

**[01:49:32.240] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001581)  
and sana po the PNP will act immediately to take possession of this property.

**[01:49:40.040] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001582)  
Kasi hanggang ngayon, pagamat may deklarasyon na ang DENR na ito po ay lupa ng gobyerno,

**[01:49:48.020] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001586)  
yung pong taong yun ay nandun pa rin po at ginagamit ang property.

**[01:49:54.320] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001587)  
Rest assured of that commitment, Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[01:49:58.200] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001588)  
And ngayon pa lang nagpapasalamat na kami sa Your Honor, Sir,

**[01:50:02.860] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001589)  
for that helping the Philippine National Police in as much as this should be utilized by the government,

**[01:50:10.780] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001590)  
particularly the PNP.

**[01:50:12.220] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001591)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:50:13.420] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-001592)  
And it will be a subject of our continuing update to the Honorable Congressman.

**[01:50:19.160] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001593)  
Salamat po.

**[01:50:20.340] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001594)  
Isa na lang punto tungkol po sa mga matagal ng laspag at tinabing mga police vehicle na Mahindra

**[01:50:32.280] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001595)  
na nakaparada po sa PNP Provincial Headquarters for the longest time.

**[01:50:43.190] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001596)  
Nabanggit ko po ito sa isang NLQ, North Luzon

**[01:50:49.060] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001597)  
Quadrangle Committee Meeting.

**[01:50:54.080] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001598)  
Dahil [unclear] ko pong mapaayos po ito at maibigay sa mga barangay para gamitin po ng barangay,

**[01:51:03.000] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001599)  
imbis na mabulok.

**[01:51:04.760] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001600)  
Ang nangyari po, after ko binanggit, nawala po ang lahat na ito.

**[01:51:11.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001601)  
Meron pong nagkainteres na siguro ayusin, gamitin,

**[01:51:20.180] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001602)  
na privado, hindi na po gobyerno.

**[01:51:24.960] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001603)  
Nasasayangan po ako sa pagkakataon sana na mabigyan ng mga sasakyan ang mga barangay.

**[01:51:34.100] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001604)  
May I know kung ano pa po ang recourse nitong representation po na ito

**[01:51:43.120] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001605)  
para mahabol po itong mga Mahindra na ito

**[01:51:47.500] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001606)  
or meron pa bang mga Mahindra

**[01:51:49.940] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001607)  
na pwede po nating makuha para maibigay sa mga barangay po ng aming distrito?

**[01:51:59.200] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001608)  
Sir, 95% of the Mahindra vehicles acquired by the PNP in 2010-2011, I think,

**[01:52:08.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001609)  
are now considered beyond economic repair.

**[01:52:13.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001610)  
If you, in the normal life cycle of a police vehicle, it is usually 12 years,

**[01:52:20.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001611)  
10 to 12 years before they are replaced.

**[01:52:23.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001612)  
Right now, sir, the Mahindra vehicles are between 13 and 14 years old already.

**[01:52:29.200] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001613)  
Mr. Chairman, Mr. Secretary, for your information,

**[01:52:35.100] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001614)  
ang naging kakulangan lang ng mga Mahindrang yan kaya naitenga

**[01:52:40.220] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001615)  
ay dahil kulang ng preno at kulang ng clutch.

**[01:52:44.600] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001616)  
Dahil wala pong mabili dito.

**[01:52:46.940] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001617)  
I agree, sir.

**[01:52:47.600] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001618)  
Wala naman pong problema na mag-order

**[01:52:50.400] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001619)  
ng pyesa sa India para maisaayos po ang lahat na yan.

**[01:52:56.320] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001620)  
Kung hindi na po magamit ng PNP dahil deteriorated na po,

**[01:53:02.580] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001621)  
gustong gusto po ng mga barangay na gamitin po yan para sa mga local functions po nila.

**[01:53:11.054] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001622)  
Okay, sir.

**[01:53:11.763] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001623)  
Yes.

**[01:53:12.160] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001624)  
So, sana po this representation will be informed. Kung meron pa po kami na makukuha.

**[01:53:19.900] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001625)  
We have 231 barangays in District 2.

**[01:53:24.580] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001626)  
and if we can get 231 ex-Mahindras police vehicles, we would be very thankful, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:53:34.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001627)  
Well taken, sir.

**[01:53:35.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001628)  
We will take an accounting and keep you up to date on the status of the vehicles.

**[01:53:38.260] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001629)  
Thank you po.

**[01:53:38.780] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-001630)  
Mr. Chairman.

**[01:53:41.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001631)  
Okay, thank you.

**[01:53:42.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001632)

**[01:54:06.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001634)  
Representative Antonio Tinio.

**[01:54:08.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001635)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[01:54:10.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001636)

**[01:54:13.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001637)  
Paki-ready.

**[01:54:14.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001638)  
In the meantime, pagbati po sa DILG at kay classmate, Sec. John Vic.

**[01:54:22.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001639)  
Good afternoon po sa inyo.

**[01:54:26.140] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001640)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:54:26.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001641)  
Yes.

Ang mga tanong po tungkol sa LGSF, Local Government Support Fund.

**[01:54:35.350] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001643)  
Special purpose.

**[01:54:36.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001644)  
Fund.

Pero may role ang DILG sa implementation and monitoring nito.

**[01:54:41.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001646)

**[01:54:50.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001647)

**[01:54:53.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001648)

**[01:54:59.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001649)

**[01:55:04.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001650)  
Yun ang pinakamalaking component ng paglobo ng LGSF. Next slide.

**[01:55:12.790] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001651)  
At that time, and up to now, pinuna natin ito, tinawag nating LGU Pork.

**[01:55:19.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001652)

**[01:55:26.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001653)

**[01:55:35.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001654)  
So doon ang galing yung increase.

**[01:55:36.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001656)

**[01:55:39.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001657)

...

**[01:56:46.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001668)
