**[01:56:55.310] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001669)  
So, pinapamahagi sa mga mayor.

**[01:56:59.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001670)  
In this case mga mayor ng Luzon, convention

**[01:57:01.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001671)  
nila yan. So, ano ang gusto nating i-highlight dito? Na yung dapat purely bureaucratic transmittal

**[01:57:12.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001672)  
ng SARO sa ilalim ng administrasyong ito, ginawa na talagang full-blown official ceremony

**[01:57:18.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001673)  
ng pork barrel distribution. Ganun ang tawag natin dito. Of course, hindi ito concern

**[01:57:24.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001674)  
ni Secretary John Vic. My question to the DILG is, importante po ang, well, una sa guidelines

**[01:57:37.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001675)  
ng financial assistance, may nilabas ang DBM, at magkano yung assistance? Meron lang general

**[01:57:48.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001676)  
guidelines dito. Pagka-ayuda, yung ibibigay na ayuda sa constituents,

**[01:57:54.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001677)  
maximum of 5 million pag barangay level, tapos 50 million pagka-province and city,

**[01:58:02.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001678)  
20 million pagka-municipality for ayuda. Yung mga para sa AICS, medical, etc. Pero pagka-

**[01:58:12.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001679)  
infrastructure, sa barangay may maximum, 10 million din. Pero pagka-municipality, city,

**[01:58:20.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001680)  
province, silent ang...

**[01:58:23.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001681)  
guidelines. In other words, it's up to the discretion of the DBM and the DILG. Tama po ba?

**[01:58:34.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001682)

**[01:58:39.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001683)  
But, accurate po ba yung sinabi ko?

**[01:58:42.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001684)  
No. We are strictly on monitoring basis and we have no policy-making basis regarding the

**[01:58:49.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001685)  
distribution and utilization of the FALGU.

**[01:58:52.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001686)  
And can I add lang sa answer ni Secretary?

**[01:58:55.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001687)  
Actually, ang LGSF ay wala po sa DILG. So ang DILG lang is to supervise kung tama yung paggawa

**[01:59:05.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001688)  
since lahat po ng local government ay may mga officer tayo from DILG.

**[01:59:11.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001689)  
We are not in the policy nor in the distribution side of the funds.

**[01:59:17.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001690)  
Yes.

**[01:59:19.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001691)  
But in DBM LBC No. 169, ito yung guidelines.

**[01:59:25.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001692)  
Meron portion po roon na, for example, mga requests, nakalagay po rito, 3.4.3.4.

**[01:59:36.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001693)  
The DBM, when necessary, shall forward the request to the DILG for the determination of the recommended amount for each LGU.

**[01:59:45.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001694)  
So meron po kayong discretion.

**[01:59:47.660] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001695)  
If it is referred to us, sir. If it is necessary or not.

**[01:59:53.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001696)  
We have received very few. The USECs take care of that. Yes, it is mostly, sir, on the water

**[02:00:03.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001697)  
systems that we are associated with. But otherwise, sir, there are far and few

**[02:00:11.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001698)  
requests for us to monitor. Thank you.

**[02:00:15.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001698-split-1)  
So, actually, mas iyon ang tanong ko. Yung gusto nating malaman ng lahat ng publiko, malaking lump sum po ito,

**[02:00:24.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001699)

**[02:00:32.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001700)

**[02:00:39.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001701)

**[02:00:43.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001701-split-1)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:00:44.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001701-split-2)  
So, yung 57 actually for 2026 naging 68

**[02:00:48.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001702)  
billion. Tapos, may proposal ulit for 57 billion

**[02:00:54.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001703)

**[02:01:16.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001704)

**[02:01:20.120] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001705)

**[02:01:24.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001706)

**[02:01:29.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001707)

**[02:01:33.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001708)

**[02:01:41.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001709)

**[02:01:53.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001710)

**[02:01:58.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001710-split-1)

**[02:02:02.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001710-split-2)

**[02:02:10.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001711)

**[02:02:13.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001711-split-1)

**[02:02:17.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001712)

**[02:02:18.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001713)

**[02:02:22.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001713-split-1)

**[02:03:39.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001730)

**[02:05:46.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001762)

**[02:05:47.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001763)

**[02:05:51.600] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001764)

**[02:05:55.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001765)

**[02:05:58.660] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001766)

**[02:06:02.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001767)

**[02:06:05.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001768)

**[02:06:50.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001779)

**[02:06:52.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001781)  
It is our position that the BJMP should be transferred

**[02:06:58.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001782)  
under the Department of Justice, under the Bureau of Corrections.

**[02:07:03.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001784)  
Dalawa po ang carpeta.

**[02:07:04.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001785)

**[02:07:08.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001786)

**[02:07:11.980] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001787)

**[02:07:18.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001788)

**[02:07:21.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001789)

**[02:07:24.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001790)

**[02:07:28.640] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001791)

**[02:07:30.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001792)  
use of the resources

**[02:07:32.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001793)  
and also a fairer treatment to the persons deprived of liberties

**[02:07:36.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001794)  
if they have to be released or not.

**[02:07:39.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001795)

**[02:07:42.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001796)

**[02:07:56.240] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001803)

**[02:08:07.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001806)

**[02:10:03.140] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001854)

**[02:10:05.460] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001855)  
Just an overview, Mr. Chair, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:10:07.620] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001856)  
When I ran for mayor

**[02:10:09.200] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001857)  
of Ormoc in 2015,

**[02:10:11.280] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001858)  
for the 2016 elections,

**[02:10:14.020] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001859)  
Ormoc used to be the drug capital of Region 8.

**[02:10:17.840] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001860)  
And when I won in 2016,

**[02:10:19.360] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001861)  
my promise to the people was to eradicate drugs

**[02:10:21.820] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001862)  
in the city and take out

**[02:10:24.200] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001863)  
those men in uniform

**[02:10:27.440] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001864)  
linked to the drug trade.

**[02:10:30.500] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001865)  
After a year, we were declared a drug-free, drug-cleared city.

**[02:10:36.400] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001866)  
And after that, Ormoc became one of the

**[02:10:39.280] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001867)  
safest cities in the country.

**[02:10:45.180] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001868)

**[02:10:47.680] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001869)

**[02:10:52.460] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001870)  
I ask you to strengthen the anti-drug campaign program,

**[02:10:56.740] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001871)  
at least in my district.

**[02:10:59.040] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001873)  
Please do not allow the drug trade to flourish.

**[02:11:04.280] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001874)  
Also, please check on your Chief of Police in Albuera

**[02:11:08.140] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001875)  
and the former Provincial Director of our province.

**[02:11:11.760] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001876)  
Pakacheck na rin yung mga former records ng CIDG

**[02:11:14.460] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001877)  
sa mga malokong mga tao sa amin.

**[02:11:20.580] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001878)

**[02:11:21.840] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001879)

**[02:11:26.380] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001880)

**[02:11:28.360] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001881)  
mga local chief executive,

**[02:11:29.580] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001883)  
sa district

**[02:11:35.160] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001887)  
regarding illegal gambling,

**[02:11:36.880] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001888)  
illegal drugs,

**[02:11:38.260] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001889)  
and illegal quarrying.

**[02:11:41.480] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001890)  
Napakabigat ng problema niyan,

**[02:11:42.800] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001891)  
especially sa environment.

**[02:11:43.660] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001892)  
Yesterday, the DENR was here.

**[02:11:45.860] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001893)  
I also told them about the problem of illegal quarrying

**[02:11:48.520] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001894)  
in our district.

**[02:11:49.440] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001895)  
Although it's not under my jurisdiction,

**[02:11:53.920] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001896)  
but as congressman, it is under my watch.

**[02:11:58.280] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001897)  
Also, Mr. Secretary,

**[02:11:59.900] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001898)  
using our allotted

**[02:12:01.200] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001899)

**[02:12:07.700] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001900)

**[02:12:14.380] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001901)

**[02:12:20.060] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001902)

**[02:12:30.980] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001903)

**[02:12:40.000] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001904)  
So, Mr. Secretary, also, I created SWAT teams in Ormoc City and it's functioning

**[02:12:46.200] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001905)  
very well. Sayang na na-miss namin kayo kahapon because they were ready to

**[02:12:50.320] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001906)  
welcome you and the Chief PNP.

**[02:12:54.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001906-split-1)  
I'll be back October 1, sir.

**[02:12:55.900] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001906-split-2)  
Nice to see you there.

**[02:12:57.300] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001907)  
And Mr. Secretary, peace and order is one of the most

**[02:13:01.120] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001908)  
important programs in the local level to attain economic dependence.

**[02:13:08.440] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001909)  
And with your continued help and support, this progress in our city and in the district will

**[02:13:13.780] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001910)  
be fully strengthened. Thank you, sir. I need, I need your commitments.

**[02:13:18.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001910-split-1)  
Yes, sir. We are very committed.

**[02:13:20.620] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001911)  
Uh, as you can see, two weeks ago I went to Leyte and we distributed, and we distributed additional

**[02:13:28.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001912)

**[02:13:29.420] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001913)

**[02:13:36.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001914)  
ambulances for your district and for your province, sir.

**[02:13:39.800] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001914-split-1)  
Thank you very much,

**[02:13:40.800] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001915)  
Mr. Secretary. Mag-ingat ka lagi.

**[02:13:43.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001915-split-1)  
Thank you, sir.

**[02:13:44.620] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001915-split-2)  
And Chief PNP.

**[02:13:47.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001915-split-3)  
Plus two fire trucks, sir, that are allotted

**[02:13:50.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001916)

**[02:13:51.400] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001916-split-1)  
Napakagandang balita. Thank you so much. Good afternoon. Thank you.

**[02:13:54.620] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001917)

**[02:14:01.100] Rep. Richard I. Gomez** (asr-001918)

**[02:14:41.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001923)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Nawala yung chairman. Anyway, good morning to Secretary

**[02:14:47.580] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001924)  
Remulla and the rest of the DILG, PNP and BJMP

**[02:14:53.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001925)  
and other agencies under the DILG.

**[02:14:57.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001926)  
Good morning.

**[02:15:00.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001927)  
Ah, good afternoon na pala.

**[02:15:02.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001928)  
12:39.

**[02:15:03.360] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001929)  
Ang una kong katanungan,

**[02:15:06.080] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001930)  
tungkol po doon sa seal of good governance.

**[02:15:09.280] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001931)  
Is it really a measure of good governance

**[02:15:12.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001932)  
or is it just a paper compliance,

**[02:15:17.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001933)  
documentary compliance?

**[02:15:19.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001934)  
Yan po yung laging nakikita natin.

**[02:15:22.860] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001935)  
Meron po bang third-party validation

**[02:15:25.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001936)  
itong seal of good governance?

**[02:15:28.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001937)  
Sir, thank you very much for that question.

**[02:15:31.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001938)  
If you notice, since I assumed office in 2024,

**[02:15:34.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001939)  
we have taken out the SGLG for 2024, 2025, and 2026.

**[02:15:41.380] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001940)  
We have decided that the seal of good local governments

**[02:15:46.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001941)  
should be only allotted once every term.

**[02:15:49.820] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001942)  
For the very reason that

**[02:15:51.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001943)  
it should not be a...

**[02:15:52.520] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001944)  
yearly assessment,

**[02:15:53.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001945)  
but a whole-of-term approach

**[02:15:56.440] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001946)  
on how the municipality or the LGU perform.

**[02:16:02.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001947)  
We have studied the matter,

**[02:16:04.320] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001948)  
and just to put perspective,

**[02:16:07.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001949)  
every year, sir, when it is a previous system,

**[02:16:10.580] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001950)  
DILG used to commit five million man-hours a year

**[02:16:14.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001951)  
just to study the documents of the local governments.

**[02:16:19.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001952)  
Now, sir, we will do it once every term,

**[02:16:21.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001953)  
whole-of-term approach

**[02:16:23.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001954)  
into their total value of governance

**[02:16:25.500] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001955)  
and what they have inputted to their system.

**[02:16:28.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001956)  
Unfortunately, there is no budget

**[02:16:29.740] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001957)  
for a third-party compliance

**[02:16:31.280] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001958)  
because that would add it to maybe

**[02:16:33.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001959)  
a couple of...

**[02:16:35.540] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001960)  
maybe another five billion

**[02:16:37.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001961)  
in order for that to happen.

**[02:16:40.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001962)  
Talaga din po, di ba,

**[02:16:41.600] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001963)  
kung mag-determine natin,

**[02:16:45.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001964)  
yung seal of good

**[02:16:46.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001965)  
governance na binibigay natin, ay talagang

**[02:16:50.840] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001966)  
nagre-reflect doon sa actual

**[02:16:53.480] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001967)  
improvement in the services.

**[02:16:55.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001968)  
That's right, sir.

**[02:16:55.780] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001969)  
Ang nangyari kasi, sir, may checklist lang,

**[02:16:58.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001970)  
tapos may compliance,

**[02:16:59.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001971)  
tapos check lang ng check,

**[02:17:01.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001972)  
tapos award ng award.

**[02:17:02.840] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001973)  
So na-diminish yung value ng SGLG.

**[02:17:06.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001974)  
But this time, sir, it'll be a whole-of-term approach.

**[02:17:09.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001975)  
Hopefully, sir, we will see better results

**[02:17:11.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001976)  
in a more comprehensive report

**[02:17:12.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001977)  
on how they functioned as a local government unit

**[02:17:15.920] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001978)  
in the first two and a half years of their [unclear].

**[02:17:19.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001979)  
For example po, in terms of

**[02:17:21.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001980)  
in terms of their compliances with

**[02:17:26.140] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001981)  
solid waste management,

**[02:17:28.360] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001982)  
in terms of their compliances with

**[02:17:31.320] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001983)  
yung pong mga

**[02:17:33.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001984)  
biddings, contracts,

**[02:17:36.100] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001985)  
malaking tulong po yan kung

**[02:17:38.700] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001986)  
yung actual talagang

**[02:17:40.760] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001987)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:17:40.820] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001988)  
actual na nangyayari,

**[02:17:43.480] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001989)  
hindi lang documentary compliance.

**[02:17:46.440] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001990)  
Malaking tulong yan para sa good governance.

**[02:17:48.480] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001991)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:17:49.473] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001992)  
Paano po kaya natin ma-relate itong

**[02:17:52.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001993)  
mga seal na ito

**[02:17:53.700] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001994)  
dun sa actual na nangyayari

**[02:17:55.520] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001995)  
dun sa mga local governments?

**[02:17:58.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001996)  
Sir, we have...

**[02:17:59.060] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001997)  
we are...

**[02:18:00.300] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001998)  
There used to be 10 indicators

**[02:18:02.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-001999)  
for the seal of good local governments.

**[02:18:04.400] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002000)  
But we have reduced it to three main,

**[02:18:07.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002001)  
which is resiliency,

**[02:18:09.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002002)  
fiscal management,

**[02:18:10.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002003)  
and innovation.

**[02:18:13.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002004)  
And then those three, sir,

**[02:18:14.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002005)  
are best indicative of how a local government performs.

**[02:18:20.020] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002006)  
For the information of the body,

**[02:18:21.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002007)  
it used to be up to 14 categories,

**[02:18:24.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002008)  
and each submission would be this thick.

**[02:18:29.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002009)  
And I think it is only right that

**[02:18:31.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002010)  
now we have revised it, simplified it,

**[02:18:33.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002011)  
and made sure that the most salient parts of

**[02:18:37.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002012)  
good local government performance

**[02:18:39.220] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-002013)  
are included in this.

**[02:18:41.140] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002014)  
Will you be amenable if we will be able to

**[02:18:44.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002015)  
maybe enact a proposal or a law

**[02:18:51.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002016)  
that would legitimize

**[02:18:53.420] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002018)  
a third-party validation

**[02:48:05.480] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002916)

**[02:48:15.840] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002918)

**[02:48:19.180] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002919)

**[02:48:26.700] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-002920)

**[02:57:30.640] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003155)

**[02:58:38.700] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003176)

**[02:58:44.960] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003178)

**[02:58:48.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003179)

**[02:58:50.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003180)

**[02:58:54.660] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003181)

**[02:58:58.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003182)

**[02:59:02.080] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003183)

**[02:59:14.180] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003187)

**[02:59:23.800] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003190)

**[02:59:26.720] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003191)

**[02:59:32.880] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003192)

**[02:59:33.720] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003193)

**[02:59:34.720] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003194)

**[02:59:35.720] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003195)

**[03:02:35.060] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003236)

**[03:02:45.160] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003239)

**[03:02:49.960] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003240)

**[03:02:59.000] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003242)

**[03:16:41.120] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003622)

**[03:16:49.040] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003626)

**[03:16:51.200] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003627)

**[03:16:53.260] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003628)

**[03:16:55.360] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003629)  
ng SK Chair,

**[03:16:56.680] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003630)

**[03:16:59.380] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003631)

**[03:17:00.880] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003632)

**[03:17:03.400] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003633)

**[03:17:06.720] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003634)  
So,

**[03:17:08.080] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003635)

**[03:17:09.600] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003636)  
Palalawigan, sabi ng

**[03:17:11.340] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003637)

**[03:17:14.160] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003638)  
Suspension lang,

**[03:17:15.300] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003639)

**[03:17:17.580] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003640)

**[03:17:17.980] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003641)  
So, noong nangyari, nag-decide yung

**[03:17:21.220] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003642)

**[03:17:22.860] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003643)

**[03:17:24.420] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003644)

**[03:17:29.060] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003646)

**[03:17:31.380] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003647)

**[03:17:33.540] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003648)

**[03:17:36.300] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003649)  
And,

**[03:17:37.140] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003650)

**[03:17:38.140] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003651)

**[03:17:40.920] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003652)

**[03:17:42.740] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003653)

**[03:17:43.940] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003654)

**[03:17:46.540] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003655)

**[03:17:48.800] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003656)

**[03:17:52.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003658)

**[03:17:54.280] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003659)

**[03:17:56.980] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003660)

**[03:17:58.660] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003661)

**[03:18:00.440] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003662)

**[03:18:01.540] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003663)

**[03:18:04.360] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003664)

**[03:18:05.820] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003665)

**[03:18:06.940] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003666)

**[03:18:10.040] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003667)

**[03:18:12.360] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003668)

**[03:18:14.240] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003669)

**[03:18:15.500] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003670)

**[03:18:18.840] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003671)

**[03:18:21.600] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003672)  
So,

**[03:18:22.380] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003673)  
ngayon,

**[03:18:22.920] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003674)  
budget call na rin ng local government.

**[03:18:25.720] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003675)  
So, noong ginawa

**[03:18:28.280] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003676)  
ng Sangguniang Bayan,

**[03:18:30.060] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003677)

**[03:18:32.120] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003678)  
Palalawigan ngayon.

**[03:18:33.460] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003679)  
Sinagot sila ng Sangguniang Palalawigan.

**[03:18:36.360] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003680)  
Akala namin,

**[03:18:38.780] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003681)  
hindi nyo nire-recognize

**[03:18:40.460] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003682)

**[03:18:42.280] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003683)  
ng Sangguniang Palalawigan.

**[03:18:44.020] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003684)  
Ngayon, ayaw din namin

**[03:18:46.320] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003685)

**[03:18:47.780] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003686)  
So, for 2027,

**[03:18:50.080] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003687)  
ang mangyayari,

**[03:18:51.300] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003688)  
walang budget yung isang bayan

**[03:18:53.840] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003689)  
dahil dito sa kaso.

**[03:18:56.570] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003690)  
So, may I know

**[03:18:57.820] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003691)  
what the decision of DILG

**[03:19:00.620] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003692)  
for this case

or opinion,

**[03:19:04.520] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003694)  
not decision, opinion of the DILG,

**[03:19:06.480] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003695)  
Mr. Chairman?

**[03:19:10.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003697)

Yusef Benito.

**[03:19:17.160] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003700)

**[03:19:19.380] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003702)  
authority of the Sangguniang Palalawigan,

**[03:19:20.780] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003703)

**[03:19:21.920] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003704)  
of the dismissal order of the

**[03:19:24.800] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003705)

**[03:19:26.400] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003706)

**[03:19:27.920] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003707)

**[03:19:30.340] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003708)

**[03:19:32.100] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003709)

**[03:19:35.000] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003710)  
if you are going to question

**[03:19:36.820] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003711)  
the dismissal of the

**[03:19:39.060] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003712)  
S.K. official by the Sangguniang Bayan,

**[03:19:41.220] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003713)  
again, this is a

**[03:19:42.820] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003714)  
recognized option by the Sangguniang Bayan

**[03:19:45.500] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003715)  
conformably with Section 18

**[03:19:47.260] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003716)  
of Republic Act 10742.

**[03:19:50.800] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003718)  
But, in answer to

**[03:19:52.020] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003719)  
your question earlier on whether or not

**[03:19:53.440] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003720)  
the Sangguniang Palalawigan can take cognizes

**[03:19:55.380] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003721)

**[03:19:56.080] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003722)  
we reply in the affirmative.

**[03:19:58.200] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003723)

**[03:20:00.720] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003724)

**[03:20:01.360] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003725)

**[03:20:03.460] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003726)

**[03:20:05.400] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003727)  
That the Sangguniang Panlalawigan can correct

**[03:20:08.060] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003728)

**[03:20:10.660] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003729)

**[03:20:12.640] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003730)

**[03:20:15.500] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003731)  
Your Honor.

**[03:20:16.020] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003732)

**[03:20:17.580] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003733)  
Yung

**[03:20:18.600] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003734)  
Yung

**[03:20:18.820] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003735)  
Number 62 na opinion nyo at saka ngayon, the Sangguniang can now recognize the appeal.

**[03:20:31.020] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003736)  
The Sangguniang Palaluigan has authority to resolve the appeal because that is consistent with Section 67 of the Local Government Code.

**[03:20:39.500] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003737)

**[03:20:46.760] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-003738)

**[03:21:54.710] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003757)

**[03:22:01.000] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003758)

**[03:22:13.400] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003759)

**[03:22:27.910] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003761)

**[03:22:30.550] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003762)

**[03:22:44.160] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003764)

**[03:22:48.420] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003765)

**[03:22:50.120] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003766)

**[03:22:57.920] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003768)

**[03:24:08.660] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003785)

**[03:24:17.320] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003788)

**[03:24:17.320] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003789)

**[03:24:19.660] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003790)

**[03:24:26.020] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003791)

**[03:24:48.800] Rep. Jose "Joboy" S. Aquino II** (asr-003798)

**[03:26:24.480] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003836)

**[03:26:45.720] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003837)

**[03:26:52.900] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003838)

**[03:26:56.200] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003839)

**[03:27:10.560] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003840)

**[03:27:14.660] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003841)

**[03:27:15.560] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003842)  
So I was just wondering whether the PNP or the DILG or the other attached agencies, meron na po ba tayong nakita, naimbestigahan na meron po ganong klaseng recruitment?

**[03:27:25.600] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003843)  
sa ating mga kabataan?

**[03:27:27.900] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003844)  
There are three parts to this, ma'am.

**[03:27:30.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003845)  
One is the, there are parts of the crimes which we cannot discuss because it will encourage copycat crimes.

**[03:27:40.560] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003846)  
So we cannot reveal everything but if there is an executive session, I can reveal the details of the crime.

**[03:27:48.460] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003847)  
So what we try to discourage are copycat crimes that they get, that the perpetrators become idolized by those.

**[03:27:58.000] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003848)  
Number two, sir ma'am, is the game, the first person shooter Roblox which has a conversation box beside it, which in the chat box which encourages the participants to commit actual violence.

**[03:28:15.040] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003849)  
That's why I've been lobbying for it to be banned here in the Philippines because it has the common occurrence in Tacloban and Zamboanga is both players, both actors were,

**[03:28:26.160] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003850)  
and heavily influenced by the game Roblox.

**[03:28:31.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003851)  
The last part, ma'am, is we are trying our best to enforce it by creating police presence outside the classrooms, outside the school sites, and inside with the barangay tanods.

**[03:28:46.860] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003852)

**[03:28:48.780] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003853)

**[03:28:55.900] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003854)

**[03:28:57.360] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-003855)

**[03:29:01.200] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003856)

**[03:29:12.000] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003857)

**[03:48:31.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004545)

**[03:48:41.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004549)

**[03:50:15.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004595)

**[03:50:21.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004599)

**[03:50:25.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004601)

**[03:50:46.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004615)

**[03:51:22.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-004635)

**[03:51:23.860] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-004636)

**[03:56:36.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004796)

**[03:57:02.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004801)

**[03:57:08.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004802)

**[03:57:15.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-004803)

it's more

**[04:14:17.060] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005263)  
of the same. It just

**[04:14:19.700] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005264)

**[04:14:22.780] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005265)  
Yes, Mr. Chair, and actually,

**[04:14:24.280] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005266)  
that is one of the most delicate

**[04:14:25.900] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005267)  
and ticklish issue insofar as

**[04:14:27.980] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005268)  
the proposed amendment to the local government

**[04:14:29.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005269)  
for this country.

**[04:14:30.380] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005270)  
These things that we are now discussing,

**[04:14:33.080] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005271)  
particularly the sharing between the national government

**[04:14:35.620] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005272)  
and the local government, and the sharing between

**[04:14:37.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005273)  
and among the different levels of local government.

**[04:14:40.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005274)  
At sabi nga ni Secretary Remulia,

**[04:14:42.460] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005275)  
Mr. Chair,

**[04:14:43.340] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005276)  
while it is a very delicate issue,

**[04:14:46.100] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005278)  
we need to start the conversation now, if only to, you know, to bring this to the attention of the policymakers.

**[04:14:55.300] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005279)  
Pinapahinog na ho namin. Actually, sa Kongreso po, sinimulan na ho namin yan.

**[04:14:58.740] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005280)  
May ilang panukalang batas na actually ako po yung chairman ng TWG tungkol sa NTA bills.

**[04:15:05.980] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005281)  
Kaya pinaparating ko lang sa inyo na sa mga darating na panahon, sooner rather than later,

**[04:15:10.520] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005282)  
we will ask for your comments on this.

**[04:15:15.100] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005283)  
Lalo na kung sa tingin ninyo, halimbawa, itatanong ko ngayon,

**[04:15:18.000] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005284)  
tingin nyo yung formula na ginagamit sa paghahati sa pagitan ng mga munisipyo.

**[04:15:24.700] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005285)  
For each category, ginagamit kayo yung population, land area, equal distribution.

**[04:15:30.820] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005286)  
Yung equal distribution yun, sabi 25%, but not all municipalities are equal in terms of income level.

**[04:15:39.180] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005287)  
Some are 1st, 2nd,

**[04:15:40.640] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005288)  
3rd, 4th, hanggang 5th class.

**[04:15:42.760] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005289)  
Ang laki po ng disparity sa NTA sa pagitan ng first and second class.

**[04:15:47.580] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005290)  
At pag pinagsama, at pag pinagsama mo yung third, fourth, and fifth class,

**[04:15:51.700] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005291)  
ang laki ng disparity po nila.

**[04:15:53.140] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005292)  
Ang laki rin po ng disparity nila sa NTA dependency o yung paggamit ng NTA.

**[04:16:00.000] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005293)  
Patayan, palagay ko, kung sasangayunan ninyo, na i-revisah itong NTA formula.

**[04:16:06.580] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005294)  
Tama po ba?

**[04:16:07.780] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005295)  
We fully agree, Mr. Chair.

**[04:16:09.520] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005296)  
Not all local.

**[04:16:10.420] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005297)  
governments are created equal.

**[04:16:11.520] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005298)  
While it is true that LG, for example, at the level of municipalities,

**[04:16:15.940] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005299)  
the basis for the sharing is 50% population, 25% land area, and 25% equal sharing,

**[04:16:25.740] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005300)  
their local governments should really need more.

**[04:16:29.060] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005301)  
If I may proceed, sir?

**[04:16:31.980] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005302)  
There's a recommendation that we look at the 25% equal sharing.

**[04:16:35.460] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005303)  
It should be equity sharing, which means giving more to those who need more.

**[04:16:38.640] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005304)  
Like, for example, there are municipalities.

**[04:16:40.400] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005305)  
which are located in the mountainous areas,

**[04:16:42.500] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005306)  
which means a kilometer of road would cost three times than a kilometer of road in the lowlands.

**[04:16:48.700] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005307)  
There are LGUs which are island local governments.

**[04:16:52.660] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005308)  
There are LGUs which are often visited by typhoons and calamities.

**[04:16:57.320] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005309)  
There are LGUs which are located in conflict-affected areas.

**[04:17:00.080] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005310)  
So these are our recommendations to look into how we can further help these local governments.

**[04:17:05.420] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005311)

**[04:17:10.480] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-005312)

**[04:17:16.420] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005313)

**[04:33:59.900] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005597)

**[04:34:05.820] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005598)

**[04:34:07.940] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005599)

**[04:34:27.960] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005601)

**[04:34:35.120] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005602)

**[04:34:57.760] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005607)

**[04:35:05.500] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005609)

**[04:35:10.320] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005610)

**[04:35:17.340] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005611)

**[04:35:26.220] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005613)

**[04:35:33.460] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005614)

**[04:35:41.400] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005615)

**[04:35:49.660] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005616)

**[04:35:56.700] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005619)

**[04:36:01.100] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005620)

**[04:36:06.520] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005621)

**[04:36:10.440] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005622)

**[04:36:14.400] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005623)

**[04:36:22.040] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005624)

**[04:36:52.100] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005629)

**[04:36:56.480] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-005631)  
maraming salamat, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:37:24.480] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005637)  
May iksi lang yung tanong ko, actually.

**[04:37:27.660] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005638)  
Right now, nag-meeting kami nung we had the initial meeting with the Department of Budget and Management when

**[04:37:32.740] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005639)  
the budget was presented.

**[04:37:35.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005640)  
So we have a recurring issue.

**[04:37:36.700] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005641)  
Not only in our city but in other cities as well.

**[04:37:40.340] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005642)  
At tinuturo kami sa inyo to find a resolution.

**[04:37:44.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005643)  
Ang nangyayari kasi sa amin is marami kaming mga programs under the GAA ng 2026, even 2025, na

**[04:37:54.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005644)  
hindi kami nabibigyan ng building permit.

**[04:37:56.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005645)  
For one reason or the other, nagpapabalik-balik kami,

**[04:37:59.780] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005646)

**[04:38:06.960] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005647)  
point in time.

**[04:38:07.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005648)  
So, tinatanong ko sa DBM, ang sabi sa akin, sa DILG, kami humingi ng sangguni kung saan kami.

**[04:38:14.420] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005649)  
Because obviously, when many of these programs are stranded at hindi sa nadi-dispense

**[04:38:20.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005650)  
o na-carry out, yung programming ng gobyerno hindi nangyayari.

**[04:38:26.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005651)  
At yung budget na yun ay nasasayang.

**[04:38:29.280] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005652)  
So, we're talking about schools, we're talking about facilities, health facilities, but primarily

**[04:38:36.100] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005653)  
schools

**[04:38:36.520] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005654)  
talaga

**[04:38:37.080] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005655)  
yung hindi namin naipapatayo.

**[04:38:38.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005656)  
...

**[04:38:38.640] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005657)  
Ano bang maitutulong sa amin dyan?

**[04:38:40.720] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005658)  
Ano bang aming recourse pagdating sa DILG?

**[04:38:47.280] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005659)  
Sir, as far as requirements for example for the issuance of building permits is concerned,

**[04:38:54.720] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005660)  
there's already a set of requirements.

**[04:38:56.660] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005661)

**[04:39:02.200] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005662)  
There should be no reason why the building permit is not issued.

**[04:39:07.580] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005663)  
Yeah, but there are fine nuances of every requirement eh.

**[04:39:11.260] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005664)

**[04:39:16.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005665)  
bordering already in fact beyond being reasonable.

**[04:39:20.040] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005666)  
So pwede ba namin yung isangguni po sa inyo yun?

**[04:39:22.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005667)  
Kasi I think this is already a matter that requires the attention of the DILG using its

**[04:39:29.060] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005668)

**[04:39:33.980] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005669)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:39:34.780] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005670)

**[04:39:38.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005671)  
So, yun lang po,

**[04:39:40.260] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005672)  
Bago ako sumulat sa inyo, kasi noon na sa DBM ko talaga gustong i-raise, pero

**[04:39:44.820] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005673)  
ang sabi nga ng DBM, sa DILG ninyo yan kasi sila may mandato dyan.

**[04:39:49.220] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005674)  
So, we will provide you with all the data we have so that malaman namin.

**[04:39:55.580] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005675)  
Kasi, obviously, if it requires disciplinary action on the part or whether it's an admin

**[04:40:00.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005676)

**[04:40:04.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005677)

**[04:40:09.780] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005678)

**[04:40:16.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005679)

**[04:40:19.320] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005680)  
So yun po nga akong sinasangguni sa inyo, Usec.

**[04:40:22.180] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005681)  
Yes, Mr. Chair,

**[04:40:23.200] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-005682)

**[04:41:10.080] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-005701)

**[04:41:42.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005716)  
to the DILG.

**[04:41:44.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005717)

**[04:41:50.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005718)

**[04:42:06.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005720)

**[04:42:06.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005721)

**[04:42:15.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005722)  
teachers

**[04:42:17.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005723)

**[04:42:29.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005725)

**[04:42:38.780] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005727)

**[04:42:41.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005731)

**[04:42:48.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005732)

**[04:43:03.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005733)  
the moment there is a threat. Regular and school visits tumutulong sa safety orientation,

**[04:43:08.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005734)  
etc. Can the PNP pilot this immediately in high-risk or densely populated school areas

**[04:43:15.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005735)  
and eventually ensure that every school has an identified police officer or police team?

the increase in appropriations for the Philippine National Police

the amount of 15 billion, your honor, Mr. Chair.

the 5 increase as granted by the President through Executive Order,

the second trans. So yun lang po yung increase na yun. So we have an increase of

billion.

**[04:44:10.000] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-005742)

able to get

**[05:03:31.060] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006107)

**[05:03:35.480] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006108)

**[05:03:41.180] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006109)

**[05:03:47.400] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006110)

**[05:03:56.920] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006111)

**[05:04:01.420] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006112)

**[05:04:05.080] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006113)

**[05:04:08.140] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006114)

**[05:04:16.840] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006115)

**[05:04:21.740] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006116)  
The Oplan Santindig is working the mechanism to ensure that anomalies and corruptions are being undertaken and addressed immediately.

**[05:04:30.340] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006117)  
Hopefully.

**[05:04:30.940] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006118)  
Mangyari pong malinis natin through a new form of recruitment.

**[05:04:36.180] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006119)  
We have e-hiring. It's an online. At the doorstep of the applicants, they can immediately apply to the Fire Bureau

**[05:04:42.440] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006120)

**[05:04:46.760] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006121)

**[05:04:50.780] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006122)  
They can immediately get the results.

**[05:04:53.600] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006123)  
With this kind of e-hiring mechanism, we can assure that at the very start of recruitment, medyo malinis po natin.

**[05:05:00.940] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006124)

**[05:05:04.620] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006125)

**[05:05:09.480] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006126)

**[05:05:18.120] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006127)

**[05:05:25.320] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006128)

**[05:05:29.220] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-006129)

**[05:08:19.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006169)

**[05:08:36.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006171)

**[05:08:44.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006172)

**[05:08:48.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006173)

**[05:09:00.520] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006177)

**[05:09:04.960] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006178)

**[05:09:07.800] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006179)

**[05:09:11.580] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006180)

**[05:09:14.260] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006181)

**[05:09:17.980] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006182)

**[05:09:39.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-006186)

**[05:09:47.980] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006187)

We are the secretariat.

**[05:09:53.820] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006189)

**[05:09:58.140] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006190)

**[05:10:01.860] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006191)

**[05:10:03.420] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006192)

**[05:10:08.460] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006193)

**[05:10:11.720] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006194)

**[05:10:17.160] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006195)  
not necessarily in line with the educational standards.

**[05:10:20.560] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006196)

**[05:10:29.520] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006197)

**[05:10:31.220] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006198)

**[05:10:33.140] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006199)

**[05:10:39.620] NYC Chairperson and CEO Joseph Francisco R. Ortega** (asr-006201)

**[05:44:29.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006716)  
Next to interpellate is Congressman Edgar Erice.

**[05:44:34.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006717)  
He's not around.

**[05:44:36.001] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006718)  
So we have Representative Antonio Tinio.

**[05:44:38.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006719)  
Congressman Tinio, you have the floor, sir.

**[05:44:41.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006720)  
Ah, hindi na ba?

**[05:44:43.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006721)  
Okay, you, sir.

**[05:44:44.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006722)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[05:44:47.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006723)  
Well, una kaugnay po sa BJMP.

**[05:44:53.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006724)  
So, kanina may nirace akong issue and regarding yung problema nga sa mga records, yung karpeta.

**[05:45:04.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006725)  
At may initial na napabutin tayo sa BJMP.

**[05:45:11.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006726)  
However, may nakausap, actually nandito sila ngayon, grupo ng mga former detainees at mga advocates for detainees' rights.

**[05:45:24.601] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006727)  
And they brought something to my attention na tama po ba na mayroong patakaran ng BJMP regarding visits.

**[05:45:32.721] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006728)  
Na ang pinahintulutan nyo lang ay ang family.

**[05:45:36.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006729)  
Yung mga pamilya ang pwedeng bumisita.

**[05:45:39.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006730)  
Pamilya lamang ang pwedeng bumisita apart from yung legal representation or lawyers.

**[05:45:46.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006731)  
Tama po ba?

**[05:45:49.780] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006732)  
Mr. Chairman, Your Honor.

**[05:45:56.040] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006733)  
Per policy, Mr. Chairman, Your Honor, the lawyer, the doctors, and the direct family of the PDL are allowed to visit.

**[05:46:07.141] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006734)  
And the PDL can also request to the warden kung may gusto pa siyang mag-visit sa kanya.

**[05:46:15.201] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006735)  
But hindi po ito, Your Honor, na pupunta yung bisita doon na hindi nag-request yung PDL natin.

**[05:46:25.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006736)  
Well, Chair, ito nga yung nakakarating sa amin na ulat na kakaiba yung patakaran nyo with regard to non-direct family members.

**[05:46:36.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006737)  
At ang sinabi rin sa akin na hindi ganito ang patakaran ng BuCor, halimbawa.

**[05:46:41.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006738)  
Na mas kayo pa, BJMP, ang may napaka-strictong patakaran na mainly direct family ang puwede.

**[05:46:51.010] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006739)  
Pero may iba pang requirements kapag hindi direct.

**[05:46:55.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006740)  
Ngayon, bakit issue to?

**[05:46:57.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006741)  
Marami pong may mga kaso po na mayroon tayong mga detainees na hindi maaaring nasa ibang isla, malayo.

**[05:47:06.601] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006742)  
Ang kanilang direct family, ibig sabihin kahit talimbawa best friend ka, kababata mo, hindi basta-bastang pwede mong bisitahin.

**[05:47:15.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006743)  
Ganun po ang inuulat sa amin.

**[05:47:21.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006744)  
Mga konkretong instances sa Bohol at saka sa Negros, ganun daw po.

**[05:47:30.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006745)  
At sinasabi rin na ang BuCor hindi ganun, mas mahigpit pa kayo sa BuCor.

**[05:47:35.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006746)  
Bakit ko hina-highlight ito?

**[05:47:37.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006747)  
So, sa BuCor, alam natin, iyon ay para sa mga convicted.

**[05:47:43.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006748)  
Whereas ang BJMP, detainees, innocent until proven guilty in the end. Diba?

**[05:47:51.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006749)  
So bakit po, mas mahigpit pa kayo?

**[05:47:55.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006750)  
At hindi ba ito paglabag sa karapatan?

**[05:48:00.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006751)  
Parang inhuman, cruel and inhuman.

**[05:48:05.021] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006752)  
Na naghihigpit kayo sa karapatan na mabisita, kahit ng hindi direct family.

**[05:48:11.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006753)  
Dahil nga, hindi sa lahat ng pagkakataon, ay readily nandyan bilang support system ang direct family.

**[05:48:19.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006754)  
Gusto ko lang po natin marinig, bakit may ganitong partikular na sa BJMP lang?

**[05:48:25.620] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006755)  
Mr. Chairman, Your Honor, hindi naman po namin pinagbabawal yung hindi direct family na bumisita sa jail facility natin.

**[05:48:35.720] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006756)  
Ang pinahintulutan po natin yan, pag yung PDL po natin mismo, ay mag-allow sa person na yun na bibisita sa kanya.

**[05:48:47.570] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006757)  
Hindi lang po natin ina-allow na automatic na pagdating doon, wala siyang, hindi naman in-allow ng PDL yun na bibisita ka sa kanya.

**[05:48:57.210] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006758)  
Baka kasi, hindi naman gusto ng PDL yung tao na yun na bibisita sa kanya.

**[05:49:02.820] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006759)  
Kaya, we need the clearance first.

**[05:49:05.041] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006760)  
From the PDL na pwedeng bumisita sa kanya yung taong yun.

**[05:49:11.201] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006761)  
Kaya kailangan, ilista niya yung name na yun, isubmit niya sa warden natin para makabisita sa kanya.

**[05:49:18.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006762)  
So, ibig sabihin, kailangan, bago dumating kailangan, mayroon authorization muna sa bisita?

**[05:49:23.840] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006763)  
Sabihin muna ng PDL po na ito, ina-allow kong magbibisita sa...

**[05:49:28.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006764)  
Mr. Chair, hindi ba puwede na halimbawa, dumating doon, gustong makita yung PDL,

**[05:49:37.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006765)  
Edi simple lang naman yan eh, itatanong lang naman sa kanya kung okay ba sa iyo na may bumisita sa iyo?

**[05:49:47.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006766)  
Tama kayo, importante yung consent ng PDL.

**[05:49:51.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006767)  
Pero then and there, pwede naman tanungin siya, o gusto mo bang makita to?

**[05:49:55.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006768)  
Ito, pag sinabi niyang yes, hindi ba satisfied na?

**[05:50:03.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006769)  
Rather than, kailangan muna...

**[05:50:06.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006770)  
Ilista niya. E paano niya malalaman? Halimbawa, may gustong, may grupo na gustong tumulong sa particular na individual na ito.

**[05:50:16.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006771)  
E hindi niya alam. Ito, paano niya marirequest yun?

**[05:50:20.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006772)  
Can I interject with your indulgence?

**[05:50:23.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006773)  
Ang tinatanong siguro, ano ba yung guidelines niyo po? May written guidelines po ba kayo?

**[05:50:30.240] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006774)  
May standard operating procedure, Mr. Chair, your honors, regarding that matter.

**[05:50:34.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006775)  
Okay.

**[05:50:35.160] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006776)  
Direct family doctors.

**[05:50:38.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006777)  
Can you submit your standard operating procedure to this committee?

**[05:50:42.880] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006778)  
Yes, Mr. Chairman.

**[05:50:44.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006779)  
So that I can furnish also?

**[05:50:46.240] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006780)  
Yes, Congressman.

**[05:50:47.280] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006781)  
But we are very willing to listen to those concerns, Mr. Chairman, Your Honor, and willing to sit down and study if we can make changes in our path.

**[05:51:03.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006782)  
Yeah, yung sinasabi ni Kong Tinio, part ba yun ng guidelines,

**[05:51:06.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006783)  
yung mga biglaan?

**[05:51:08.880] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006784)  
Hindi po pwede yung biglaan, your honor, because...

**[05:51:12.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006785)  
What's the reason?

**[05:51:13.880] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006786)  
Majority of our jail facilities are locked spaces for visitation, your honor.

**[05:51:22.340] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006787)  
That’s why ang inaalaw lang talaga natin muna.

**[05:51:27.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006788)  
Siniset niyo yung...

**[05:51:28.441] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006789)  
Yes, yung direct family at saka yung doctor at saka yung lawyers niya, Mr. Chairman, your honor.

**[05:51:33.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006790)  
Yeah, but submit yung standard operating procedure, please.

**[05:51:36.280] BJMP Chief Jail Director Efren A. Nemeño** (asr-006791)  
Yes, we will submit, Mr. Chairman.

**[05:51:38.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006792)  
Thank you for that, Chair.

**[05:51:40.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006793)  
Maraming salamat po for requesting the written guidelines or standard operating procedures.

**[05:51:46.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006794)  
Thank you rin kay director for openness to reviewing such guidelines.

**[05:51:55.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006795)  
Ang gusto lang po natin ibalance, of course, sabi niyo may mga considerations kayo, pero kailangan din yung kilalanin na may mga karapatan ng mga PDL.

**[05:52:05.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006796)  
So malinaw po ngayon.

**[05:52:06.981] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006797)  
Hindi ina-allow na biglaan may bumisita, talagang i-deny.

**[05:52:12.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006798)  
So kailangan talaga nakalista ng PDL.

**[05:52:17.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006799)  
Pero para sa amin, deprivation of rights yun.

**[05:52:23.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006800)

Thank you, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[05:54:04.000] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006819)  
Insofar as the proposed government-to-government procurement,

**[05:54:08.520] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006820)  
fire trucks with the Republic of Korea,

**[05:54:10.541] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006821)  
the department is having advanced discussions with the relevant agencies

**[05:54:13.361] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006822)

**[05:54:17.500] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006823)

**[05:54:20.180] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006824)

**[05:54:25.540] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006825)  
Sila po kasi talaga yung mag-open po ng bid po niyan.

**[05:54:30.280] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006826)  
They will conduct evaluation.

**[05:54:32.340] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006827)  
They will constitute two committees to screen the proposed suppliers.

**[05:54:37.421] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006828)

**[05:54:43.980] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006829)  
And then once, ano po yan, na-vet pa po nila yan,

**[05:54:48.260] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006830)  
they will send the recommendation of the suppliers.

**[05:54:50.700] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006831)

**[05:54:53.421] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006832)

**[05:55:09.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006837)

**[05:55:14.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006838)

**[05:55:17.920] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006839)

**[05:55:19.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006840)

**[05:55:25.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006841)

**[05:55:26.780] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006842)

**[05:55:27.081] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006843)

**[05:55:32.080] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006844)  
Wala po.

**[05:55:32.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006845)

**[05:55:35.360] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006846)  
Tama po, Mr. Chair.

**[05:55:36.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006847)  
However, the status is, it is still

**[05:55:38.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-006848)

**[05:55:40.520] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006849)

**[05:55:43.421] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006850)

**[05:55:50.940] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006851)

**[05:55:53.980] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006852)

**[05:55:58.740] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006853)  
Yun po.

**[05:55:59.480] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006854)

**[05:56:02.540] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006855)

**[05:56:06.340] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006856)  
So, yun ho, we will continue coordinating.

**[05:56:07.340] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006857)  
We will continue coordinating with the Republic of Korea on this one.

**[05:56:11.760] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-006858)  
With KOTRA.

**[05:57:24.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006880)

**[05:58:53.041] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006916)

**[05:58:54.781] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006917)

**[05:59:09.280] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006921)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair,

**[05:59:11.000] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006922)  
totoo po yan.

**[05:59:12.980] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006923)  
Katulad ng sinabi ko kanina,

**[05:59:14.640] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006924)  
as deputized agents of the

**[05:59:18.041] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006925)  
Philippine Drug Enforcement Agency,

**[05:59:22.520] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006926)  
ay meron po tayong programa.

**[05:59:24.460] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006927)  
And the program in our campaign against illegal drugs

**[05:59:27.241] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006928)  
is no less than the supply and demand reduction.

**[05:59:30.741] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006929)  
On the part of

**[05:59:32.341] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006930)  
sa demand reduction,

**[05:59:35.040] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006931)  
marami po tayong mga programa.

**[05:59:36.461] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006932)  
Like, for example,

**[05:59:37.620] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006933)  
we go with,

**[05:59:39.630] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006934)  
with the community.

**[05:59:40.451] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006935)  
The community in various campaign para maiwasan ang pagtangkil sa droga and the like.

**[05:59:48.110] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006936)  
And we are also on the front line with regards to the supply reduction, especially in the conduct of police operations.

**[06:00:00.200] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006937)  
The conduct of police operations, nagsisimula po yan one by one painstakingly sa the conduct of information and intelligence gathering such that meron po tayong probable person of interest or target.

**[06:00:19.300] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006938)  
At yan po ang pinupuso natin. And that is why meron po tayong minimaintain na persons of interest or target person.

**[06:00:29.940] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006939)  
These target personalities came from doon sa mga nahuhuli na mismo natin, those who have been arrested based on interview and based on the arrests that were made in the last several years.

**[06:00:49.761] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006940)  
At tuloy-tuloy po yun. And so that's how we maintain our list. That's why we have our list.

**[06:01:29.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006947)

**[06:01:59.381] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-006952)
