**[06:04:21.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006993)  
My next question is in relation po to the Barangay Development Program.

**[06:04:27.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006994)  
Dahil from last year,

**[06:04:30.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006995)  
naging parang 10 million per barangay, naging 5 million na lang per barangay.

**[06:04:35.261] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006996)  
And dumami po yung mga barangay na beneficiaries neto.

**[06:04:38.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006997)  
May tanong ko, ano po bang actual physical and financial accomplishment rate

**[06:04:44.221] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006998)  
ng Barangay Development Program na bilyong piso po talaga yung nailalaan dito?

**[06:04:49.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-006999)  
And paano po na-audit ng DILG?

**[06:04:52.981] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007000)  
At nabiverify yung authenticity ng ECLIP beneficiaries?

**[06:04:58.010] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007001)  
Yes.

**[06:04:59.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007002)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. I will reply to that. The DILG is only one of the many agencies under the Ending Local Communist Armed Conflict Task Force, and the responsibility of the DILG is to facilitate the consultation at the barangay level between and among the people and barangay officials so that they can identify priority programs funded by the Barangay Development Program.

**[06:05:28.680] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007003)  
For 2021, the delivery rate is 99.86%. For 2022, it's 100%. For 2023, it's 98.48%. For 2024, dun sa original na allocation, 2.5 million, it's 90%. And then there's an additional 5 million which was given towards the third quarter or last quarter of the year. The delivery rate is 71%.

**[06:05:55.561] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007004)  
For 2025, our performance is at the level of 29%. For 2026, wala pong completed na project. As much as the fund was released only the second quarter and the third quarter of this year, Mr. Chair.

**[06:06:15.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007005)  
Can these documents be submitted to the representation, Mr. Chair?

**[06:06:20.320] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007006)  
Gladly, Mr. Chair. We will provide a copy to the committee.

**[06:06:23.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007007)  
So yung tanong ko rin po sa...

**[06:06:25.301] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007008)  
...na isang bahagi. Paano po nagiging bahagi yung DILG? Ano pong ginagawa ng DILG to audit and verify yung authenticity? So isang bahagi yung distribution, di ba po? Pero pagkatapos na na-distribute, paano po na-insure na nagigamit po yun sa purposes stated?

**[06:06:44.520] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007009)  
Yung support to Barangay Development Program is lumped under the Local Government Support Fund. And for this purpose, the DILG was given the responsibility to do the monitoring and evaluation.

**[06:06:55.580] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007010)  
And as mentioned by the Secretary a while ago, we hired, at this time, 700 engineers and program evaluation officers to monitor not only the SBDP, this fund, but also the other financial assistance to local government units, the growth equity fund, which are under the LGSF, Mr. Chair.

**[06:07:15.700] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007011)  
So we have people on the ground to make sure that these projects are implemented accordingly, and we require our local government units to upload...

**[06:07:25.741] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007012)  
...the progress status of the projects in the Subay Bayan portal regularly, Mr. Chair.

**[06:07:33.861] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007013)  
Ang re-report po natin, Mr. Chair, ay meron po tayong advocates that wish to access yung mga relevant documents nga po. Yan, for example, ASARO on the website. Pero unfortunately, ay hindi po siya available. Parang last year, kayang ma-access yung ilang documents, pero this year ay hindi na po.

**[06:07:51.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007014)  
So ayaw natin magkaroon ng sitwasyon.

**[06:07:55.861] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007015)  
Kung saan hindi po kaya ma-scrutinize ng mamamayan, maging bahagi ng paano ba na-ensure na yung pondo ng gobyerno galing sa pera ng mamamayan ay hindi po na-account kung saan na pupunta.

**[06:08:10.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007016)  
But you mentioned po, may engineers, etc. So may audit processes po ang DILG dito. So maaari po bang makapagsubmit ang DILG ng full financial and physical performance audit ng funds under BDP?

**[06:08:25.641] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-007017)

**[06:08:29.581] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007018)

**[06:08:44.721] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007019)

**[06:09:25.800] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007025)

**[06:09:35.220] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007026)

**[06:10:54.820] DILG Undersecretary for Plans and Programs Jon Paulo V. Salvahan** (asr-007047)

**[06:27:49.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007275)  
Kung baga, kung kikilos

**[06:27:52.040] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007276)  
po tayo sa araw ng baha,

**[06:27:53.500] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007277)  
huli na yun. So, we need to

**[06:27:55.580] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007278)  
prepare. And as we all know,

**[06:27:57.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007279)  
our PNP is also

**[06:27:59.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007280)  
talagang first

**[06:28:01.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007281)  
responders yan. At saka po

**[06:28:03.620] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007282)  
ang ating pong barangay at ang

**[06:28:05.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007283)  
ating pong mga mayor. So, how is

**[06:28:07.560] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007284)  
the DILG preparing

**[06:28:09.640] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007285)  
po for disasters?

**[06:28:11.760] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007286)  
Kasi alam ko, one of the largest

**[06:28:13.320] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007287)  
concerns din po ninyo ang disaster

**[06:28:15.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007288)  
management or disaster

**[06:28:17.720] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007289)  
preparedness. Hindi na po tayo

**[06:28:19.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007290)  
dapat i-manage eh, dapat i-prepare.

**[06:28:21.960] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007291)  
Mr. Chair,

**[06:28:22.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007292)  
Your Honor,

**[06:28:25.040] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007293)  
the DILG is the Vice Chair for Preparedness

**[06:28:27.740] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007294)  
under the National Disaster

**[06:28:29.780] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007295)  
Reduction Management Council.

**[06:28:31.180] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007296)  
And on this note, we are working with other

**[06:28:33.220] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007297)  
national government agencies, particularly

**[06:28:35.080] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007298)  
the science agencies, to provide

**[06:28:37.360] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007299)  
timely information and updates

**[06:28:39.760] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007300)  
to our local governments, particularly on

**[06:28:41.580] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007301)  
the high-risk areas that they have in the

**[06:28:43.660] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007302)  
LGU. Those areas

**[06:28:45.700] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007303)  
which are flood-prone,

**[06:28:47.080] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007304)  
susceptible or vulnerable to landfills,

**[06:28:49.520] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007305)  
landslides, so that our barangays

**[06:28:51.340] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007306)  
particularly can already identify

**[06:28:53.660] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007307)  
beforehand or prepare beforehand

**[06:28:55.140] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007308)  
their constituents. So,

**[06:28:57.360] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007309)  
in furtherance of the mandate

**[06:28:59.600] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007310)  
of the DILG, we came up with our operation

**[06:29:01.500] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007311)  
list of protocols. And this operation list

**[06:29:03.640] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007312)  
of protocols have been implemented

**[06:29:05.320] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007313)  
since 2016, 2017.

**[06:29:06.820] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007314)  
And we have trained local government

**[06:29:09.440] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007315)  
units on how to properly

**[06:29:11.620] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007316)  
respond to and mitigate the effects

**[06:29:13.620] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007317)  
of disasters.

**[06:29:15.040] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007318)  
We have operation list of protocol for

**[06:29:17.400] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007319)  
typhoons.

**[06:29:20.180] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007320)  
Operation list of protocol for volcanic

**[06:29:21.240] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007321)  
eruptions, for tsunamis, landslides,

**[06:29:24.040] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007322)  
and even El Nino.

**[06:29:26.340] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007323)  
So, that's one of the strategy

**[06:29:28.600] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007324)  
that we are doing in the DILG.

**[06:29:30.420] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007325)  
But more than that, we are

**[06:29:32.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007326)  
in constant contact, for example, with

**[06:29:34.620] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007327)  
Pag-Asa in order to provide our local governments

**[06:29:36.740] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007328)  
with the latest update, particularly

**[06:29:38.760] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007329)  
when

**[06:29:39.240] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007330)  
there are typhoons and local weather disturbance in their area.

**[06:29:43.120] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007331)  
Even during the disaster, nandun yung DILG officer sa munisipyo?

**[06:29:48.780] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007332)  
Yes, Mr. Chair, because we are also actually monitoring the preparedness measures of the LGUs

**[06:29:54.840] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007333)  
every time there's, for example, typhoons coming in our area.

**[06:30:00.100] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007334)  
Mabuti po. Thank you so much for that information.

**[06:30:02.540] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007335)  
And mabuti po nabanggit niyo yung pag-asa.

**[06:30:06.420] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007336)  
We all know that mayors are the ones who declare po na walang pasok.

**[06:30:11.000] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007337)  
So, napakagulo po ng sitwasyon.

**[06:30:14.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007338)  
Is there some way that we could put a deadline or something?

**[06:30:20.540] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007339)  
Parang guidelines po ng DILG.

**[06:30:22.600] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007340)  
Kasi po, alas-tris na po ng hapon, biglang wala na po.

**[06:30:25.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007341)  
Tapos kung ano-ano pa.

**[06:30:27.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007342)  
Sari-sariling LGU po ang nagde-declare.

**[06:30:31.040] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007343)  
So, kuminsan ho, magkakatabi, iba-iba po yung hatol.

**[06:30:34.800] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007344)  
Is there some way that the DILG can coordinate with this?

**[06:30:38.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007345)  
Because this is very confusing to parents, to businesses, at sa lahat-lahat na.

**[06:30:43.500] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007346)  
Tapos nakikita niyo po ito sa Facebook.

**[06:30:45.860] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007347)  
Ang aangas po ng mga bata, demanding na wala na pong school sa kanila pong chief executives.

**[06:30:53.820] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007348)  
Is there some way that DILG can work on this?

**[06:30:56.600] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007349)  
Or talagang sari-sariling lang ba not?

**[06:30:59.000] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007350)  
Mr. Chair, there is now an ongoing effort to revisit.

**[06:31:04.700] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007351)  
Executive order, particularly on the declaration of suspension of classes.

**[06:31:10.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007352)  
But the policy that is now being used is still the same.

**[06:31:16.260] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007353)  
We leave it to the local chief executives to declare localized suspension of classes

**[06:31:21.360] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007354)  
if it's really necessary, taking into consideration the forecast given by the PAGASA.

**[06:31:29.180] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007355)  
Late po ba talagang mag-forecast ng PAGASA para sa ating mga mayors?

**[06:31:34.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007356)  
And diba forecasting is a science?

**[06:31:38.500] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007357)  
Hindi naman ho hinuhulaan yan.

**[06:31:40.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007358)  
Well, there are, if I may, there are other apps na not only PAGASA na pwede.

**[06:31:47.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007359)  
May Windy, may iba-iba.

**[06:31:49.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007360)  
So minsan yun na po yung ginagamit rin ng mga ibang LGUs.

**[06:31:53.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007361)  
So yeah, yung ginagamit po natin.

**[06:31:56.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007362)  
But syempre, officially PAGASA pa rin.

**[06:31:59.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007363)  
Hindi ho pa pwedeng we work out something na may deadline naman po?

**[06:32:06.380] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007364)  
Hindi naman siguro dapat alauna ng hapon.

**[06:32:10.020] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007365)  
Ideally, the basis for the suspension of classes should be 11pm forecast of PAGASA

**[06:32:15.640] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007366)  
so that the following day, parents and students will no longer have to go to school

**[06:32:21.980] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007367)  
before the suspension is declared.

**[06:32:25.680] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007368)  
I do believe that PAGASA is also doing its best to provide most accurate information

**[06:32:30.660] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007369)  
to all local governments and to all our citizens.

**[06:32:33.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007370)  
Yes, thank you.

**[06:32:38.580] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007371)  
Sa mga climate change threatened and forever flooded municipalities,

**[06:32:44.140] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007372)  
first of all, I would like to commend the PNP sa kanila pong tulong sa amin sa 4th District of Pampanga.

**[06:32:50.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007373)  
Napakasipag po nila and they were with us last year and this year.

**[06:32:55.580] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007374)  
And sila po nagbibigay ng libring sakay so gusto ko po silang pasalamatan.

**[06:33:00.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007375)  
Now, the second question sa amin, I would also like to thank the BFP.

**[06:33:06.620] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007376)  
Meron din po silang Rinescu.

**[06:33:11.120] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007377)  
So, my question is, pwede po ba natin silang i-capacitate at i-train?

**[06:33:19.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007378)  
At saka nagahanap po talaga sila ng mga gamit for water rescue.

**[06:33:23.380] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007379)  
Can we identify po specific flooded areas like the entire Pampanga or maybe the 4th District of Pampanga?

**[06:33:31.320] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007380)  
Kasi wala man po kami mga boats eh.

**[06:33:34.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007381)  
Konting-konti lang po talaga.

**[06:33:36.380] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007382)  
And I think that we should...

**[06:33:38.300] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007383)  
Identify areas that need to be rescued po.

**[06:33:41.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007384)  
Pwede po ba yun?

**[06:33:43.340] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007385)  
And can you share with us what you are doing po for water rescue, lalo na po sa flooded areas?

**[06:33:50.220] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007386)  
Ma'am, good afternoon po.

**[06:33:52.820] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007387)  
Yung Bureau for Protection plays a very vital role pagdating po sa HIDR, especially on flooding.

**[06:33:58.880] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007388)  
Yung nakikita niyo po na nakatancherate are all BFP.

**[06:34:02.640] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007389)  
Ang ginagawa po ng BFP ngayon, ma'am, continuous po yung capacity building po ng mga local disaster restaurants.

**[06:34:08.260] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007390)  
At yung management office ng mga LGUs nila.

**[06:34:12.680] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007391)  
Isa po yan, ma'am, sa nakasama po sa listahan na mga bibiliin po ng BFP.

**[06:34:17.760] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007392)  
Pagdating po sa HIDR, kasama na po yung mga watercraft natin na magagamit automatically.

**[06:34:22.980] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007393)  
That includes also the life vest.

**[06:34:24.940] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007394)  
So hopefully po, madagdagan lang po yung pondo ng BFP para po mabiliin natin yung ating kailangan po.

**[06:34:31.340] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007395)  
Rest assured, susuportahan ko po yan.

**[06:34:33.800] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007396)  
And is it possible po that we would give the boats?

**[06:34:38.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007397)  
Mga gamit po sa mga places na talagang alam po natin na binabaha?

**[06:34:43.280] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007398)  
Ma'am, meron na po kaming identified mga localities na constantly po, maliit lang na ulan, binabaha kagad.

**[06:34:50.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007399)  
Like yung mga nandun po sa Project NOAA, for example?

**[06:34:53.720] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007400)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[06:34:54.880] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007401)  
Pagka po, nadagdagan lang po yung pondo at mabibili po yung mga gamit namin,

**[06:34:58.900] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007402)  
that will be distributed geographically kung ano po yung needs ng mga local fire stations natin.

**[06:35:05.540] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007403)  
Kasi po sa amin, umaagos talaga yung tubig.

**[06:35:08.000] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007404)  
And somebody drowned actually in front of me.

**[06:35:10.920] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007405)  
At talagang kulang po yung gamit nila.

**[06:35:13.260] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007406)  
So, if you could support us po, we would be very grateful.

**[06:35:17.340] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007407)  
Rest assured, ma'am, that we will do our best to help in the capacity building po ng mga local disaster reduction management office po natin,

**[06:35:23.820] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007408)  
yung mga parang gay disaster po.

**[06:35:25.300] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007409)  
Constant naman po yung mga trainings namin sa kanila.

**[06:35:27.540] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007410)  
And hopefully in the future, ma-acquire po namin yung mga necessary equipments

**[06:35:32.520] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007411)  
and that will be placed doon po sa mga localities na talaga po tinatamaan po, baha or whatever.

**[06:35:39.200] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007412)  
Kahit o hydromet hazards na meron po sila.

**[06:35:40.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007413)  
Sige po. And we look forward to that.

**[06:35:43.360] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007414)  
Sana po our PNPG would look into that also.

**[06:35:46.000] BFP Officer-in-Charge Fire Chief Superintendent Wilberto Rico Neil A. Kwan Tiu** (asr-007415)  
Thank you.

**[06:35:46.360] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007416)  
So, after climate change po, my next question is regarding po yung

**[06:35:53.080] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007417)  
alam na po natin na talagang laganap na po ang mga threats sa bawat school po.

**[06:35:59.720] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007418)  
At araw-araw daw yung mga PNP nandun na po sa bawat school checking up on threats.

**[06:36:06.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007419)  
So, my question is,

**[06:36:08.220] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007420)  
Paano po, parang nagkaroon po ng access sa mga barel ang mga bata?

**[06:36:14.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007421)  
And I believe these are registered firearms given to licensed personnel.

**[06:36:21.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007422)  
Like yung isa po, yung tita niya was a nurse, a lawyer, and a policeman, a policewoman.

**[06:36:28.080] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007423)  
And the other one po, yung daddy niya po, customs.

**[06:36:30.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007424)  
How are you training po?

**[06:36:32.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007425)  
Parang hindi po unregistered eh. Registered pa po yung mga firearms.

**[06:36:36.640] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007426)  
Tama po ba ako doon?

**[06:36:38.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007427)  
Thank you for the last question.

**[06:36:46.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007428)  
Kindly answer.

**[06:36:47.520] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007429)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[06:36:50.640] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007430)  
Those recovered firearms that were used in June 16 as well as in Zamboanga are all registered firearms except for the other one.

**[06:37:05.280] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007431)  
So, the Philippine National Police, of course, in coordination with other law enforcement units,

**[06:37:12.540] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007432)  
and as well,

**[06:37:13.900] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007433)  
as a unit responsible for regulating these firearms.

**[06:37:18.180] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007434)  
Ginagawa po ng paraan with regards to several policies.

**[06:37:21.120] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007435)  
As to when we say these are within the reach of children, of course, or minor,

**[06:37:30.780] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007436)  
of course, nandun na yung indication.

**[06:37:32.780] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007437)  
But that's why we have prepared cases against these personalities

**[06:37:37.840] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007438)  
who are supposed to be the custodian of these firearms.

**[06:37:41.200] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007439)  
And one of...

**[06:37:43.720] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007440)  
The recommendation was, of course, the enactment of law such that maripil yung naging bailable ang ating violation of firearms,

**[06:37:56.380] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007441)  
illegal possession of firearms.

**[06:37:58.240] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007442)  
We are recommending that a higher penalty be imposed for those in possession of unlicensed firearms.

**[06:38:07.500] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007443)  
Especially so that unlicensed firearms are being used in the commission of crime.

**[06:38:13.300] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007444)  
About 98% of the cases involving firearms are unlicensed firearms.

**[06:38:22.680] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007445)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[06:38:28.300] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007446)

**[06:38:35.780] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007447)

**[06:38:42.360] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007448)

**[06:38:44.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007449)

**[06:38:47.160] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007450)  
Mr. Chair and Your Honor.

**[06:38:49.500] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007451)  
Ang alam naman natin ng peace and order ay shared responsibility of the community and the...

**[06:38:56.920] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007452)

**[06:38:58.640] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007453)

**[06:39:01.280] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007454)

**[06:39:04.160] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007455)

**[06:39:10.080] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007456)  
Pero again, that is not realistic.

**[06:39:12.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007457)  
Pero kung tatangin niyo po, gusto ko yun.

**[06:39:14.680] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007458)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[06:39:15.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007459)  
Yes.

**[06:39:15.700] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007460)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[06:39:17.280] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007461)  
Actually, there are...

**[06:39:18.180] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007462)  
42,000 barangays nationwide.

**[06:39:21.840] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007463)  
Kung pibilangin na lang natin ang number of schools, there are, of course, more than 42,000.

**[06:39:31.560] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007464)  
42,000 schools.

**[06:39:32.660] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007465)  
So, let's say it's about 56,000 schools nationwide, including those private schools.

**[06:39:39.420] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007466)  
So, it is a must that we multiply each other, especially in negating the occurrence of crime

**[06:39:47.700] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007467)

**[06:39:49.620] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007468)

**[06:39:55.640] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007469)

**[06:40:02.800] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007470)  
presence of this police in the community, especially in the school.

**[06:40:08.180] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007471)  
And if we have 230,000 and then say another times 3, 42,000 times 7 of barangay,

**[06:40:19.100] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007472)

**[06:40:24.100] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007473)

**[06:40:30.160] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007474)  
Yes.

**[06:40:30.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007475)  
Teamwork na lang.

**[06:40:33.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007476)  
Sila muna yung una, tapos pag kayo na po yung re-rescue sa kanila.

**[06:40:39.960] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007477)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[06:40:41.260] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007478)  
through,

**[06:40:41.360] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007479)  
We have five statistics of...

**[06:40:45.200] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007480)  
We are sitting together, engaging each other, and...

**[06:40:49.200] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007481)

**[06:40:55.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007482)  
May mga data po ba kayo regarding crime, kung ina niyong pinatay, kanila, or...

**[06:41:01.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007483)  
Actions going down.

**[06:41:03.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007484)  
Napakagaling po ng polis natin.

**[06:41:05.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007485)  
Under the leadership of General Atates.

**[06:41:09.040] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007486)  
Meron po ba kayong crime statistics?

**[06:41:11.280] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007487)  
Like, pwede naman po...

**[06:41:12.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007488)  
Yes. Can you submit the...

Yes, I'm actually curious lang po.

**[06:41:17.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007490)  
To this committee?

**[06:41:17.860] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007491)  
Sir, yes.

**[06:41:19.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007492)  
I'm hoping pababa po.

**[06:41:22.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007493)  
Actually, it is.

**[06:41:23.660] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007494)  
Sir Chair, we'll be submitting the data.

**[06:41:27.200] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007495)  
But as far as I can remember,

**[06:41:30.880] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007496)  
when we implemented and or launched our Safer Cities,

**[06:41:36.580] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007497)

**[06:41:43.460] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007498)

**[06:41:48.280] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007499)  
If we're...

**[06:41:49.000] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007500)  
To compare a period of one year,

**[06:41:56.280] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007501)

**[06:42:02.700] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007502)  
ay about 25% po ang reduction.

**[06:42:06.140] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007503)  
So napakababa po.

**[06:42:07.660] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007504)  
That's why, per tourist index,

**[06:42:11.660] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007505)

**[06:42:20.040] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007506)  
We're ranked.

**[06:42:20.780] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007507)  
By about 60%.

**[06:42:23.300] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007508)  
65%.

**[06:42:24.800] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007509)  
As to the crime index,

**[06:42:27.440] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007510)  
crime index, bumaba po.

**[06:42:30.360] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007511)  
Bumaba po.

**[06:42:32.140] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007512)  
Sa datang crime index,

**[06:42:32.800] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007513)  
nandito tayo sa ranking.

**[06:42:34.500] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007514)  
Pang-rank,

**[06:42:36.360] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007515)  
45%.

**[06:42:40.220] PNP Chief Police General Jose Melencio C. Nartatez Jr.** (asr-007516)  
Your Honor, Mr. Chair.

**[06:42:41.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007517)  
Thank you.

**[06:42:42.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007518)  
So, thank you, ma'am.

**[06:42:43.620] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007519)  
So, I'm sure po pababa yun.

**[06:42:45.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007520)  
And I would just like to see a listing po.

**[06:42:48.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007521)  
Baka ho, Central Luzon,

**[06:42:50.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007522)  
and Metro Manila.

**[06:42:52.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007523)  
Opo.

**[06:42:52.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007524)  
So, that concludes po yung mga tanong ko.

**[06:42:56.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007525)  
I would again like to thank our PNP po sa tulong po sa akin

**[06:43:01.620] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007526)  
netong tumating pong habagat.

**[06:43:04.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007527)  
And sana po, wag po kayong bibitaw sa amin

**[06:43:06.920] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007528)

**[06:43:10.520] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007529)  
And rest assured po of the full support for Secretary Remolia

**[06:43:14.420] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007530)  
and our Chief PNP po sa inyo pong budget.

**[06:43:18.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007531)  
And to the DILG family, maraming salamat.

**[06:43:21.140] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007532)  
po.

**[06:43:21.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007533)  
Ah

**[06:43:21.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007534)  
Thank you, Congressman Anna York Bondoc

**[06:43:26.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007535)  
Ang last na po mag-interpellate, pero bago po yan,

**[06:43:29.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007536)  
I would like to recognize the presence of our SDS,

**[06:43:34.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007537)  
Dinan Hernandez.

**[06:43:35.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007538)  
Ang pinakahuli na po magtatanong sa inyo,

**[06:43:38.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007539)  
hinihintay po po natin si Chairman Micah Sonsing.

**[06:43:41.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007540)  
Pero habang wala siya, may clarificatory question lang po ako.

**[06:43:46.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007541)  
Pwede pong magtanong sa DILG and even sa police.

**[06:43:50.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007542)  
So, we, ayan.

**[06:43:52.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007543)  
Yes, boss.

**[06:43:53.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007544)  
May incident lang po sa amin pong district

**[06:43:58.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007545)  
na may nagsulat na po ang mga winning contractors sa amin

**[06:44:05.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007546)  
regarding yung mga hulihan na mga bako, mga equipment nila.

**[06:44:10.250] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007547)  
So, hindi na po nila alam ang difference between excavation and quarrying.

**[06:44:17.430] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007548)  
So, we have the letter already.

**[06:44:19.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007549)  
It was received na by Secretary Vince Dizon.

**[06:44:23.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007550)  
And for sure, tatanungin po ang DNR and then tatanungin po rin ng DILG,

**[06:44:30.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007551)  
then ang ating kapulisan.

**[06:44:33.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007552)  
Dahil ang involved po dito, ang tinatawag po sa amin na Sagip Kalikasan Task Force.

**[06:44:37.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007553)  
So,

**[06:44:38.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007554)  
So, even po yung mga re-blocking na mga daan,

**[06:44:41.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007555)  
siyempre po gagamit ka ng bakho,

**[06:44:44.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007556)  
ang tawag po nila doon, quarrying na.

**[06:44:48.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007557)  
And ang tinatanong po namin, are they allowed?

**[06:44:52.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007558)  
Is the Sagip Kalikasan Task Force na mga tao ng provincial government,

**[06:45:00.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007559)  
are they allowed to impound heavy equipment

**[06:45:04.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007560)  
na ginagamit po sa development, sa paggawaan ng mga daan

**[06:45:08.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007561)  
sa buong-buong Pilipinas, huwag lang po sa amin sa Camarines World.

**[06:45:14.030] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007562)  
I would like to seek the first DILG, the opinion.

**[06:45:22.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007563)  
Yes.

**[06:45:23.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007564)  
Meron pong contractor doon, anim na po na backhoe ang nakuha

**[06:45:30.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007565)  
dahil kumagawa po ng flood control.

**[06:45:36.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007566)  
Yes.

**[06:45:39.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007567)  
And I will submit also a copy.

**[06:45:42.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007568)  
Unahan ko na.

**[06:45:42.700] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007569)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[06:45:47.220] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007570)  
As far as confiscation of equipment belonging to the,

**[06:45:50.760] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007571)  
belonging to some people engaged in quarrying or excavation,

**[06:45:58.050] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007572)  
now the possibility of...

**[06:46:00.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007573)  
First, first, they're not engaged.

**[06:46:03.210] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007574)  
What is the difference of excavation and quarrying?

**[06:46:07.910] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007575)  
For example, sir, para mas malinaw,

**[06:46:10.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007576)  
gumagawa po ako online kanal.

**[06:46:14.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007577)  
Is that quarrying or excavation?

**[06:46:17.480] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007578)  
That is excavation, sir.

**[06:46:18.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007579)  
Exactly.

**[06:46:19.040] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007580)  
Now, when you quarry...

**[06:46:20.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007581)  
Kumagawa po tayo ng re-blocking.

**[06:46:24.730] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007582)  
Is that quarrying or excavation?

**[06:46:26.800] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007583)  
I think that is also excavation, Your Honor.

**[06:46:28.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007584)  
Excavation. Okay.

**[06:46:29.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007585)  
Clearing po, dahil nag-landslide.

**[06:46:32.650] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007586)  
May landslide sa bundok, nag-clearing po sa daan.

**[06:46:35.970] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007587)  
Is that quarrying or clearing?

**[06:46:39.080] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007588)  
Same, same answer, Your Honor.

**[06:46:39.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007589)  
Okay.

**[06:46:40.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007590)  
Ang tanong ko po ba, pwede po bang i-impound?

**[06:46:45.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007591)  
Nang mga tao ng probinsya,

**[06:46:47.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007592)  
ang mga equipment na ito po yung ginagawa.

**[06:46:52.600] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007593)  
Well, by and large, as long as the act is not illegal,

**[06:46:56.520] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007594)  
it is not a travesty of any existing law.

**[06:46:59.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007595)  
And plus, we have to remember,

**[06:47:02.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007596)  
any, nag-mayor po ako,

**[06:47:04.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007597)  
any local ordinance, municipal,

**[06:47:07.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007598)  
or even po, provincial,

**[06:47:09.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007599)  
we cannot supersede any national law.

**[06:47:12.560] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007600)  
That's correct, Your Honor.

**[06:47:13.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007601)  
That's why I'm asking,

**[06:47:14.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007602)  
are they allowed to impound?

**[06:47:16.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007603)  
Yes or no?

**[06:47:17.960] DILG Undersecretary for External, Legal and Legislative Affairs Romeo P. Benitez** (asr-007604)  
I don't think that authority is vested on the part of the LGU for that matter.

**[06:47:24.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007605)  
Yes, kindly study it.

**[07:20:14.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007849)  
I would like to recognize the chairman for the APRO Committee, Mika Suansing.

**[07:20:22.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007850)  
Thank you very much, Vice Chair Arnie Fuentebella.

**[07:20:25.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007851)  
Good afternoon to everyone.

**[07:20:28.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007852)  
As I was told by Pong Arnie, tapos na po, wala na pong ibang mag-i-interpolate.

**[07:20:33.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007853)  
And hindi po ako mag-i-interpolate, I promise.

**[07:20:35.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007854)  
I support the budget of DILG.

**[07:20:38.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007855)  
Alam nyo naman po yan, we're always very supportive.

**[07:20:41.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007856)  
Last year, we took care of the subsistence allowance.

**[07:20:45.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007857)  
We funded it totally, completely under the 2026 General Appropriations Act.

**[07:20:52.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007858)  
And for us naman po, talagang long overdue and well-deserved po talaga yun

**[07:20:56.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007859)  
for our military uniformed personnel.

**[07:21:00.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007860)  
So hopefully po, napapakidabangan na po at nararamdaman na po

**[07:21:04.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007861)  
ng ating mga kasamahan yung karagdagan po na subsistence allowance

**[07:21:11.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007862)  
for 2026

**[07:21:12.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007863)  
So moving forward po, coming into 2027, we want to, the Committee on Appropriations

**[07:21:20.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007864)  
and myself, we'd like to once again express our support for the budget of the DILG.

**[07:21:27.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007865)  
You're doing such important work for our country, ensuring peace and order

**[07:21:32.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007866)  
all throughout the country.

**[07:21:35.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007867)  
And making sure the LGUs din po are working closely

**[07:21:41.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007868)  
with the national government in terms of all of the priorities

**[07:21:45.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007869)  
in serving our countrymen.

**[07:21:46.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007870)  
So hindi pa akong mag-interpolate.

**[07:21:49.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007871)  
I'm just here to express my support.

**[07:21:50.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007872)  
And actually, baliktad po, I'm here to ask actually what else we can do

**[07:21:56.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007873)  
for the DILG moving forward as we go through the deliberations.

**[07:22:01.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007874)  
So siguro po, nabanggit na po ni Cong. Arnie.

**[07:22:07.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007875)  
He's had discussions.

**[07:22:08.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007876)  
As of course, the...

**[07:22:10.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007877)
