**[07:22:15.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007878)  
with the different, with DILG and all the attached agencies

**[07:22:20.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007879)  
with regard to the additional requirements or proposals

**[07:22:24.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007880)  
or additional budget for consideration that you would like to put forth.

**[07:22:31.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007881)  
So siguro po, sinabi na po yan Nikong Arnie.

**[07:22:37.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007882)  
I don't know if you'd like to say it to me.

**[07:22:41.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007883)  
Ano po yung laman ng wish list ninyo?

**[07:22:43.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007884)  
Or would you just prefer to write to the committee?

**[07:22:46.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007885)  
Sulat na lang po.

**[07:22:46.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007886)  
Okay.

**[07:22:46.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007887)  
In terms of logistics po, I'll just request if we could have the request

**[07:22:53.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007888)  
for additional consideration by Thursday po ngayon.

**[07:23:01.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007889)  
By the first half of next week, if possible.

**[07:23:03.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007890)  
Because we...

**[07:23:03.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007891)  
One of the major reforms that we've done in the Committee on Appropriations is

**[07:23:07.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007892)  
we are now

**[07:23:11.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007893)  
institutionalizing

**[07:23:11.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007894)  
The Budget Amendments Reviews of Committee,

**[07:23:12.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007895)  
which openly deliberates on all the requests for institutional amendments

**[07:23:17.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007896)  
emanating from the agencies.

**[07:23:19.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007897)  
So yun po sana yung request namin.

**[07:23:21.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007898)  
As we are targeting to convene the BARC in about a week or so

**[07:23:26.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007899)  
before the plenary.

**[07:23:27.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007900)  
Para po mahimay na namin yung mga budget requests po.

**[07:23:32.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007901)  
At saka maisama po namin for consideration of BARC.

**[07:23:36.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007902)  
So hopefully po, if it's okay po, by the first half of next week,

**[07:23:40.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007903)  
if we could have those.

**[07:23:42.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007904)  
And siguro, Kong Arnie, if I may,

**[07:23:45.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007905)  
this is actually one of the engagement models that we have been doing since last year,

**[07:23:54.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007906)  
which is to give the civil society organizations the opportunity to pose questions to the agencies

**[07:24:01.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007907)  
through the Committee on Appropriations.

**[07:24:04.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007908)  
Just to clarify din po, siguro, we've seen some feedback.

**[07:24:10.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007909)  
With regard to this, the engagement model this year.

**[07:24:13.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007910)  
Siguro po, ang kaklaruhin ko lang po,

**[07:24:16.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007911)  
for the record, this is the first time that the Committee on Appropriations

**[07:24:20.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007912)  
received questions emanating from the CSOs.

**[07:24:24.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007913)  
Ngayong araw pa lang po, yung una na natanggap namin.

**[07:24:27.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007914)  
Because siguro po, just to clarify,

**[07:24:30.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007915)  
meron po kasing perception that it's the Committee on Appropriations

**[07:24:35.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007916)  
who is handling all of the direct coordination

**[07:24:40.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007917)  
with the civil society organizations.

**[07:24:42.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007918)  
But in fact po, it's actually the CPBRD within Congress

**[07:24:46.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007919)  
who directly coordinates with the civil society organizations.

**[07:24:51.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007920)  
So ang nangyayari po, umaasa po kami sa CPBRD every morning

**[07:24:57.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007921)  
to give us a list of the civil society organizations present during that day,

**[07:25:02.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007922)  
as well as the list of questions emanating from the civil society organizations.

**[07:25:08.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007923)  
So

**[07:25:09.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007924)  
Gusto ko lang po siguro kaklaruhin for all our partner CSOs who are watching

**[07:25:15.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007925)  
and also everyone who would like to engage with us.

**[07:25:22.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007926)  
Nito pong mga nakarang araw, wala po kaming natatanggap na mga tanong

**[07:25:26.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007927)  
galing po sa mga civil society organizations.

**[07:25:29.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007928)  
So that's something that we have to coordinate with CPBRD with moving forward.

**[07:25:35.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007929)  
Sana po, araw-araw, mabigyan na po nila kami

**[07:25:38.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007930)  
ng listahan

**[07:25:39.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007931)  
ng mga civil society organizations at yung mga katanungan po para sa araw na yun.

**[07:25:44.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007932)  
Because on the part of the Comitee on Appropriations, we are really sincere

**[07:25:48.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007933)  
when we were really adamant in upholding the engagement models

**[07:25:54.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007934)  
with the civil society organizations.

**[07:25:55.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007935)  
So we would like to apologize to the civil society organizations

**[07:26:00.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007936)  
who felt that they have been left out of the deliberations in the previous days

**[07:26:07.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007937)  
when in fact po,

**[07:26:09.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007938)  
talaga honest to goodness, utmost sincerity, we have not been receiving any of the questions

**[07:26:14.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007939)  
from the CSOs.

**[07:26:16.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007940)  
But now that we've received this today, hopefully po in the next days, kukulitin po namin yung

**[07:26:24.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007941)  
CPBRD to give us the questions because we really want to be your voice, we want to be

**[07:26:31.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007942)  
your avenue in asking questions to our agencies.

**[07:26:34.900] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007943)  
So, buwena mano po itong DILG.

**[07:26:39.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007944)  
In terms of the questions.

**[07:26:41.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007945)  
So, if I may po, on behalf of the civil society organizations, I would like to pose a few questions

**[07:26:47.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007946)  
to the DILG family.

**[07:26:51.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007947)  
These questions po, emanate from Code NGO, and these are the questions po for DILG.

**[07:27:01.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007948)  
The first is the rationale for the Local Government Support Fund.

**[07:27:04.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007949)  
I believe this was actually discussed at length by Secretary Jonvic earlier today.

**[07:27:11.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007950)  
The Mandanas-Garcia ruling broadened the tax base used to compute the just share of local government units in national taxes,

**[07:27:20.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007951)  
now released through the National Tax Allotment.

**[07:27:24.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007952)  
Considering the resulting increase in the NTA of LGUs,

**[07:27:27.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007953)  
What is the policy justification for retaining a separate local government support fund

**[07:27:32.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007954)  
whose allocations are still determined and directly released by the national government

**[07:27:37.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007955)  
to beneficiary LGUs?

**[07:27:39.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007956)  
Follow-up question po, what specific programs, needs, or fiscal gaps funded through the LGSF

**[07:27:45.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007957)  
cannot be adequately supported through the increased NTA and other regular revenues of LGUs?

**[07:27:52.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007958)  
How does the government ensure that the LGSF supplements rather than duplicates or substitutes

**[07:27:58.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007959)  
for resources already available to LGUs?

**[07:28:01.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007960)  
So yun po yung katanungan. If we may pose that to DILG.

**[07:28:06.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-007961)  
Usec. Mario?

**[07:28:06.580] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007962)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Your Honor.

**[07:28:10.080] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007963)  
For 2027, the estimated national tax allocation share of local government is like 1.32 to 1.4 trillion pesos.

**[07:28:19.400] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007964)  
But this amount is divided between and among the 82 provinces, 149 cities, 1,493 municipalities,

**[07:28:28.380] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007965)  
and more than 42,000 barricades.

**[07:28:30.840] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007966)  
So on the surface, it looks like it's a very, very big amount.

**[07:28:34.500] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007967)  
But when divided between those local governments that I mentioned,

**[07:28:37.680] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007968)  
a while ago, it will not really be enough to fund the priority projects of the local governments,

**[07:28:45.140] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007969)  
considering that there are devolved functions that are given to LGUs.

**[07:28:50.440] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007970)  
So the local government support fund, which will be like 58 billion for 2027,

**[07:28:56.300] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007971)  
will be an additional resource, additional fund, which the local governments can use

**[07:29:03.360] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007972)  
in responding to the other felt needs.

**[07:29:07.700] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007973)  
of their constituents.

**[07:29:09.460] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007974)  
These funds are earmarked for projects like water system, water sanitation system,

**[07:29:20.960] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007975)  
farm-to-market roads, electrification, health centers,

**[07:29:29.640] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007976)  
and for other basic services of the people in the different localities.

**[07:29:36.440] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007977)  
So I believe, Mr. Chair, Your Honor, that's the rationale why there's the local government support fund.

**[07:29:42.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007978)  
Just to clarify po, I believe this was something that Sec. Mulya raised earlier today, that for the record, DILG, what is the role that DILG plays in LGSF? Para lang po malinaw.

**[07:29:58.320] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007979)  
Yes, Your Honor, Mr. Chair. The local government support fund is directly released to the local governments.

**[07:30:03.720] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007980)  
The DILG's task is to, per the General Appropriations Act, is to do the monitoring and evaluation in the implementation of these projects.

**[07:30:12.540] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007981)  
So for that purpose, as I mentioned a while ago, we have a fund allocated for the hiring of engineers and project evaluation officers, which we deploy to our provinces and regions to help us monitor, to ensure the faithful implementation of all those projects funded from the LGSF.

**[07:30:30.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007982)  
So in terms of validation po, vetting?

**[07:30:37.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007983)  
Yes, Your Honor, Mr. Chair. There's a portal created by DBM where LGUs can submit their project proposals.

**[07:30:45.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007984)  
So just to answer the last question of our CSO partner, hindi po DILG. Kasi po ang tanong, how do we ensure that these are not duplicate projects?

**[07:30:59.180] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007985)  
So the vetting, the validation, all that process is not...

**[07:31:02.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007986)  
It's not handled by DILG, but rather DBM. So we can, perhaps, we can also post that as a question to the DBM.

**[07:31:11.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007987)  
That's correct, Mr. Chair, Your Honor. It's the LGUs themselves which identify the priority projects that they will fund from the LGSF.

**[07:31:11.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007988)  
Yes.

**[07:31:11.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-007989)  
through

**[07:31:19.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007990)  
Thank you. Thank you, po, USEC. So we'll move to the next question. This is with regard to community participation, project validation, and oversight, also from Code NGO.

**[07:31:30.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007991)  
Could the DILG explain the complete process

**[07:31:30.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007992)  
for identifying, endorsing, validating, procuring, and monitoring projects funded under the LGSF, particularly the FALGU, to the Barangay Development Program?

**[07:31:44.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007993)  
At what stages are local development councils, local sanggunians, accredited civil society organizations, sectoral representatives, and affected communities meaningfully involved?

**[07:31:55.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007994)  
What safeguards are... Sorry.

**[07:31:57.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007995)  
Sorry, sorry.

**[07:31:57.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007996)  
What safeguards are in place?

**[07:32:02.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007997)  
To ensure that projects respond to documented community needs, are aligned with local development plans, do not duplicate existing national government or LGU programs, and are protected from conflicts of interest and undue influence in procurement.

**[07:32:19.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007998)  
Last paragraph, po.

**[07:32:44.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-007999)  
The 2027 National Expenditure Program also proposes P563.925 million for the DILG's monitoring and evaluation of LGSF projects, as mentioned by USEC.

**[07:32:44.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008002)  
What measurable outputs, public disclosure requirements, and accountability mechanisms correspond to this allocation?

**[07:32:44.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008003)  
Will CSOs and community representatives be formally involved in independent project monitoring and validation?

**[07:32:48.520] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008004)  
Yes, thank you, Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[07:32:50.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008005)  
As I mentioned a while ago, the identification of priority projects, the procurement for these projects now are less with local governments.

**[07:32:58.400] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008006)  
What we are doing today is to just make sure that the projects are...

**[07:33:02.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008009)  
are not cost projects and that they are properly implemented.

**[07:33:05.580] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008010)  
To do that, we came up with a three-tiered monitoring of all these projects.

**[07:33:12.360] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008011)  
The first-party monitoring is done by the local project monitoring committees.

**[07:33:19.830] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008012)  
This is the mandated committee that should be in the local government unit.

**[07:33:31.180] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008013)  
And the local...

**[07:33:31.520] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008014)  
project monitoring committee is composed of LGU functionaries and civil society organizations.

**[07:33:37.640] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008015)  
The second tier is second-party monitoring.

**[07:33:42.100] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008016)  
That is us.

**[07:33:42.800] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008017)  
And as I mentioned a while ago, and it was also mentioned by the good congressman a while ago,

**[07:33:47.240] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008018)  
by the good chair, that there is an allocation of 563 million for the Mialgo monitoring and evaluation of LGU projects.

**[07:33:56.220] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008019)  
So the 560 million is used for salaries of the...

**[07:34:01.980] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008020)  
engineers and the project evaluation officers that we hired to do the monitoring on the ground.

**[07:34:06.900] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008021)  
Because the field officers of the DLG really do not have the technical know-how to do the monitoring themselves.

**[07:34:14.420] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008022)  
And the third-party monitoring, the third tier is the third-party monitoring.

**[07:34:18.720] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008023)  
In the past, we have engaged civil society organizations and non-government organizations

**[07:34:24.060] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008024)  
to help us monitor the projects implemented by the local governments.

**[07:34:28.400] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008025)  
So those are the...

**[07:34:29.640] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008026)  
the monitoring mechanisms that we have put in place.

**[07:34:35.340] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008027)  
But aside from that, Your Honor,

**[07:34:37.080] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008028)  
we also have the Subay Bayan Portal,

**[07:34:38.840] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008029)  
which is a platform where local government units are required

**[07:34:43.600] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008030)  
to upload status updates of projects implemented through the...

**[07:34:49.160] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008031)  
or funded by the local government support fund.

**[07:34:54.760] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008032)  
Yes, that's already the safeguards that we have put in place to ensure the integrity of...

**[07:35:01.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008033)  
the implementation of all these projects.

**[07:35:04.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008034)  
Thank you, Yusek. Thank you for clarifying.

**[07:35:08.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008035)  
Next is with regard to participatory budgeting.

**[07:35:11.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008036)  
The next question also from Code NGO is,

**[07:35:15.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008037)  
the 2027 NEP retains 1 billion pesos for the support and assistance fund to participatory budgeting.

**[07:35:23.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008038)  
While the overall LGSF increases from approximately 57.87 billion in 2026

**[07:35:29.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008039)  
to 58.53 billion...

**[07:35:31.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008040)  
in 2027.

**[07:35:33.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008041)  
During the same period,

**[07:35:35.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008042)  
the proposed allocation for the SBDP increases from 8.08 billion to 9.74 billion.

**[07:35:42.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008043)  
What performance evidence and policy considerations inform these relative allocations?

**[07:35:47.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008044)  
Consistent with the government's commitments to open and participatory governance,

**[07:35:51.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008045)  
what plans does DILG have to expand the coverage of participatory budgeting

**[07:35:56.720] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008046)  
and institutionalize meaningful CSO and community participation?

**[07:36:00.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008047)  
From

**[07:36:01.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008048)  
Project Identification and Prioritization through Implementation, Monitoring, and Evaluation.

**[07:36:07.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008049)  
Participatory Budgeting, Usec.

**[07:36:09.260] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008050)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Actually, the good chair mentioned two projects a while ago.

**[07:36:14.760] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008051)  
That's the Support to Barangay Development Program and the Support and Assistance Fund to Participatory Budgeting.

**[07:36:21.840] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008052)  
The SBDP is the program that was put in place by the National Task Force to End Local Communist Armed Conflict

**[07:36:28.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008053)  
to help barangays.

**[07:36:30.240] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008054)  
To help address the needs of people in barangays

**[07:36:33.660] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008055)  
which were previously influenced by the communist terrorist groups.

**[07:36:36.840] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008056)  
Because for the longest time, these barangays have been neglected.

**[07:36:39.920] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008057)  
Basic services supposedly delivered by the national and local governments.

**[07:36:44.220] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008058)  
So the 10 million for 2026, for example, is used to fund priority projects

**[07:36:50.000] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008059)  
identified through consultations at the grassroots level.

**[07:36:53.480] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008060)  
So that's the rationale of the SBDP or Support to Barangay Development Fund.

**[07:36:58.280] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008061)  
To gain or to regain the trust of...

**[07:37:00.560] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008062)  
of people to government, we need to also address their basic needs.

**[07:37:06.680] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008063)  
The other one is the Support and Assistance Fund to Participatory Budgeting.

**[07:37:10.340] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008064)  
For 2026, Mr. Chair, Your Honor, there's an allocation of 1 billion pesos.

**[07:37:15.780] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008065)  
And the 1 billion is used to fund water systems project by identified LGUs.

**[07:37:24.540] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008066)  
Also, evacuation centers by the identified LGUs.

**[07:37:31.220] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008067)  
The water system project amounts to 13.3 million.

**[07:37:35.100] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008068)  
And the evacuation centers amount to 25 point something million pesos.

**[07:37:40.460] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008069)  
So these are the municipalities which are considered waterless municipalities.

**[07:37:46.280] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008070)  
Meaning 50% of their population do not have access to potable water.

**[07:37:52.420] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008071)  
So these are already pre-identified municipalities for the water system project.

**[07:37:57.460] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008072)  
For the evacuation centers, these are also the pre-identified.

**[07:38:00.880] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008073)  
And these municipalities which do not have evacuation centers.

**[07:38:04.680] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008074)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor.

**[07:38:06.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008075)  
Thank you very much, Yusek.

**[07:38:09.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008076)  
We'll move to the fourth question still from Code NGO.

**[07:38:12.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008077)  
Human Rights Safeguards and Grievance Mechanisms.

**[07:38:14.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008078)  
United Nations Special Reporters and the Commission on Human Rights

**[07:38:19.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008079)  
have raised serious concerns regarding red tagging

**[07:38:22.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008080)  
and its effects on the freedoms of expression, association, and participation.

**[07:38:26.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008081)  
The CHR's National Inquiry found that red tagging creates a chilling effect,

**[07:38:30.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008082)  
on democratic participation

**[07:38:32.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008083)  
and may serve as a precursor

**[07:38:34.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008084)  
to grave human rights violations.

**[07:38:36.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008085)  
The CIVICUS monitor likewise

**[07:38:38.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008086)  
currently rates civic space

**[07:38:40.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008087)  
in the Philippines as repressed

**[07:38:42.580] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008088)  
and identifies continuing red-tagging

**[07:38:44.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008089)  
as one of its concerns.

**[07:38:45.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008090)  
Within the DILG's mandate,

**[07:38:48.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008091)  
what concrete safeguards are in place

**[07:38:50.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008092)  
to ensure that the implementation

**[07:38:51.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008093)  
of LGSF and SBDP projects

**[07:38:54.420] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008094)  
does not expose CSOs,

**[07:38:56.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008095)  
human rights defenders,

**[07:38:58.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008096)  
indigenous peoples,

**[07:38:59.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008097)  
community leaders,

**[07:39:00.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008098)  
and other stakeholders

**[07:39:01.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008099)  
to labeling, profiling,

**[07:39:03.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008100)  
retaliation, or exclusion?

**[07:39:04.740] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008101)  
What independent and accessible

**[07:39:06.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008102)  
grievance and accountability mechanisms

**[07:39:08.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008103)  
are available to individuals

**[07:39:10.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008104)  
and organizations?

**[07:39:11.060] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008105)  
Will the department publicly report

**[07:39:13.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008106)  
the number and nature of complaints received,

**[07:39:15.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008107)  
the agencies or officials involved,

**[07:39:17.940] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008108)  
and the corresponding actions taken?

**[07:39:23.260] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-008109)  
Mr. Chair, Your Honor,

**[07:39:25.100] DILG Secretary Juanito Victor C. Remulla** (asr-008110)  
may I request the PNP to respond to that, please?

**[07:39:29.180] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008111)  
Madam Chair,

**[07:39:30.140] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008112)  
National Police Commission,

**[07:39:32.660] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008113)  
The NAPLCOM recently released

**[07:39:34.980] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008114)  
a manual for media relations

**[07:39:37.280] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008115)  
which includes prohibition on red-tagging.

**[07:39:41.480] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008116)  
In that manual,

**[07:39:42.860] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008117)  
it is clearly stated that red-tagging is prohibited

**[07:39:45.640] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008118)  
and there are sanctions attributed to it.

**[07:39:49.740] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008119)  
And if anyone is listening,

**[07:39:51.840] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008120)  
be it an ordinary citizen

**[07:39:53.120] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008121)  
or a member of the media

**[07:39:55.120] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008122)  
who might be a victim of red-tagging,

**[07:39:57.920] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008123)  
the doors of the National Police Commission

**[07:40:00.200] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008124)  
are always welcome to accept any concern or complaint

**[07:40:03.960] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008125)  
and to report it.

**[07:40:04.840] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008126)  
And definitely, we will act on them.

**[07:40:08.900] NAPOLCOM Vice Chairperson and Executive Officer Atty. Rafael Vicente R. Calinisan** (asr-008127)  
Thank you.

**[07:40:09.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008128)  
Thank you, po.

**[07:40:32.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008130)  
Thank you so much.

**[07:40:32.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008131)  
So, I hope that clarifies the question.

**[07:40:35.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008132)  
Next is the fifth question,

**[07:40:39.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008133)  
also from Code NGO,

**[07:40:41.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008134)  
which is the basis for the proposed increase

**[07:40:43.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008135)  
in the SBDP budget.

**[07:40:45.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008136)  
So, same program, po.

**[07:40:46.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008137)  
The 2027 NAP proposes 9.74 billion for the SBDP,

**[07:40:51.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008138)  
an increase from the 8.8 billion appropriated

**[07:40:54.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008139)  
in the 2026

**[07:40:55.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008140)  
General Appropriations Act.

**[07:40:57.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008141)  
What verified needs assessment,

**[07:40:59.100] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008142)  
utilization and completion data,

**[07:41:00.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008143)  
and independent outcome evaluation

**[07:41:02.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008144)  
support this proposed increase?

**[07:41:05.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008145)  
Could the department please provide

**[07:41:07.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008146)  
the following information?

**[07:41:08.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008147)  
First, the objective criteria for selecting

**[07:41:11.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008148)  
beneficiary barangays and projects.

**[07:41:13.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008149)  
Second, the proposed allocation per barangay

**[07:41:50.340] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008165)

**[07:41:59.360] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008169)

**[07:42:05.760] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008171)

**[07:42:06.800] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008172)

**[07:42:11.760] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008174)

**[07:42:19.400] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008176)

**[07:42:21.140] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008177)

**[07:42:24.860] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008178)

**[07:42:28.040] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008179)  
Actually, it decreased, Your Honor.

**[07:42:29.740] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008180)

**[07:42:33.360] DILG Undersecretary for Local Government Marlo L. Iringan** (asr-008181)
